> Nkọwa zuru oke maka mgbanwe gburugburu ebe obibi ọ bụla nke OmniRoute ghọtara. Maka ndebiri mmalite ngwa ngwa, hụ [`.env.example`](../../.env.example).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Ngbanwe ọ bụla edere ebe a ga-apụtakwa na `.env.example`, na mgbanwe ọ bụla dị na `.env.example` ga-apụtarịrị ebe a. `npm run check:env-doc-sync` kwadoro nke a na ime na CI. Ka ịhapụ mgbanwe na ebumnuche, tinye ya na listi ohere n'ime `scripts/check/check-env-doc-sync.mjs`.

## 1. Ihe nzuzo achọrọ

Akara ihe nzuzo ewepụtara** Ee** A na-emepụta onwe ya na agba nke mbụ (`JWT_SECRET`, `API_KEY_SECRET`) wee nọgide na `server.env` na ndekọ data — na-edobe ha nke ọma mgbe akwụkwọ ndekọ ahụ bụ ephemeral. Wezụga bụ `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET`: ọ naghị anọgidesi ike na `server.env` — ihe ntinye ihe nkesa mint ihe ephemeral kwa-boot uru naanị akwa mmiri na-arụ ọrụ nwere ike iji, ya mere ndị ahịa akwa akwa na-achọ ka edobere ya nke ọma.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe           | Achọrọ           | Ọdabara               | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ---------------------------- | ---------------- | --------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `JWT_SECRET`                 | **Ee**           | _(o nweghị)_          | `src/lib/auth`                                            | Signs/verifies kuki nnọkọ dashboard niile (JWT). Mepụta na `openssl rand -base64 48`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `API_KEY_SECRET`             | **Ee**           | _(o nweghị)_          | `src/lib/db/apiKeys.ts`                                   | igodo nzuzo AES maka ụkpụrụ igodo API na izu ike na SQLite. Mepụta na `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `INITIAL_PASSWORD`           | Mba              | _(Edobeghi)_          | `src/lib/auth/managementPassword.ts`                      | Na-edozi okwuntughe dashboard na mmalite ebe ọ nweghị nke echekwara; eleghara anya ozugbo echekwara paswọọdụ. npm na isi iyi na-etinye akwụkwọ `.env.example` na `.env`, yabụ ha na-amalite site na ebe a ma ama `CHANGEME`: dochie ya tupu buut mbụ, ma ọ bụ tọọ `INITIAL_PASSWORD=` (efu) na `.env` ahụ iji mepụta paswọọdụ na ọkachamara onboarding na nleta mbụ, dị ka uru ewepụtaghị (ndabara ihe oyiyi Docker ) na-eme. Gbanwee paswọọdụ echekwara na Dashboard → Ntọala → Nche, ma ọ bụ mee `omniroute reset-password` (`node bin/reset-password.mjs` na ndenye ọpụpụ isi mmalite) yana `DATA_DIR` ka edobere na ndekọ data nkesa. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET` | **Ee** (mmepụta) | _(Edobeghi)_          | `src/app/api/internal/codex-responses-ws/route.ts`        | Ekekọrịtara nzuzo maka ime Codex Azịza WebSocket akwa. Na-enyocha arịrịọ n'etiti Electron/browser WS relay na OmniRoute. ⚠️**REQUIRED na mmepụta — mgbe ịtọsịrị ya, a na-ajụ arịrịọ àkwà mmiri WS niile.** Mepụta na `openssl rand -base64 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SW_BUILD_ID`      | Mba              | _(git SHA) _          | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Id cache-busting onye ọrụ doro anya maka shei PWA (#11779); mbụ na agbụ mkpebi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `SOURCE_VERSION`             | Mba              | _(Edobeghi)_          | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Nke abụọ n'agbụ — nke ndị na-ewu ụlọ PaaS setịpụrụ (dịka ụdị Heroku) dị ka ewepụtara ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SW_BUILD_ID`    | Mba              | _(nke ewepụtara)_     | `src/shared/components/PwaRegister.tsx`                   | Uru ọha na-ewulite oge onye ahịa na-eji edebanye aha `/sw.js?v=…`; ewepụtara site na abụọ dị n'elu, wee git SHA.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_STAMP_TOKEN` | Mba (akpaaka)    | _(ụgbọ ala kwa buut)_ | `src/server/authz/policies/management.ts`                 | Nzuzo otu-usoro na-egosi stampụ ndị ọgbọ-IP ntụkwasị obi sitere na nkesa HTTP nke OmniRoute(`scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`). The authz middleware Trust na-arịọ mpaghara (loopback/LAN gating of LOCAL_ONLY roads) naanị mgbe stampụ na-ebu akara a. Emepụtara akpaaka nke ọ bụla buut — hapụ ịtọghe; naanị pinye ya maka nhazi ọtụtụ usoro nke ga-ekekọrịta stampụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUSTED_PROXIES`  | Mba              | _(Edobeghi)_          | `scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`                              | IPs ma ọ bụ CIDR kewapụrụ rịkọm nke proxies ndị `X-Forwarded-For` / `X-Real-IP` ndepụta IP allow/deny nwere ike ikwere, n'elu loopback, adreesị netwọk nkeonwe yana akụkụ Cloudflare, nke a na-atụkwasị obi mgbe niile. Achọrọ naanị maka proxy na adreesị ọhaneze ọ bụla ọzọ; ma ọ bụghị na adreesị proxy ahụ bụ ihe nzacha na-ekpe ikpe. Igodo trotul onye ahịa ọ bụla (`/api/v1/relay/chat/completions`) nke relay n'otu adreesị onye ahịa edozira, ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-ebuga nkụnye eji isi mee naanị.                                                                                                                                  |

### Iwu ọgbọ

```bash
# Generate all four secrets at once:
echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)"
echo "API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)"
echo "INITIAL_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 16)"
echo "OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

> [!CAUTION]
> Emela faịlụ `.env` nwere ezigbo nzuzo na njikwa ụdị. `.gitignore` ewepularị `.env`, mana nyochaa tupu ịkwanye.

## 2. Nchekwa & database

OmniRoute na-eji**SQLite** (site na `better-sqlite3`) maka ntachi obi niile. Ụdị mgbanwe ndị a na-achịkwa ọnọdụ data, izo ya ezo, na okirikiri ndụ.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                         | Ọdabara                       | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `DATA_DIR`                                 | `~/.omniroute/`               | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Ndekọ mgbọrọgwụ maka SQLite DB, ndabere na faịlụ data. Wepụ maka mpịakọta Docker ma ọ bụ ụzọ omenala.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_DEFAULT_DATA_DIR`         | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `src/lib/dataPaths.ts`                                | Ịgbapụ maka onye nche DATA_DIR test/eval #10428). Nnwale na Node eval/print probes (`-e`/`--eval`/`-p`/`--print`, gụnyere ụdị `--eval=`/`--print=` ) na-enweghị `DATA_DIR` ka a na-atụgharị gaa na temp dir nke a na-atụfu ka ha enweghị ike imepe ezigbo nchekwa data onye ọrụ; tọọ `1` ka ị nwetaghachi na ezigbo ndekọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_SHA`                      | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `src/lib/monitoring/buildSha.ts`                      | Git SHA nke artifact na-agba ọsọ. akara nke `npm run build:release`; injectable n'ime arịa ndị na-ebuga na-enweghị `dist/BUILD_SHA` sentinel. Elu dị ka `system.buildSha` na `/api/monitoring/health`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELEASE_REF`                    | `origin/main`                 | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Ref ọnụ ụzọ ámá nke mkpọ artifact na-enyocha ihe wuo SHA megide (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CANARY_BUILD`             | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Tọọ `1` iji kwe ka ịkwakọ ihe owuwu nke SHA adịghị na ahịrị ntọhapụ, na-edekọ ya dị ka canary kpachaara anya kama ịdaba n'ọnụ ụzọ (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_API_KEY`                  | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `scripts/ops/deploy-canary.mjs`                       | igodo API maka nyocha nke canary-deploy anwụrụ ọkụ, ezitere dị ka `Authorization: Bearer` na `/v1/chat/completions`. Naanị script deploy (#10429) na-eji ya, ọ nweghịkwa ihe nkesa. Ọ bụghị ihe jikọrọ ya na mgbanwe `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_*` nke nbanye CLI anwụrụ ọkụ (`RUN_CLI_SMOKE=1`, `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_BASE_URL/MODEL/API_KEY_ENV/TARGETS/TIMEOUT_MS` na `tests/integration/upstream-cli-smoke.int.test.ts`) — hụ [CLI Integrations → Ezi anwụrụ ọkụ](../guides/CLI-INTEGRATIONS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILDING`                       | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `src/lib/buildPhase.ts`                               | Mgbama nrụpụta (#10060): setịpụrụ na `1` site na `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs` ma ketara onye ọ bụla na-arụ ọrụ na-arụ ọrụ spawn ka oyi akwa DB weghachi stub enweghị-op kama ịkwanye better-sqlite3 addon (nke na-ewepụ onye ọrụ na ọpụpụ). Edokwala maka ihe nkesa na-agba ọsọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_NATIVE_DEP_CHECK`          | `0`                           | `scripts/check/check-native-deps.mjs`                 | Tọọ na `1` ka ịwụpụ nlele mbido ntọala dabere na nhọrọ maka osisi ndị a na-ere ere. Nke a anaghị eme ka ndabere efu wuo ya; na-eji naanị mgbe ebupụtara ndabere nke ala na-esiteghị na band.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`                       | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `open-sse/executors/promptql/threadSticky.ts`         | **utu aha ọdịda** maka `DATA_DIR`, a na-enyocha naanị mgbe emebere `DATA_DIR`. A na-eji ya chọta ebe nchekwa ihe nkedo nke PromptQL na diski eri-sticky session cache (`<dir>/promptql-thread-sessions.json`); Ọ bụrụ na edoghị var, cache ahụ na-anọ na ebe nchekwa naanị (anaghị adịgide n'ofe malitegharịa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR`                    | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `src/lib/plugins/scanner.ts`                          | Akwụkwọ ndekọ aha** ngwa ngwa ngwa ngwa nyocha** na-agụ — na mgbọrọgwụ onye njikwa ngwa mgbakwunye na-etinye n'ime — na-ekpuchi ndabara nke ụlọ enwetara (#11827). Tụtụ ya aka n'osisi ngwa mgbakwunye a na-ekekọta na Docker/K8s kama ịkwaga HOME naanị ka ịkwaga ụzọ nyocha (HOME na-achịkwa omume ndị ọzọ na ụlọ). Tọgharia = `~/.omniroute/plugins`, ma ọ bụ `/tmp/.omniroute/plugins` mgbe usoro a na-ebupụ enweghị ụlọ ma ọlị — nke na-adịghị achọpụta nke ọma na mgbanwe a na-ewepụ. A na-abanye ndekọ ndekọ aha edoziri otu ugboro na mmalite dị ka `scanner.dir_resolved` site na ntinye nke meriri. Naanị akụkụ nkesa: plugins iwu CLI na-edobe `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH` nke ha (ngalaba 9). |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`                   | _( = efu enweghị nkwarụ)_     | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | igodo AES maka nchekwa data SQLite zuru ezu na izu ike. Mepụta na `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY_VERSION`           | `v1`                          | `scripts/build/bootstrap-env.mjs`, `electron/main.js` | Akara ụdị maka igodo nzuzo. Na-abawanye mgbe ị na-eme ntụgharị igodo iji kwado nkwụsị nke ndabere ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`               | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/backup.ts`                                | Mgbe `true`, na-amali routine/pre-write SQLite faịlụ ndabere (models.dev pricing save/clear, ntọala na-ede). Ndabere akwụkwọ ntuziaka na tupu eweghachite ka na-arụ ọrụ. Ọ na-eme** ọ bụghị** gbanyụọ ihe nchekwa nchekwa na-adịgide adịgide nke onye na-agba ọsọ mbịambịa ma ọ bụ onye nche ọpụpụ maka DB na-adịgide adịgide. A na-atụba nkwado ndabere na mpaghara na-abụghị akwụkwọ ntuziaka ka ọ bụrụ otu ugboro n'ime nkeji iri isii. Dashboard** Ntọala → Nchekwa** nwere ike gbanyụọ nkwado ndabere na mpaghara onwe ya.                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_SLOW_MS`                 | _(unset/off)_                 | `src/lib/db/adapters/slowDiagnostics.ts`              | Iri nkeji; ụkpụrụ dị n'okpuru 1 ma ọ bụ enweghị ọnụọgụ; na-adọ aka ná ntị kacha otu ugboro kwa 10s; ahịrị log nwere ọkwọ ụgbọ ala, ọrụ na elapsedMs naanị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_OPTIMIZE_ENABLED`        | `true`                        | `src/lib/db/sqlitePlannerStats.ts`                    | Ndozi ọnụ ọgụgụ ndị na-eme atụmatụ ndabere, na-adabereghị na mkpochapụ njide: nke mbụ na-enyere aka nhichapụ mkpochapụ (nke na-adịkarị 30s mgbe mmalite, mgbe ọpụpụ gasịrị) na-agba `PRAGMA optimize=0x10002`; na-esote gafere kwa awa 6 na-agba `PRAGMA optimize` (ma ọ bụ `PRAGMA optimize=0x00002` maka nyocha na-akparaghị ókè). `false` ma ọ bụ `0` na-egbochi mmezi niile (ihe na-enweghị isi, ebe ọcha edoziri). Ebe nchekwa data na ụzọ ndabere naanị ọgụgụ enweghị mmetụta. A na-edebanye aha ọdịda, anaghị atụba ya.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_ANALYSIS_LIMIT`          | `400`                         | `src/lib/db/sqlitePlannerStats.ts`                    | Tọọ `PRAGMA analysis_limit` tupu nkwalite. A na-akụdo ọnụọgụ iriba na [0, 100000]; malformed/empty ụkpụrụ ndabara na 400. `0` na-arịọ na-akparaghị ókè, kpọmkwem nyocha nke tebụl ahọpụtara site na njikarịcha; ngafe nke oge na-ekpochapụ SQLite na-ebuli sampling-cap bit iji sọpụrụ nke a. Ọ nweghị nkwupụta nyocha SQL kwụpụrụ onwe ya a na-eme.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CRYPT_KEY`                      | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Aha aha Legacy** maka `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`. Nabata dị ka ndaghachi azụ mgbe isi mgbanwe adịghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_BASE64`                 | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Aha aha Legacy** (ụdị Base64-encoded ) nabatara dị ka ọdịda. Decoded na-akpaghị aka tupu ejiri ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_DB_HEALTHCHECK_INTERVAL_MS`     | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Wepụ oge nlele ahụike SQLite oge (ms). Mgbe emegharịrị, a na-enweta ndabara site na `NODE_ENV`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`       | _(wepụrụ)_                    | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | **Ewepụrụ.** `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` dị ndụ nke oge ọ bụla nwere ike imebi maapụ wal-index nkekọrịta wee jiri SIGBUS (#13973) daa usoro a, yabụ na onye nhazi agaghịzi adị. Ihe agbanwe agbanwe bụ inert: uru dị mma na-edekọ ịdọ aka ná ntị nbibi otu oge, ebe `0` ma ọ bụ ịtọghe na-agbachi nkịtị. A na-echekwa WAL na ebe nlele PASSIVE (n'okpuru) ma gbachie ya site na ebe nleba anya nkwụsị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_PASSIVE_INTERVAL_MS`        | `300000` (5m)                 | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Wepụ oge `wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE)` ugboro ugboro (ms). Ọ na-edobe okpokolo agba WAL dị obere ka ebe nlele na-adị ngwa ngwa yana faịlụ WAL na-anọ n'agbụ n'etiti mkpọchi mmechi. `0` gbanyụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_GUARD_MAX_MB`               | `256`                         | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Mgbe akara PASSIVE chọtara faịlụ WAL dị n'elu nha a, gbaa `wal_checkpoint(RESTART)` ka WAL ga-amalite ọzọ na-edegharịghị wal-index nke nkewa. Ewepụrụ ebe nleba anya n'ụdị mkpọpu (lee ahịrị `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS` ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Tọọ `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` ka ịpụ na usoro ahụ mgbe a kwadoro nrụgide akụ dị egwu maka `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS`, na-ahapụ onye nlekọta (usoro `Restart=always`, Docker malitegharịa amụma) weghachite usoro dị ọcha kama ijere 503s ruo mgbe ebighị ebi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS` | `120000` (2m)                 | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Ogologo oge nrụgide dị oke mkpa ga-adịru tupu mmalite ọpụpụ onwe ya agbawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_PSI_DISABLED`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressurePolicy.ts`            | Tọọ na `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` ka ọ leghara onye ọbịa niile `/proc/pressure/memory` (`psi_some` / `psi_full`) mgbe onye agbata obi na-eme mkpọtụ na-aga 503s ebe usoro a dị mma (#13124). Ndị nche otu na V8 na-anọ. PSI na-akwụsịkwa ịgụta dị ka kernel throttle proof maka `cgroup_high`: otu mkpokọta n'elu `memory.high` wee tụgharịa dị egwu naanị na mmụba `memory.events` `high`/`max`. Na-enweghị `memory.events` a na-edobe oke raw.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLJS_WASM_PATH`                | _( chọpụta na akpaghị aka)_   | `src/lib/db/adapters/sqljsAdapter.ts`                 | Ụzọ doro anya (zuru oke ma ọ bụ n'ihe gbasara cwd) ruo `sql-wasm.wasm` mgbe ị na-eji `sql.js` WASM fallback nkwụnye. Achọpụtara na akpaghị aka site na ndabere ngwugwu yana nhazi ọkwa mgbe edobere ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`           | `30`                          | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Ụbọchị ọnụ ọnụ (completed/failed/cancelled/expired) Batch API ebe nlele ọrụ, faịlụ input/output/error ntụnyere na ahịrị ka a na-edobe site na nhichapụ akpaka tupu ihichapụ. Naanị na-arụ ọrụ ozugbo agbanyere `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED` ; dakọtara mpio ndodo ogbe API nke ya OpenAI. Adịghị emetụta ụzọ `DELETE /api/v1/batches/delete-completed` onye ọrụ kpalitere, nke na-anọ n'enweghị ihe ọ bụla (enweghị ihe nzacha afọ) site na imewe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`      | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Mgbe `true`, hapụ ka nhichapụ akpaka ihichapụ ọrụ Batch API (na ebe nlele ha) gara aga `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`, wee kpochapụ ọdịnaya BLOB nke faịlụ ebugoro gafere `expires_at` nke ha. Gbanyụọ na ndabara: nrụnye ọ bụla dị na-edobe data a ka ọ dị na mbụ ruo mgbe onye ọrụ abanyela. Ọzọkwa ọkọlọtọ dashboard-editable flag — hụ `docs/reference/FEATURE_FLAGS.md` → Oge ojiri gaa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`            | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`, `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`     | Tọọ `1` ka ịwụpụ nlele ahụike DB kpamkpam na mmalite. Bara uru maka ọrụ dị mkpirikpi na ule ntinye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORCE_DB_HEALTHCHECK`           | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Tọọ `1` ịmanye akaghị DB nlele ahụike, ọbụlagodi mgbe a ga-amafe ya (dịka ọmụmaatụ, ọrụ dị mkpụmkpụ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_POSTINSTALL`               | `0`                           | `scripts/postinstall.mjs`                             | Tọọ na `1` ka ịwụpụ ikpo ọkụ oge oge nke obodo n'oge `npm install`. Ọ bara uru na nrụnye CI/headless ebe arụrụla sqlite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_MIGRATIONS_DIR`                 | _( chọpụta na akpaghị aka)_   | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Wepụ ndekọ ndekọ nke onye na-agba ọsọ ọpụpụ na-enyocha. Ọ bara uru mgbe ị na-ebupu mbugharị ekpokọtara n'ụdị arụrụ arụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_EXTRA_MIGRATIONS_DIRS`          | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner/extraDirs.ts`             | Ndenye akwụkwọ ndenye ndị ọzọ dị ka ndenye `namespace=dir` kewapụrụ site na nchapụta ụzọ ikpo okwu (dịka `ee=/opt/app/enterprise/db/migrations`). A na-edekọ faịlụ ndị achọtara n'ebe ahụ dị ka `<namespace>-<number>`, yabụ nkesa mbupu mbugharị nke ya anaghị adaba na oghere ọnụọgụ elu. Ntinye na-adịghị mma, oghere aha na-ezighi ezi ma ọ bụ ndekọ na-efu efu na-atụba na mmalite kama ịwụpụ atụmatụ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_PENDING_MIGRATIONS`         | `50`                          | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Oke nchekwa oke-echere-mbugharị (#3416). Mmalite na-etepu ma ọ bụrụ na karịa nke a, ọtụtụ mbugharị na-echere na DB dị adị (nchekwa megide tebụl nsochi ehichapụrụ). Welie ya ka iweghachite ndabere ochie; tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ nlele.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTALL_UPGRADE_WORKDIR`        | _(`<repo>/.install-upgrade`)_ | `scripts/check/check-install-upgrade.mjs`             | Akwụkwọ ndekọ aha maka ọnụ ụzọ ntọhapụ `check:install-upgrade`. Ọ chọrọ ihe dịka 12 GB (2 ~ 3 GB tinye osisi gbakwunyere tarball), yabụ na ọ gaghị agba ọsọ na obere tmpfs — na onye na-agba ọsọ nke onwe ya `/tmp` bụ tmpfs 12 GB RAM na-akwado ma ọnụ ụzọ gwụ ya, na-akụ ngwugwu ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS`        | _(Ndabere na koodu)_          | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Ntugharị oge (ms) maka odee spend/cost batched. Ụkpụrụ dị ala na-ebelata nchịkọta ederede; ụkpụrụ dị elu na-ebelata esemokwu DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_MAX_BUFFER_SIZE`          | _(Ndabere na koodu)_          | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Ntinye ego kacha akwadoro tupu ịkwanye mmanye. Bulite na ntinye ọkwa QPS dị elu; wedata ala mgbe oke ebe nchekwa dị mkpa karịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_FETCH_DEBUG`              | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                        | Tọọ na `"true"` ka ịwepụta ndekọ nbibi `[ProxyFetch]` n'ụzọ mgbagharị Vercel. Gbanyụọ na ndabara ka ịzenarị ihie ụzọ ụzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_BINARY_PATH`         | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/sync.ts`        | Ụzọ zuru oke nke ọnụọgụ abụọ proxy core mpaghara (faịlụ aha ya bụ `sing-box` / `mihomo`) nke na-enyocha nhazi isi emepụtara tupu ọ dochie faịlụ anabatara. Unset na-ede `<path>.generated` n'akụkụ naanị faịlụ anabatara. Gburugburu ebe obibi naanị: echekwabeghị na nchekwa data ma ọ bụ anabata ya site na njikwa API, n'ihi na a na-egbu ọnụọgụ abụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_RELOAD_COMMAND`      | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/reload.ts`      | Kedu ka proxy isi mpaghara si ebughachite mgbe ngbanwe nhazi akwadoro: usoro arụmụka JSON na-agba ọsọ na-enweghị shei, ma ọ bụ `external` kpọmkwem mgbe onye nleba anya n'èzí tinye faịlụ ahụ. Echekwaghị na nchekwa data ma ọ bụ kpughee ya site na API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                    | `false`                       | `src/lib/proxyLogger.ts`                              | Tọọ na `"true"` ma ọ bụ `"1"` ka ịgụnye client/egress IPs na prefix akaụntụ na verbose `[ProxyEgress]` usoro-log ahịrị. Debere OFF na ndabara ka ndekọ usoro agaghị ahapụ IPs ma ọ bụ nganiihu akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG`                          | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `bin/cli/commands/quota.mjs`                          | Tọọ na `1` ka ibipụta nchọpụta oge ọ bụla (`[omniroute] GET <path> completed in Nms`) site na iwu oke CLI nye stderr.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_PATH`               | _(na akpaaka)_                | `scripts/dev/healthcheck.mjs`                         | Ụzọ doro anya site na nlele ahụike akpa nyochara. Akwụsịghị, nyocha ahụ na-enweta ya site na `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`; ịtọ ya na-ahọrọ azụ n'ime ebe nleba anya miri emi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG_COMPLETION`               | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `bin/cli/commands/completion.mjs`                     | Tọọ na uru ọ bụla na-adịghị efu iji wepụta nchọpụta `[omniroute completion]` site na CLI shell-completion cache ụzọ (read/refresh/write). Gbanyụọ na ndabara — cache ndị ahụ na-ada nwayọ, yabụ cache missing/corrupt anaghị agbaji mmecha taabụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG_STACK`                    | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `bin/cli/utils/formatCliError.mjs`                    | Tọọ `1` ka ibipụta nchịkọta nchịkọta zuru ezu mgbe iwu CLI dara. Gbanyụọ na ndabara — CLI na-ebipụta naanị ozi mperi. CLI-naanị; azịza HTTP agaghị agụnye nchịkọta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `BATCH_RETRY_DURATION_MS`                  | `86400000` (24h)              | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | mpio nnwagharị kacha maka ihe ọ bụla (ms). Ihe karịrị oge a ka akara na-akụ afọ n'ala.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_BASE_MS`                    | `5000`                        | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Nkwụsị oge (ms) maka nkwụghachi ụgwọ ngụkọ na nnwagharị ihe batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_MAX_MS`                     | `3600000` (1h)                | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Okpu (ms) maka nkwụghachi ụgwọ n'etiti ihe nnwagharị ihe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `BATCH_MAX_CONCURRENT`                     | `1`                           | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | A na-ahazi ọnụ ọgụgụ kacha nke batches n'otu oge. Welie elu ka ịbawanye mmepụta; gbadaa ala ka ịzenarị oke oke mmiri ozuzo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Tupu ịgbanwe nchekwa data na-adịgide adịgide, onye na-agba ọsọ na-ebuga na-ebipụta foto zuru ezu, ọdịnaya nwere n'okpuru `DATA_DIR/db_backups/`. Mbipụta chọrọ sistemụ faịlụ na-akwado otu sistemụ faịlụ, enweghị ike idegharị njikọ siri ike yana mmekọrịta faịlụ na-adịgide adịgide. Ndị ọbịa POSIX chọkwara mmekọrịta ndekọ aha; na Windows, Node nwere ike jụ njikwa ndekọ aha, yabụ OmniRoute na-ekpochapụ faịlụ e bipụtara ma na-emeso mmekọrịta ntinye ndekọ aha dị ka mgbalị kacha mma. Ọ bụrụ na `DATA_DIR` etinyegoro enweghị ike ịnye nkwa ndị ahụ, mmalite agaghị emechi tupu itinye mbugharị. Bugharịa `DATA_DIR` gaa na olu ya na primitives ndị ahụ; ejikwala `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP` gafee nchekwa mbata.

### Ihe atụ

| Ihe atụ                  | Nhazi                                                                               |
| ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **mmepe obodo**          | Hapụ ndabara niile. DB bi na `~/.omniroute/omniroute.db`.                           |
| **Docker**               | `DATA_DIR=/data` + bulie otu olu na `/data`.                                        |
| **Ezoro ezo na izu ike** | Tọọ `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY` + dobe ndabere igodo! Na-efunahụ ya = na-efunahụ data. |
| **CI/Testing**           | `DATA_DIR=/tmp/omniroute-test` — ephemeral, ọ nweghị ihe nzuzo achọrọ.              |

---

## 3. Netwọk & ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                          | Ọdabara                              | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PORT`                                      | `20128`                              | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri nke mbụ maka ma Dashboard UI na API njedebe (ụdị ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`                       | _(mgbọrọgwụ = efu)_                  | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/docker/ensure-docker-base-path.mjs`          | Ụzọ ụzọ URL maka ijere OmniRoute ozi n'azụ proxy (na-edozi Next.js `basePath`; auth redirects bụ basePath-aware). Dịka `/omniroute`. Na Docker a na-eme ọnụ ahịa n'oge `docker build` (`ARG OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`); Onyonyo mgbọrọgwụ arụgoro nke ọma nwere ike itinye uru oge ojiri dị iche otu oge na akpa mmalite tupu akpụkpọ ụkwụ Next.js. Tọọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` na mmalite ọha gụnyere otu ụzọ ụgbọ ala.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`           | _(mgbọrọgwụ = efu)_                  | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`                                  | Ngosipụta ihe nchọgharị a na-ahụ anya nke `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`, etinyere ya n'oge arụ ọrụ ka ihe ngosi njedebe dashboard na-egosi `https://host/omniroute/v1` kama `https://host/v1`. Daghachi azụ na `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` mgbe ịtọghị ya. Ewughachi ma gbanwechara ( `basePath` na-esote bụ oge nrụpụta).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED`                     | _(unset = agaghị adị ike)            | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/dashboardEmbed.mjs`                    | Wepu tinye iframe nke ibe HTML. Atọkwaghị, ụzọ ọ bụla na-ebu ụgbọ mmiri `frame-ancestors 'none'` + `X-Frame-Options: DENY`. Tọọ `vscode` iji jee ozi ibe (dashboard, nbanye, docs, landing) na `frame-ancestors 'self' vscode-webview:` na enweghị `X-Frame-Options`, yabụ VS Code Simple Browser nwere ike ịnye ha (ụdị OmniCopilot's `dashboardOpen: "editor"` ). Elu API (`/api`, `/v1`, `/v1beta`, `/a2a`, `/healthz`, aliases ọkwa ọkwa ) na-edobe nkụnye eji isi mee n'ụzọ ọ bụla. Naanị `vscode` ka amatara — `1`/`true` adịghị eme ya. Nrụpụta oge: wughachi mgbe agbanwechara (`docker build --build-arg DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED=vscode` maka onyonyo; ịtọ ya na nrụnye arụgoro arụgoro enweghị mmetụta).                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `API_PORT`                                  | _(Edobeghi)_                         | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Mgbe edobere, na-eje ozi `/v1/*` proxy API (gbakwunyere Gemini-native `/v1beta/*`) n'ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri a dị iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `API_HOST`                                  | `0.0.0.0`                            | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Kechie adreesị maka ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `DASHBOARD_PORT`                            | _(Edobeghi)_                         | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Mgbe atọrọ, na-eje ozi UI Dashboard n'ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri a dị iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNI_MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS`           | `0` _(enweghị nkwarụ)_               | `src/sse/utils/backpressure.ts`                                          | Njikọ nkata ụgbọ elu na-emekọ ọnụ; arịrịọ n'elu okpu nweta `503` na `Retry-After`. Ọnụ ọgụgụ dị mma na-enyere onye nche aka; unset/`0` gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTANCE_ID`                     | _(Edobeghi)_                         | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | ID kwụsiri ike, pụrụ iche maka ọnụ ụzọ ámá a mgbe ị na-eke ihe atụ OmniRoute. Na-eme ka ndenye nbanye nbanye anya. Ederede anabatara: mkpụrụedemede, mkpụrụọnụọgụgụ, `.`, `_`, `:`, na `-`; mkpụrụedemede 64 kacha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_URLS`                       | _(Edobeghi)_                         | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`     | OmniRoute base URLs nke nwere ike ịnweta `X-OmniRoute-Peer-Trace`. Naanị URLs anabatara nke ọma na-enweta metadata ndị ọgbọ; ndị ọzọ niile na-enye ihe na-emetụghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_MAX_HOPS`                   | `4`                                  | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kachasị nke ihe omume OmniRoute gara aga nabatara na arịrịọ ejiri eriri (`1`-`32`). Ule ugboro ugboro ma ọ bụ nkwụghachi mmefu ego ike gwụrụ HTTP `508 Loop Detected`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT`                       | `20130`                              | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri ebipụtara n'akụkụ ndị ọbịa maka Dashboard na ụdị mmepụta Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PROD_API_PORT`                             | `20131`                              | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri ebipụtara n'akụkụ ndị ọbịa maka API na ụdị mmepụta Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PORT`                            | _(Edobeghi)_                         | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Na-ebute ụzọ karịa `PORT` mgbe ị na-agba ọsọ n'ime Electron ma ọ bụ ihe mkpuchi ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `LIVE_WS_PORT`                              | `20129`                              | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Port maka ezigbo oge WebSocket live nlekota nkesa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `LIVE_WS_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                          | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Kechie adreesị maka sava WebSocket dị ndụ. Tọọ na `0.0.0.0` ka ikpughe na LAN (hahazikwa `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`). Njikọ na-egosipụta na kuki nnọkọ dashboard ma ọ bụ igodo API nke na-ejide oke njikwa (`manage`/`admin`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                   | _(Edobeghi)_                         | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Mmalite mgbakwunye rịkọm kewapụrụ ekwere imepe WebSocket dị ndụ. Enyerelarị mmalite dashboard loopback site na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_HOSTS`                     | _(Edobeghi)_                         | `src/server/ws/liveServerAllowList.ts`                                   | Aha nnabata agbakwunyere rịkọm anabatara maka mmalite WebSocket dị ndụ. N'adịghị ka `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (sitere zuru oke URLs), dakọtara naanị akụkụ — ndị ọbịa bara uru maka ntọlite LAN/Tailscale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_WS_PUBLIC_URL`            | _(Edobeghi)_                         | `src/hooks/useLiveDashboard.ts`                                          | URL ọha maka dashboard dị ndụ WebSocket (akụkụ ihe nchọgharị). Tọọ mgbe ị na-eche ihe nkesa WS n'ihu ya na proxy reverse ma ọ bụ ọwara Cloudflare (dịka `wss://ws.my-ai.com/live-ws`); ihe nchọgharị na-ejikọta ebe ahụ kama `ws://hostname:20132`. A na-ejikwa akụkụ ụzọ aha dị ka ụzọ nkwalite WebSocket (ndabara: `/live-ws`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_LIVE_WS`                  | `true`                               | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts` na `scripts/start-ws-server.mjs`           | Tọọ na `0` ma ọ bụ `false` iji gbanyụọ ihe nkesa WebSocket n'ezie (nke ndabara kwadoro, loopback-bound). CI/harness ntụgharị nke na-ewepụ edemede enyemaka WebSocket dị ndụ kwụụrụ onwe ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE`                       | `30`                                 | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/route.ts`                         | Per- (token, IP) oke ịgbagharị, requests/minute. N'ime ebe nchekwa, kwa ọmụmaatụ. `0` ma ọ bụ nke na-adịghị mma na-ewepụ ọnụ ụzọ akụkụ-IP (oke DB kwa-token ka na-emetụta).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `NODE_ENV`                                  | `production`                         | Next.js core                                                             | Na-achịkwa ịkọ ọnụ okwu ọnụ, caching, ikpughe nkọwa zuru oke yana nkwalite Next.js.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK`                   | _(Edobeghi) _ (nhọrọ nrụpụta akpaka) | `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs`                                  | Maka `npm run build`, ịtọgheghị ma ọ bụ ihe efu na-ahọrọ akpa weebụ mgbe oke ebe nchekwa dị mma ( RAM anụ ahụ ma ọ bụ obere cgroup/process ) kacha karịa.** 16 GiB** ma ọ bụ enweghị ike ikpebi nke ọma; n'elu 16 GiB, Turbopack ahọpụtara. Mpako ndị agha `0` doro anya; `1` doro anya na ụkpụrụ enweghị efu ndị ọzọ na-amanye Turbopack. Ọnụ ahịa gburugburu efu nke Compose zipụrụ na-eme** ọ bụghị** ike Turbopack. Ọnụ ụzọ a sitere na 15.6 GiB wulite-memory regression (#18184); Eji 24/32 GiB wuo onye ọbịa enweghị akara akara. Mpempe webụ mmepụta na-amapụ na ndabara cache ya na-adịgide adịgide (lee `OMNIROUTE_WEBPACK_BUILD_CACHE` n'okpuru; #18141) wee wuo ya na obere RSS kacha nta karịa ụzọ Turbopack dị ugbu a. Mmepe na-ejigide omume ngwugwu dị adị.**Docker ewulitelarị ndabara na `0` (akpa webụ)** ; jiri `--build-arg OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=1` wee banye. Lee `docs/guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WEBPACK_BUILD_CACHE`             | _(Edobeghi)_                         | `next.config.mjs`                                                        | Mmepụta akpa weebụ na-ewuli elu (`OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=0`) mafee cache faịlụ sistemụ na-adịgide adịgide ma nke a bụ `1`. Ide cache ahụ na-agbakwunye ihe dịka 2.5 GB nke ebe nchekwa kachasị elu na okpu okpu 6144 MB, na ụlọ dị ọcha anaghị agụghachi ya azụ.
