Tọọ `1` ka ọ dowe `.build/next/cache/webpack` maka imegharị ọsọ ọsọ na-ewuli n'igwe nwere RAM iji chekwaa; Docker na-ewuli were ya dị ka `--build-arg OMNIROUTE_WEBPACK_BUILD_CACHE=1` (`Dockerfile` na `Dockerfile.bun` onye nrụpụta ọkwa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`             | _(Edobeghi)_                         | `src/lib/db/core.ts` / `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`                       | Tọọ `1` ka ịwụpụ nlele ahụike SQLite na mmalite. Bara uru maka buut ngwa ngwa na nnukwu ọdụ data.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `NOTIFY_SOCKET`                             | _(Edobeghi)_                         | systemd (sd_notify protocol)                                             | Tọọ site na sistemụ mgbe usoro a na-aga n'okpuru ngalaba ọrụ yana ntinye sd_notify; OmniRoute gụrụ ya (lee `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`) iji zipu ọkwa READY/WATCHDOG. Onye ọrụ edobeghi ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`               | _(Edobeghi)_                         | `scripts/dev/systemd-notify.mjs`                                         | Tọọ `1` iji gbanyụọ systemd sd_notify (Ụdị = notify / WatchdogSec=) ọbụlagodi mgbe ị na-agba ọsọ n'okpuru ngalaba sistemụ. Ihe ngosi bụ enweghị op n'èzí sistemu n'agbanyeghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK_INTERVAL`          | `3600000`                            | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts` | Ogologo oge (ms) maka onye nhazi nyocha ahụike ndabere ndabere. Opekempe: 10000 (10s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL`               | `300000`                             | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/cache.ts`     | TTL (ms) maka ọkwa ahụike echekwabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK` | `false`                              | `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts`                                  | Tọọ `1` ma ọ bụ `true` iji gbanyụọ nyocha ndabere oge nke njikọ ndị na-eweta. Ndị na-achọ ihe (SEARCH_VALIDATOR_CONFIGS na `src/lib/providers/validation/searchProviders.ts`, dịka `tavily-search`) na-ewepu mgbe niile na — "nkwalite" ha bụ ajụjụ a na-akwụ ụgwọ n'ezie, n'ihi ya, a naghị enyocha ahụike na ngụ oge (#9970).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `DEEP_HEALTH_CHECK_ENABLED`                 | `0`                                  | `src/app/api/monitoring/health/route.ts`                                 | Tọọ na `1` ka ikwe ka onye na-akpọ oku kwenye ka ọ rịọ `/api/monitoring/health?deep=1`, nke na-egosi n'elu mmecha ya otu ugboro kwa TTL. Ndị na-akpọ oku amaghị aha anaghị akpalite nyocha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVENT_LOOP_STALL_THRESHOLD_MS`   | `5000`                               | `src/lib/healthzLag.ts`                                                  | Ọnụ ụzọ nkwụsị (ms) maka ihe ndekọ ihe omume-loop boot: friza ogologo oge karịa nke a na-edekọ otu ahịrị `event loop stalled` na malitegharịa yana ebe nchekwa na ọnụ ọgụgụ ụgbọ elu, wee kụda `eventLoopStall` counter/maximum na `/api/monitoring/health`. Tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ; uru enweghị ike ịgụ ma ọ bụ nke na-adịghị mma na-adaghachi na `5000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `HOST`                                      | `0.0.0.0`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next.mjs`                                               | Kechie adreesị maka sava Next.js dev/start. Na-ewepụ `0.0.0.0` ndabara mgbe atọrọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `HOSTNAME`                                  | `127.0.0.1`                          | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`                                    | Adreesị ejikọta nke onye na-agba ọsọ Playwright na-eji mgbe ọ na-ebupute Next.js. Ihe ndabara na `127.0.0.1` maka ule hermetic.** Ejila `omniroute serve`** — jiri `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST` kama (POSIX shells auto-set `HOSTNAME` na aha igwe; `.env` enweghị ike ịkagbu ya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST`                     | `0.0.0.0`                            | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                             | Kechie adreesị maka `omniroute serve`. Na-ezere nkukota na POSIX shei `HOSTNAME` variable (na-edobe aha igwe mgbe niile site na bash/zsh). Daghachi azụ na `0.0.0.0` mgbe ịtọghe. (#6194)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

### Ụdị ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri

```
┌─────────────────────────── Single Port (default) ──────────────────────────┐
│  PORT=20128                                                                 │
│  → Dashboard: http://localhost:20128                                        │
│  → API:       http://localhost:20128/v1/chat/completions                    │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Split Ports ─────────────────────────────────────┐
│  DASHBOARD_PORT=20128                                                       │
│  API_PORT=20129                                                             │
│  API_HOST=0.0.0.0                                                           │
│  → Dashboard: http://localhost:20128                                        │
│  → API:       http://0.0.0.0:20129/v1/chat/completions                     │
│  Use case: Expose API to LAN while restricting Dashboard to localhost.      │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Docker Production ──────────────────────────────┐
│  PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT=443   PROD_API_PORT=8443                              │
│  → Maps container ports to host ports in docker-compose.prod.yml.          │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 4. Nchekwa & Nyocha

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                          | Ọdabara                                                                               | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `MACHINE_ID_SALT`                           | `endpoint-proxy-salt`                                                                 | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Nnu jikọtara ya na njirimara ngwaike maka akara mkpịsị aka igwe. Gbanwee n'otu n'otu maka ịnọpụ iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SALT`                        | _(Nnụ na-ewepụ = random kwa-wụnye nnu nọgidere na `<DATA_DIR>/cli-token-salt.json`) _ | `src/lib/machineToken.ts`                                | nnu HMAC maka inweta akara ngosi CLI mpaghara. Ịtọlite uru a na-atụgharị akara CLI niile na igwe ma na-ebute ụzọ karịa nnu na-adịgide adịgide. Lee `docs/security/CLI_TOKEN.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE`                        | `false`                                                                               | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Na-edobe ọkọlọtọ `Secure` na kuki nnọkọ.** Kwesịrị ịbụ `true`** mgbe na-agba ọsọ n'azụ HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `REQUIRE_API_KEY`                           | `false`                                                                               | API middleware                                           | Mgbe `true`, arịrịọ proxy `/v1/*` niile ga-enwerịrị igodo API bara uru.** Ọkọlọtọ a anaghị abanye `GET /v1/models`** , nke na-esote ọnọdụ nbanye dashboard (`requireAuthForModels`) kama — ya mere `401` sitere na `/v1/models` ka NOT pụtara na echekwabara nbanye. Lee `docs/security/INFERENCE_AUTH_POSTURE.md` (#13695). Nabata `true`, `1` ma ọ bụ `yes`, na-eleghara ikpe na oghere ndị gbara ya gburugburu; uru ọ bụla ọzọ (gụnyere `on`) agbanyụrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL`                      | `false`                                                                               | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`         | Na-enye ohere ikpughe ụkpụrụ igodo API zuru ezu na Dashboard UI. Enwere ike ịhazi ya site na ọkọlọtọ njirimara Dashboard; ihe ize ndụ nchekwa n'ihe gbasara òkè.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `NO_LOG_API_KEY_IDS`                        | _(efu)_                                                                               | `src/lib/compliance/index.ts`                            | Igodo API kewapụrụ rikoma IDs nke na-agafe arịrịọ ntinye akwụkwọ (nkwenye GDPR ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `DEFAULT_RATE_LIMIT_PER_DAY`                | _(Edobeghi = unlimited)_                                                              | `src/shared/utils/apiKeyPolicy.ts`                       | Ndaghachi azụ kwa ụbọchị mmefu ego etinyere na igodo API nke kọlụm `rate_limits` bụ ihe efu. Ewepụghị ma ọ bụ tọgbọ chakoo: enweghị okpu doro anya (#2289, #11017). `0` bụ otu (na-akparaghị ókè). Integer N dị mma na-enyere aka N/day, 5N/week, 20N/month. Ụkpụrụ na-adịghị efu na-adaghachi azụ na windo 1000/day, 5000/week, 20000/month.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES`                       | `10485760` (10 MB)                                                                    | `src/shared/middleware/bodySizeGuard.ts`                 | Ndabere maka ntọala `maxBodySizeMb` a na-ahụ anya; ntọala echekwara na-ebute ụzọ. `/api/v1/chat/completions` na `/api/v1/responses` dobe ndabara MB 50 ruo mgbe echekwara oke; Oke echekwara wee dochie ya, elu ma ọ bụ ala, maka onye nche nkụnye eji isi mee yana maka nnabata nkata-byte n'ezie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

Otu [#10437](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/pull/10437) ahụ ike-ụlọ mgbapụ dị ka nhazi-dị arọ; ka ejiri `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / [#10110](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/10110) kechie ya mere [#7849](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/7849) anaghị alaghachi azụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_BODY_BYTES`        | `52428800` (50 MB)                                                                    | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Amanye okpu siri ike nkata nkata megide bytes na-agụ n'oge nri oke oke, gụnyere arịrịọ ndị na-efu efu, na-adịghị mma, ma ọ bụ emeghị ihe n'eziokwu `Content-Length`; ngafe na-anata `413`. `maxBodySizeMb` echekwara na-anọchi ndabara 50 MB ; mgbe agbanwere mgbanwe a, ọ na-agbadakwa uru echekwara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT`        | _(wepụ — enweghị okpu arịrịọ-ọnụ) _                                                   | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | **#503-fanout:** this legacy request-COUNT cap now binds only when explicitly set. Left unset (the default), heavyweight chat admission is instead gated by `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` — an auto-derived BYTE budget sized from the process's real memory ceiling in **one process** (one V8 heap). Two overlapping ~750k-token `/v1/responses` abort ~12 Gi heaps (#7849) — a **memory-budget** warning, not a hard product max of 2. A healthy process (heap below the shed ratio) MAY admit more concurrent long `/v1/responses` via `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM`. Tens of long SSE clients (40–50) is heap + `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / #10110, not “max 2”. Blindly raising this to “use the host” reintroduces #7849. Multiply **heaps** with **N independent `DATA_DIR`s** (#11024); never `replicas>1` on one SQLite file.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES`         | _(Enwetara akpaaka)_                                                                  | `src/shared/middleware/admissionBudget.ts`               | **#503-fanout:** mebie maka mmefu ego inest byte enwetara onwe ya (25% nke uko ụlọ nchekwa V8/cgroup siri ike kewara site na nkwalite 8x na-agafe agafe). Ọbara ewepụtara na nke doro anya jigidere 8 MiB–2 GiB. Ahụ buru ibu karịa mmefu ego dị irè na-ada ozugbo na `413 body_exceeds_budget`; esemokwu dị n'etiti ahụ ndị a na-arụ ọrụ n'otu n'otu na-anọgide na-emegharị `503`. 40–50 ndị ahịa SSE ogologo oge bụ mkpọmkpọ ego + a, ọ bụghị “max 2” siri ike. Gụọ `chatAdmission.maxInflightBytes` / `budgetSource` / `pressureSeverity` na `/api/monitoring/health` tupu ịmegharị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

A na-awụfu arịrịọ dị arọ n'otu oge gara aga `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` naanị site na `503` enwere ike ịnwale mgbe ikpo ahụ dị ALSO na ma ọ bụ karịa oke nke a; A na-anabata ya n'obo dị mma site na ime ụlọ ahụike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

Na-enweghị nke a, arịrịọ ọ bụla na-arụ ọrụ mana ọ nwere ahụike gafere nnabata na-enweghị ụlọ. Ozugbo ọtụtụ mgbazinye ego a na-arụkọ ọrụ na-arụ ọrụ site n'ụzọ ahụ siri ike, arịrịọ ndị ọzọ na-arụsi ọrụ ike na-adaba n'ụzọ SAME bounded-wait/shed nke ejiri n'okpuru nrụgide ikpo ọkụ. `0` gbanyụọ uzo uzo kpamkpam.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

Ọ na-enye ohere ebumnuche webhook nkeonwe, belụsọ ebe njedebe cloud-metadata/link-local, nke na-egbochi ya ọbụlagodi ọkọlọtọ a. Tọọ `false` maka ọnọdụ naanị ọha, nke na-egbochikwa ndị ọbịa nzuzo na loopback. Onye nche na-enyocha aha nnabata ma ọ bụ IP nkịtị ka edere ya, tupu nyocha DNS ọ bụla; n'ụdị naanị ọhaneze, ihe mkpuchi nchekwa nchekwa na-edozikwa aha nnabata wee jụ azịza nzuzo ọ bụla. `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` na-atụgharị nlele ndị a n'ụzọ ndepụta ahịrị ya. Ndị na-eweta mpaghara arụrụ arụ, dị ka LM Studio, Ollama, vLLM, Llamafile, na Triton, wụpụ ndenye ndị ọbịa niile maka nkwado isi na nkata. Gbanyụọ na ndabara — na-ebugharị gaa na onye ọbịa dịpụrụ adịpụ na-agbanwe njirimara egress na ga-abụrịrị mkpebi onye ọrụ doro anya. A na-anabata oghere loopback (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) na enweghị mmetụta. Ebe dịpụrụ adịpụ URL ga-agaferịrị amụma URL na-apụ apụ (`OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` / `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

Ndị ọbịa edepụtara tozuru oke maka `/v1/rerank`, `/v1/audio/*` na nlele ahụike mpaghara na-agbanyeghi `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` / `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` maka ọnụ ụzọ ọ bụla dịpụrụ adịpụ, a na-akpọkwa ya — ozugbo site na `HTTP(S)_PROXY` ma ọ bụ proxy njikọ njikọ. Ha ka na-eziga igodo API njikọ ahụ. Naanị otu akara aha na aha `.internal`/`.local` ka anabatara; A na-eleghara aha FQDNs ọha na eze, IP na aha metadata site na ịdọ aka ná ntị. (#14635)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
