A na-anabatakwa utu aha efu `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` ; Ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ Dashboard nke otu igodo ahụ na-ebute ụzọ. (#10889)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`               | `false`                                                                               | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Bare utu aha `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` (#10889).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_MFA`                     | `false`                                                                               | `src/server/auth/dashboardMfa.ts`                        | Ngwa mgbake furu efu maka dashboard Nwepu-nbanye TOTP nbanye ihe abụọ. Mgbe `true`, ihe nke abụọ na-awụpụ naanị maka ntinye aka na onye ọbịa n'onwe ya (loopback peer and loopback `Host`); Nbanye nbanye na `POST /api/cli/connect` sitere na mmalite ndị ọzọ ka chọrọ koodu ahụ. Banye na mpaghara, gbanyụọ ihe abụọ n'okpuru Ntọala → Nche (koodu nwere ike ịhapụ oghere ka edobere nke a, yabụ enwere ike wepu ihe na-anaghị enyocha ya; okwuntughe ka achọrọ), wee weghachi ya. TOTP na-ekpuchi nbanye paswọọdụ yana `omniroute connect` naanị nbanye — OIDC/SSO dabere na ihe nke abụọ nke onye na-eweta njirimara. Lee [AUTHZ_GUIDE](../architecture/AUTHZ_GUIDE.md#optional-totp-two-factor-login-16619).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

### Ndepụta nleba anya siri ike

```bash
# Production security minimum:
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true        # Requires HTTPS
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true           # Authenticate all proxy calls
ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL=false     # Never expose keys in UI
CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://your.domain.com
MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES=5242880    # 5 MB limit
```

---

## 5. Ntinye ịcha ọcha & PII Nchekwa

OmniRoute na-enye nchekwa nke oyi akwa abụọ: nyocha-n'akụkụ ịgbanye arịrịọ na iwepụ PII nzaghachi.

> **⚠️ :** Ihe nchekwa ndị a bụ nchọpụta _kachasị mbọ ike heuristic_, ọ bụghị ngwa ngwa ịgbanye ọkụ ọkụ ma ọ bụ sistemu PII DLP. Ha nwere ike iwepụta ihe nrịbama ụgha (benign persona/RPG kpaliri ọkọlọtọ) yana ihe ọjọọ (leetspeak, spacing, na-abụghị nke Bekee). Ha ezughị naanị maka nnabata. Dezie ụdịdị wee nwalee megide okporo ụzọ gị tupu ị dabere na ha.

### Akụkụ arịrịọ: Nchekwa ịgbanye ngwa ngwa

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                | Ọdabara      | Isi mmalite faịlụ                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| --------------------------------- | ------------ | ---------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED`         | `true`       | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Kwado nyocha nke ozi mbata maka usoro ịgba ọgwụ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                 |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE`            | `warn`       | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Amụma ịgba ọgwụ: `warn` = log naanị, `block` = jụrụ arịrịọ ya na 400. Legacy `redact` na-eme ya.** ọ bụghị** ederede ịgba ntụtụ; jiri `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED` maka arịrịọ PII degharịa. |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_MODE`            | _(Edobeghi)_ | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Legacy utu aha maka `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE` — otu omume ahụ.                                                                                                                             |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | `high`       | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Ogo kacha nta nke `MODE=block` jụrụ: `high` (ndabere), `medium`, ma ọ bụ `low`. A na-ahụ ụkpụrụ nke ọkara ma ọ bụrụ na ewedara ya.                                                      |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | _(Edobeghi)_ | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Legacy utu aha maka `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` — otu omume ahụ.                                                                                                                  |
| `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`           | `false`      | `src/lib/guardrails/piiMasker.ts`        | Mgbe `true`, megharịa PII na arịrịọ mbata (na-adabereghị na ọnọdụ ịgba ntụtụ).                                                                                                          |
| `PII_REVERSIBLE_VAULT_ENABLED`    | `false`      | `src/lib/pii/requestTokenVaultScope.ts`  | Site na `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`, arịrịọ nkpuchi PII dị ka arịrịọ kwa-ịgbanwe akara wee weghachi ha na nzaghachi 2xx. Ọzọkwa ọkọlọtọ njirimara DB (DB > ENV).                            |
| `CREDENTIAL_REDACTION_ENABLED`    | `false`      | `src/lib/guardrails/credentialMasker.ts` | Megharia ụkpụrụ akara igodo API / nzuzo ama ama nke ọma site na ibu request/response. Ịbanye; enyo `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`.                                                             |

### Azịza-akụkụ: PII Sanitizer

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe               | Ọdabara  | Isi mmalite faịlụ         | Nkọwa                                                                     |
| -------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION`      | `false`  | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | Nyochaa nzaghachi LLM maka PII agbapụtara tupu ịlaghachi na onye ahịa.    |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION_MODE` | `redact` | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | `redact` = nkpuchi PII, `warn` = log naanị, `block` = dobe nzaghachi dum. |

### VS Code Tokenized-Route Context Sanitizer

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                  | Ọdabara | Isi mmalite faịlụ                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VSCODE_SANITIZE_CONTEXT` | `1`     | `src/app/api/v1/vscode/contextSanitizer.ts` | Mwepu ọnọdụ onye editọ na-arụsi ọrụ ike (`editorContext`, `activeEditor`, `currentFile`, `selection`, `openTabs`…) site na arịrịọ `/v1/vscode/[token]/*` wee megharịa ọdịnaya nke faịlụ nwere mmetụta agbakwunyere nke ọma. Nchekwa-na-ndabere; tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ. |

### Ihe atụ

| Ihe atụ              | Nhazi                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| -------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **nnabata ụlọ ọrụ**  | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=block`, `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED=true`, `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION=true` (ngbochi injection + request/response PII redaction; ụdịdị nwere onwe) |
| **Nleba anya naanị** | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=warn` — ndekọ ma ọ dịghị ihe mgbochi                                                                                                          |
| **Ojiji nkeonwe**    | Hapụ — efu niile nwere nkwarụ n'elu                                                                                                                                                                 |

---

## 6. Ngwá ọrụ & Usoro ụzọ

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                                          | Ọdabara                      | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `TOOL_POLICY_MODE`                                          | `disabled`                   | `src/lib/toolPolicy.ts`                               | Na-achịkwa ohere LLM tool/function-calling. `allowlist` = naanị ngwaọrụ edepụtara, `denylist` = niile ma ewezuga edepụtara, `disabled` = enweghị mgbochi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_PATH`                              | `./config/payloadRules.json` | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`                   | Ụzọ iji kwụọ ụgwọ njikwa iwu JSON faịlụ (per-model/protocol upstream tweaks).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_RELOAD_MS`                         | `5000`                       | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`                   | Bugharịa nkeji oge (ms) maka ibugharị faịlụ iwu ịkwụ ụgwọ dị ọkụ. Nke kacha `1000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PREFER_CLAUDE_CODE_FOR_UNPREFIXED_CLAUDE_MODELS` | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                          | Wepụ: ụzọ efu `claude-*` ụdị IDs sitere na ndị ahịa Claude Koodu site na akaụntụ Claude Code OAuth kama ịchọ prefix nke ndị na-eweta. prefixes ndị na-eweta ihe ka na-emeri. Enwere ike ịhazi ya site na ntụgharị dashboard na ibe ndị na-eweta Claude.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDACT_THINKING`                                 | `true`                       | `open-sse/executors/base/redactThinkingBeta.ts`       | Tọọ `false` ka ọ kwụsị OmniRoute ịgbakwụnye `redact-thinking-2026-02-12` Anthropic beta n'onwe ya (ihe mgbochi mbinye aka na-enweghị isi). Arịrịọ na `thinking.display: "summarized"` mafee ya n'agbanyeghị; beta redact nke ndị ahịa kparịtara ụka ma ọ bụ `requestDefaults.redactThinking` dakọtara na CC na-edobe ya. Arịrịọ Claude OAuth dobe beta (ọ bụ akụkụ nke akara mkpisiaka Claude Code OAuth ) ma ọ bụrụ na agbanyeghi ọkọlọtọ njiri mara `CLAUDE_OAUTH_SUMMARIZED_THINKING_UNREDACTED` adịghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `BARE_MODEL_DEFAULT_OVERRIDE_LEGACY`                        | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                          | Omume `resolveBareModelToConnectionDefault` nkeha (#4825): jiri `defaultModel` njikọ ahọpụtara dochie aha ụdị ọ bụla. Gbanyụọ na ndabara, naanị `auto` ma ọ bụ ihe efu ka edochiri; na-edekọ ịdọ aka ná ntị otu ụzọ mgbe ọ nọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `COMBO_CONCURRENCY_PER_MODEL`                               | `3`                          | `open-sse/services/comboConfig.ts`                    | Okpu concurrency ihe nlere nke ọ bụla maka gburugburu robin combos (#9100). Semaphore gburugburu robin combo bụ nke siri ike na arịrịọ ugboro atọ n'otu n'otu na-enweghị nkwụsị, na-ahazi okporo ụzọ dị elu n'azụ okpu ahụ. Ekwenyere na `>= 1`, ejigidere na `<= 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_COMBO_MAX_DEADLINE_FAILURES`                     | unset (`0` = nwere nkwarụ)   | `open-sse/services/combo/comboPredicates.ts`          | Ịbanye n'okpuru mmefu ego maka** iyi-njikere ọdịda njedebe** n'ọsọ ọsọ. Igwe elu akwụsịla akwụsị na-adaba windo njikere na ebumnuche ọ bụla ebe combo na-atụgharị — ntụgharị ọ bụla na-ezipụ ahụ zuru oke mgbe iri iri nke sekọnd na nkeji nke ichere — na-amụba latency onye ọrụ na ebe nchekwa ọnụ ụzọ tupu okpu mbọ zuru ụwa ọnụ (nke a na-ahazi maka ọdịda ngwa ngwa) na-amalite. Nweliteghị /`0` (nke ndabara), enweghị ike ịgbanwe omume = ; integer dị mma na-akwụsị ngwakọta na `504` dị ọcha ozugbo na ọtụtụ ọdịda-ngwụcha achịkọtala. Ọdịda na-agụta mgbe mbọ a kwụsịrị na `504` nke `error.code` ahaziri bụ oge iyi-njikere: enweghị mmemme na-abụghị ping SSE n'ime windo njikere, ma ọ bụ iyi iyi dara ada tupu mmemme mbụ na-abụghị ping. N'oge EOF (iyi mechiri tupu emume mbụ ya) anaghị agụta. |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENTIC_STALL_FAILOVER`                          | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/combo/agenticStallFailover.ts`     | Ịbanye: tọọ `1` (ma ọ bụ `true`) yabụ onye otu combo na-aza ntụgharị ngwa-ọrụ yana `finish: stop` + narration + oku ngwá ọrụ efu na-adaba n'ebumnobi na-esote (atụmatụ-anya ụzọ, okirikiri-robin na pinned sessions). Ọ bụrụ na enweghị ebumnuche na-eme nke ọma, a ga-eweghachi azịza akwụsịla akwụsị kama ịbụ njehie, ọnweghịkwa onye na-emebi ihe na-eweta ma ọ bụ mkpọchi ihe atụ anaghị akwụ ụgwọ. A na-ejigide ntụgharị iyi iyi n'aka onye ahịa ruo mgbe emechara mkpebi ahụ. Arịrịọ na-eji azịza API `input` ọdịdị adịghị nkewa. OFF.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `DISABLE_CONTEXT_WINDOW_CHECKS`                             | `false`                      | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`                       | Nbanye dị ize ndụ nke na-awụpụ ọnọdụ mpaghara OmniRoute-window / max-input-token maka arịrịọ otu ụdịdị kpọmkwem. Ndị na-eweta elu ka na-amanye oke oke ha; mkpakọ ngwa ngwa na okpu ihe nrụpụta nke ihe nlereanya ahụ ka na-arụ ọrụ. Ụzọ dị irè bụ ọkọlọtọ njirimara DB na-ewepụ > gburugburu ebe obibi agbanwe > ndabara; ọ dịghị Malitegharịa ekwentị a chọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`                           | _(Edobeghi)_                 | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`                | Akwụkwọ YAML `providers:` n'ahịrị (RIC-738, D4). Mgbe atọrọ (ya na ma ọ bụ na-enweghị ngọngọ `strategy:` ), `/v1/chat/completions` na-atụgharị gaa na ntinye dakọtara ọnụ OpenAI jikọtara ọnụ kama ịbụ ọkpọkọ igwe ojii. Ewepụghị (nke ndabara): ụzọ ahụ dabara ozugbo na ọkpọkọ igwe ojii dị. Lee `docs/routing/SELF_HOSTED_OPENAI_ENTRY.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS_FILE`                      | _(Edobeghi)_                 | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`                | Ụzọ na faịlụ YAML na-ejide otu akwụkwọ `providers:` dị ka `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, maka ntinye akwụkwọ na-ahọrọ faịlụ karịa inline env var. Onye ọ bụla na-eme ka ntinye nke onwe ya rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_API_KEY`                             | _(wepụ ụzọ mepere emepe — )_ | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`                | Igodo API ekekọrịtara nhọrọ maka ntinye ahaziri nkeonwe (D5 scaffold, edobere maka sistemụ oke-otu). Mgbe atọrọ, arịrịọ ga-agụnye `Authorization: Bearer <key>`. Ewepụghị: ụzọ a ghere oghe, dabara n'ụkpụrụ onye na-eweta mpaghara nwere onwe ya (loopback/trusted-network deployment).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`                            | _(Edobeghi)_                 | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`              | Akwụkwọ YAML `strategy:` n'ahịrị maka injin ntugharị deterministic (M2/RIC-740, D3) — blacklist/whitelist, onye na-akụda oyi, ọnụ ahịa-ebute ụzọ, latency-aware, chain fallback. Na-ewepụ ngọngọ `strategy:` inline etinyere n'ime `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, otu igodo. Lee `docs/routing/DETERMINISTIC_ROUTING.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY_FILE`                       | _(Edobeghi)_                 | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`              | Ụzọ na faịlụ YAML na-ejide otu akwụkwọ `strategy:` dị ka `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`, maka ntinye akwụkwọ na-ahọrọ faịlụ karịa inline env var.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONVERSATION_TRACKING`                   | _(Edobeghi)_                 | `open-sse/services/conversationTracker.ts`            | Tọọ `1` ka ịkwụsị ịnakọta akụkọ mkparịta ụka. `resolveConversationId()` na-eweghachite nsonaazụ a na-emebeghị tupu ọ gụọ SQLite ma ọ bụ tụsa akụkọ ozi, yana IDs nke ndị ahịa wetara na-ekpuchikwa. Agbanweghị njikwa usoro-mgbagharị ma anaghị ehichapụ ndekọ dị adị. Maka mbugharị ndị na-adịghị eji nlele mkparịta ụka dashboard ma chọọ ka tebụl tụgharịa kwụsị itolite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_API_KEY`                                   | _(Edobeghi)_                 | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`        | igodo API maka atụmatụ Nwepu-na `nadir` auto-combo router atụmatụ, nke a na-eji mgbe agwakọtaghị `config.nadir.apiKey` nke combo. Nhọrọ: oku enweghị igodo ala na ọkwa Nadir na-amaghị aha, nke nwere oke nha kwa IP na enweghị ike iji ya n'azụ ọnụ ụzọ mbata. Mmetụta; na-ahọrọ ubi ọ bụla combos mgbe combos na-eji akaụntụ dị iche iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_BASE_URL`                                  | `https://api.getnadir.com`   | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`        | Isi mmalite nke mkpebi Nadir API, ejiri ya mgbe mkpọmkpọghị `config.nadir.baseUrl` nke onwe ya. Naanị achọrọ maka Nadir kwadoro onwe ya, nke na-edobe isi ozugbo n'ime netwọkụ nke gị. A na-anabata `/v1` na-eso ya ma yipụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW`                           | `provider`                   | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`                  | Window kwa izu maka oke-aPI-key USD oke ojiji (arịrịọ mmanye, `GET /api/keys/{id}/usage-limits`, `@@om-usage`). `provider`: izu ahụ na-agwụ na nrụpụta izu mbụ n'etiti njikọ ndị dị n'elu igodo nwere ike iru, ma ọ bụ ụbọchị 7 na-atụgharị mgbe amaghị nke ọ bụla. `calendar`: izu mpaghara site na Mọnde 00:00 ruo Mọnde na-esote 00:00, yiri igodo ọ bụla. A na-atụ aro `calendar` mgbe ọtụtụ igodo emebere ego na-ekekọrịta otu ọdọ mmiri nke akaụntụ dị elu. Ụkpụrụ ezighi ezi na-adaghachi na `provider`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW_TIMEZONE`                  | _(usoro oge mpaghara)_       | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`                  | IANA timezone (dịka ọmụmaatụ `Asia/Shanghai`) nke na-akọwa Monday 00:00 maka windo `calendar` kwa izu. Atọghị ma ọ bụ adịghị mma: mpaghara oge usoro OmniRoute ( `TZ` nke onye ọbịa ma ọ bụ akpa, nke a na-edobekarị, ya bụ oge a na-ahazi oge niile, na foto akpa). Agbagharala ya na ọnọdụ `provider`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_TOOLS_LIMIT`                                 | `128`                        | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                        | Ọnụọgụ ngwaọrụ kacha anabatara na arịrịọ (OpenAI ndabere). Ọnụ ọgụgụ dị mma naanị; ụkpụrụ edobereghị ma ọ bụ ezighi ezi dowe `128`. Onye na-eweta ya nwere oke known/proactive (`grok-cli` 200, `nvidia` 1536) ka na-etinye okpu — nke ya naanị nke a na-ebuli elu ụlọ ndabara. Ebumnuche Claude-format enweghị okpu ndabara (ozi API enweghị nke ọ bụla); oke mmụta ka na-emetụta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOOL_OUTPUT_IMAGE_MAX_COUNT`                     | `16`                         | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-responses.ts`     | Azịza nye ntụgharị asụsụ nkata: ole onyonyo arụpụta ngwa ọrụ (`function_call_output`, `custom_tool_call_output`, ọrụ `tool`) ezigara ọzọ dị ka akụkụ onyonyo onye ọrụ kwa arịrịọ. A na-edobe nke ọhụrụ ma nke ọ bụla toro eto na-aghọ ebe nchekwa ederede na `tool_call_id` ya. Ọnụ ọgụgụ dị mma naanị; ụkpụrụ adịghị edobe ma ọ bụ ezighi ezi dowe `16`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOOL_OUTPUT_IMAGE_MAX_BYTES`                     | `20971520` (20 MB)           | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-responses.ts`     | Ngụkọta nha nke ihe nrụpụta ngwaọrụ URLs ezitere ọzọ kwa arịrịọ n'otu ntụgharị asụsụ ahụ. Foto ndị gara aga na mmefu ego na-ebu ụzọ tufuo nke ochie, yana otu ịdọ aka ná ntị n'otu arịrịọ. Ọnụ ọgụgụ dị mma naanị; ụkpụrụ edobereghị ma ọ bụ ezighi ezi dowe `20971520`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_RESPONSES_STRICT_TOOL_HISTORY`                   | `false`                      | `open-sse/translator/helpers/responsesToolHistory.ts` | Otu esi edozi akụkọ ngwa nzaghachi na-ekwekọghị ekwekọ: mmepụta na-enweghị oku, oku na-enweghị mmepụta, na-efu, ogologo oge ma ọ bụ `call_id` na-ezighi ezi, ma ọ bụ arụmụka akụkọ ihe mere eme adịghị mma. Gbanyụọ, OmniRoute na-arụzi akụkọ ihe mere eme wee zipu ya (mpụta nke ụmụ mgbei na-adaba, oku azaghị azịza na-enweta ihe efu, ids na-atụgharị ma ọ bụ na-akụchi, arụmụka na-adịghị mma na-aghọ `{}`). Na, arịrịọ ahụ na-enweta `400` tupu izipu ya; ezi akụkọ ihe mere eme na-ebufe ya na ids oku ya na arụmụka JSON dịka ezipu ya. Na-anabata `true` ma ọ bụ `1`; uru ọ bụla ọzọ na-edobe ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROMPT_EMULATE_TOOLS`                            | _(na)_                       | `open-sse/executors/syntxChat.ts`                     | SYNTX.ai ngwa ngwa-emulated ngwá ọrụ ịkpọ: unset ma ọ bụ `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` igbanye mpaghara ngwá ọrụ katalọgụ n'ime ngwa ngwa; uru ọ bụla ọzọ (dịka `0`) na-amafe ntụtụ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

---

## 7. URLs & Mmekọrịta igwe ojii

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                          | Ọdabara                                                         | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `BASE_URL`                                  | `http://localhost:20128`                                        | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                                                      | N'akụkụ sava URL maka ọrụ mmekọrịta ime ka ịkpọ `/api/sync/cloud`. Debe nke a dị ka loopback/container URL ọbụlagodi mgbe ngwa ahụ bụ proxied ọha. nka A2A na-ejikwa ya (lee `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`) wee daa kama iziga igodo API na plain-http URL ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CLOUD_URL`                                 | _(efu)_                                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                                                      | Cloud relay endpoint URL (atụmatụ adịchaghị).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CLOUD_SYNC_TIMEOUT_MS`                     | `12000`                                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                                                      | HTTP agwụla maka arịrịọ mmekọrịta igwe ojii.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_PROFILE`                   | `full`                                                          | Nhazi nhazi nke ngwugwu Webpack                                             | Profaịlụ nrụpụta oge (dobere na `minimal` ka ewepu modul ndị nwere oke n'anụ ahụ na ngwugwu).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                  | _.build/ nke kwụụrụ onwe ya pụta_                               | `scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`                                     | Mwepụta oge wuo maka ndekọ ndekọ mmepụta nke kwụụrụ onwe ya site na nzọụkwụ nrụgharị nrụpụta gachara. Ọ bụghị nhazi oge ojiri gaa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIST_DIR`                        | `dist/` (mgbọrọgwụ repo)                                        | `scripts/build/prepublish.ts`                                               | Mechie oge maka akwụkwọ ndekọ usoro mbipụta (`prepublish.ts`) na-ede ngwugwu CLI n'ime, kama `dist/` n'okpuru mgbọrọgwụ repo. A na-ehichapụ akwụkwọ ndekọ aha tupu arụ ọrụ ahụ. Ọ bụghị nhazi oge ojiri gaa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET`               | _(efu)_                                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                                                      | Ihe nzuzo ekekọrịtara ejiri iji nyochaa mbinye aka HMAC-SHA256 nke nzaghachi mmekọrịta igwe ojii.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRETS`              | `false`                                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                                                      | Tọọ na `true` iji kwe ka njedebe mmekọrịta Cloud megharịa nzere mpaghara. Ihe ndabara bụ `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_ENFORCE_SIGNATURE`    | `false`                                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                                                      | Tọọ `true` ka jụ nzaghachi mmekọrịta igwe ojii na-edoghị aka mgbe ahazighị nzuzo nzuzo (#13679). A na-enyocha mbinye aka dị ugbu a — mgbe niile ma na-ajụ mgbe ọ bụla `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET` adịghị edozi — n'agbanyeghị ọkọlọtọ a. Ihe ndabara na-atụgharị ka etinyere na v3.9.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ZED_IMPORT_LEGACY_ONE_STEP`      | `false`                                                         | `src/app/api/providers/zed/import/route.ts`                                 | Tọọ `true` ka ọ daa azụ na v3.8.5 otu nzọụkwụ "bubata ihe niile" omume na-enweghị nkwenye onye ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`                      | `http://localhost:20128`                                        | OAuth, Dashboard, mmekọrịta                                                 | URL chere ihu ọha maka OAuth redirect_uri, njikọ Dashboard, yana URLs ọha emepụtara. Tọọ nke a na URL ọha kwụsiri ike mgbe OAuth azụghachi azụ ma ọ bụ njikọ ihe nchọgharị ewepụtara ga-ejirịrị onye nnabata reverse-proxy host.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUD_URL`                     | _(efu)_                                                         | Ndị ahịa-akụkụ                                                              | mirror n'akụkụ onye ahịa nke `CLOUD_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL`                       | _(Edobeghi)_                                                    | `src/shared/services/cloudSyncScheduler.ts`                                 | Ndaghachi azụ azụ maka `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_PORT`                          | _( — na-eweghachighị azụ na `PORT`) _                           | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`                                     | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri ọdịda nke ndị ahịa maka ngosipụta URLs mgbe amaghị ebe esitere (SSR/tests); gụọ tupu `PORT`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`                 | _(Edobeghi)_                                                    | Onye nbibi sitere na ọha, onyonyo URLs                                      | Ebe OmniRoute na-eche ihe nchọgharị ebute ụzọ kacha elu ejiri maka ọhaneze URL yana nkwado mmalite ihe nchọgharị na-abụghị dashboard. Tọọ nke a mgbe OpenWebUI ma ọ bụ relay ọzọ rutere OmniRoute site n'ime URL mana ihe nchọgharị onye ọrụ ga-ebuterịrị mgbasa ozi emepụtara site na LAN, ọwara, ma ọ bụ ebe ọha. Mee** ọ bụghị** gụnyere `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL`           | _(Edobeghi)_                                                    | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`                              | Ngwa mgbakwunye zuru oke na-eweta URL kpọsara ndị ahịa sidecar. Mgbe edobere, OmniRoute na-enweta `/api/v1/provider-plugin-manifest` site na mbido arịrịọ ma ọ bụ HOST/PORT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_PROTOCOL`                 | `http`                                                          | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`                              | Protocol eji eme ihe mgbe ị na-enweta ngwa mgbakwunye onye na-eweta ihe ngosi URL site na HOST/PORT na-enweghị mmalite arịrịọ. Tọọ `https` n'azụ proxy ọha TLS na-akwụsị mgbe enweghị `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL` doro anya edobere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY`                     | _(Edobeghi)_                                                    | `src/server/origin/publicOrigin.ts`                                         | Ụdị ntụkwasị obi nhọrọ maka nkụnye ndị isi sitere na ọha ezigara. Akwụsịghị = atụkwasịla `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` maka mkpebi nchekwa. `true` / `loopback` tụkwasịrị host/proto ntụkwasị obi naanị site na proxy loopback akara akara. `private` / `lan` na-atụkwasịkwa obi na ndị ọgbọ proxy-LAN nkeonwe, na-ahapụ àkwà mmiri `API_PORT` na-edebe `X-Forwarded-For` ndị ọgbọ dị otú ahụ, na-ahapụkwa ụzọ LOCAL_ONLY ịnakwere onye ahịa netwọk nke onwe nke na-akọ proxy (ọ dịghị mgbe arịrịọ Cloudflare-tunnel, loopback-only chain, or the loopback-only CLI-token gate). Masịrị `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` doro anya na mmepụta.   |
| `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`                          | _(Edobeghi)_                                                    | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                                                 | Nkpọghachi ọhaneze URL maka ọrụ asynchronous kie.ai. Mbugharị kacha kacha elu tupu `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL` na `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

Daghachi azụ mgbe edobere isi mgbanwe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

Ọdaghachi azụ kacha dị ala maka kie.ai oku azụ; ejirikwa ya dị ka URL ọha na eze maka relays ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

Wepụ maka relays / nnwale ndozi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

Wepụ maka relays / nnwale ndozi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS`           | `true`                                                          | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                                            | Synthesize OpenCode CLI nkụnye eji isi mee (Agent-Agent, x-opencode-client/project, request/session UUIDs) na arịrịọ opencode-go/zen elu nke onye ahịa eziteghị ya, yabụ Cloudflare na VPS egress na-anabata ha (#6210/#5997). Na ndabara kemgbe #10571; pụọ na `false`/`0`/`no`/`off`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OPENCODE_USER_AGENT`                       | `opencode/1.18.31`                                              | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`                                         | A na-eji onye ọrụ-nnọchite anya mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị na ọ nweghị onye na-eweta `<PROVIDER>_USER_AGENT` akwadoro. Tinyere naanị na ndị mmebe oghe koodu. Na arịrịọ enweghị igodo ọnụ ụzọ mgbago elu, uru ahaziri nke na-adịghị ebu `opencode/<version >= 1.17>` na-eji ndabara dochie ya kama ịjụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARD_ATTRIBUTION`             | `1` (na)                                                        | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`                                         | Bugharịa nkụnye eji isi mee ngwa ndị ahịa (`x-session-id`, `x-title`, `http-referer`) gaa na ndị na-eweta ihe, yabụ arịrịọ na-amalite n'ime ihe eji edepụtara na-edobe njirimara ya site na rawụta ( igodo mbipụta OpenRouter na ndị a). `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` na-edobe njirimara registry/operator wee tụda ụkpụrụ ndị ahịa wetara. nkụnye eji isi mee ahaziri onye ọrụ na-emeri ụkpụrụ ndị ahịa mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OPENCODE_CLIENT`                           | `desktop`                                                       | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                                            | Uru maka nkụnye eji isi mee `x-opencode-client` agwakọtara mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OPENCODE_PROJECT`                          | `global`                                                        | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                                            | Uru maka nkụnye eji isi mee `x-opencode-project` agwakọtara mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_IP_LOCK_PROVIDERS`        | _(Edobeghi)_                                                    | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`                                     | Otu ndị na-eweta ọrụ dị mma bụ ndị egress IP na-akwakọba ọdịda nke elu ha (otu 429 ogige ọ bụla na-ekekọrịta njikọ nwanne na IP). Unset na-eji ndabara-ọkwa efu `opencode,opencode-go,opencode-cli`; `none`/`off`/`false`/`0` na-agbanyụ kascade ahụ kpamkpam; ndepụta nke kewara rikoma na-anọchi ntọala. A na-akwakọba atụmatụ ugwo n'elu akaụntụ ma nwee ike gbanyụọ ya (#9611).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_QUOTA_ROTATION_PROVIDERS` | _(Edobeghi)_                                                    | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`                                     | Oke ngbanwe ngbanwe ọjụjụ. Unset na-ewepụta onye otu ọdọ mmiri na 429 naanị maka ezinaụlọ nwere bọket IP n'elu; `all` na-agbatị akwụkwọ ahụ nye onye ọ bụla na-eweta ya, yabụ oke ọnụego a na-enweta site n'aka onye otu na-eme nhọrọ ọzọ n'ebe ọzọ. A na-eleghara uru ọ bụla na-abụghị `all` anya. Chọrọ `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED` (na ndabara). A na-emechi ụlọ ahịa ahụ site na adreesị egress, ọ bụghị (onye na-eweta, adreesị), ya mere ogige ntụrụndụ 429 gbasaa maka onye ọ bụla na-enye ọrụ ruo mgbe oge ahụ gafere (Nkeji 5, okpukpu abụọ ruo 15). Gụọ kwa nsonaazụ nzaghachi, yabụ mgbanwe na-emetụta na-enweghị mmalite. |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_REQUEST_CONTRACT`       | _(Edobeghi)_                                                    | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts`                            | Tọọ `off` ka ịkwụsị ịhazigharị ahụ arịrịọ OpenCode enweghị igodo (ọkọlọtọ nkwanye na ndepụta ngwaọrụ). A ka na-etinye nkụnye eji isi mee. Gụọ kwa arịrịọ, ka mgbanwe na-amalite ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_PLACEHOLDER_TOOLS`      | _(Edobeghi)_                                                    | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts`                            | Aha ngwa nke kewapụrụ rikoma iji kwuwapụta na arịrịọ OpenCode na-enweghị isi nke na-ebughị nke ọ bụla, mgbe a hụbeghị ihe ọ bụla maka ụdị ahụ. Ihe efu daa azụ n'otu ebe a gwara ihe nlereanya ahụ ka ọ ghara ịkpọ. Ọtụtụ ndenye 32, `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}`; eleghara ndenye na-ezighi ezi anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_CREDENTIAL`             | `public`                                                        | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts`                            | Ezitere nzere n'okporo ụzọ n'efu (`https://opencode.ai/inference/v1`). Kemgbe 2026-10-21 ụzọ ahụ na-aza 403 `Model access is disabled` na ezigbo igodo API ma na-eje ozi nkịtị na-amaghị aha kama. `key` na-edobe igodo API onye ọrụ, `none`/`off` anaghị eziga nkụnye eji isi mee nzere, a na-ezigara okwu nkịtị ọ bụla ọzọ dị ka akara onye na-ebu ya. Gụọ kwa arịrịọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_RELAY_URL`              | ịtọhapụ                                                         | `src/lib/opencodeFreeRelay.ts`                                              | Ntọala URL nke Bun relay maka arịrịọ OpenCode n'efu. Mgbe edobere, arịrịọ ndị ahụ na-ebu usoro OpenCode CLI ngwa ngwa na ntọala ngwaọrụ wee ziga ya site na relay (`scripts/ops/opencode-free-relay.ts`), n'ihi na elu na-aza 429 nye ndị ahịa na-abụghị Bun HTTP. A na-eji sava ahụ malite loopback URLs mgbe `bun` nọ na PATH; a na-eleghara ụzọ na URL. A na-anabata `http://` dị larịị naanị maka ndị ọbịa loopback (`localhost`, `127.0.0.0/8`, `::1`); onye ọbịa ọ bụla ga-eji `https://`, ma ọ bụghị ya, arịrịọ na-ada na njehie doro anya. Naanị mmecha nkata. Gụọ kwa arịrịọ.                                                 |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_BUN`                   | `bun` na PATH                                                   | `src/lib/opencodeFreeRelay.ts`                                              | Bun binary ji malite relay.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_SCRIPT`                | `scripts/ops/opencode-free-relay.ts` n'okpuru mgbọrọgwụ ngwugwu | `src/lib/opencodeFreeRelay.ts`                                              | Ụzọ nke edemede relay. A na-edozi ihe ndabara site na mgbọrọgwụ ngwugwu arụnyere, mgbe ahụ ndekọ ndekọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_HOST`                  | `127.0.0.1`                                                     | `scripts/ops/opencode-free-relay.ts`                                        | Relay listen host.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_PORT`                  | `20199`                                                         | `scripts/ops/opencode-free-relay.ts`                                        | Relay ntị ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_UPSTREAM`              | `https://opencode.ai`                                           | `scripts/ops/opencode-free-relay.ts`                                        | Malite relay na-aga n'ihu. Ga-abụrịrị `https://` (`http://` naanị maka mmalite nnwale loopback); relay jụrụ ịmalite ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_TOKEN`                 | ịtọhapụ                                                         | `scripts/ops/opencode-free-relay.ts`, `open-sse/executors/opencodeRelay.ts` | Ekekọrịtara nzuzo. OmniRoute ezipu ya na `x-opencode-relay-auth`; relay na-aza 401 na-enweghị ya. Achọrọ: relay nwere `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_HOST` na-abụghị loopback jụrụ ịmalite na-enweghị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_PROXY_ALLOWLIST`       | ịtọhapụ                                                         | `scripts/ops/opencode-free-relay.ts`                                        | `host` ma ọ bụ `host:port` egress kewapụrụ rịkọm na-anabata relay na-abụghị loopback na-anabata ndị na-akpọ oku (`x-opencode-relay-proxy`); ọ bụla ọzọ proxy zara 403. Ignored on a loopback bind.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_URL`          | `https://ollama.com/settings`                                   | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                                                | Ollama Ntọala igwe ojii URL ejiri maka mkpochapụ oke. Wepụ maka relays / nnwale ndozi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_BALANCE_URL`        | `https://ollama.com/api/balance`                                | `open-sse/services/ollamaCloudBalance.ts`                                   | Ollama Cloud dekọtara njedebe nguzozi n'elu (`GET https://ollama.com/api/balance`, `Authorization: Bearer <API key>`). Gụọ otu ugboro na modul ibu. Ejiri mbụ maka Ollama Cloud quota mgbe njikọ ahụ nwere igodo API ; kuki kuki ntọala-ibe ahụ na-anọ dị ka ọdịda azụ. Wepụ maka relays / nnwale ndozi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`                       | _(Edobeghi)_                                                    | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                                                | Kuki Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` ejiri mee ihe maka mkpochapụ ibe-ibe nhazi. Mmetụta; na-ahọrọ ogige Dashboard njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahazire ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_COOKIE`                 | _(Edobeghi)_                                                    | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                                                | Kuki Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` ọzọ env var. Mmetụta; na-ahọrọ ogige Dashboard njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahazire ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`             | _(Edobeghi)_                                                    | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                                                | Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` kuki env var ọzọ ejiri tupu utu aha dị mkpụmkpụ. Mmetụta; na-ahọrọ ogige Dashboard njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahazire ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEWHISPERER_BASE_URL`          | `https://codewhisperer.us-east-1.amazonaws.com`                 | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                                                | CodeWhisperer (AWS Kiro) njedebe njedebe ojiji. Wepụ maka relays / nnwale ndozi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Mgbe ị na-ebugharị n'azụ proxy (nginx, Caddy), tọọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` na URL ọha gị kwụsiri ike (dịka, `https://omniroute.example.com`) mgbe OAuth akpọghachite ma ọ bụ njikọ ọha emepụtara ga-eji aha nnabata ahụ. Na-enweghị nke a, OAuth callbacks nwere ike daa n'ihi na redirect_uri agaghị adaba na njikọ ọha ewepụtara nwere ike tụọ aka na mbido akpa.
>
> Debe `BASE_URL` dị ka ime loopback/container URL maka ọrụ nkesa-na-sava. Ejila `Origin` ihe nchọgharị ma ọ bụ aha nnabata ọhaneze maka ihe njiri mara nke ime onwe ya.
>
> Akwụkwọ dashboard enwetara nke ọma anaghị achọ ebe ọhaneze kwụ ọtọ URL: dashboard ahụ na-eziga arịrịọ na-adịghị mma site na iji akara CSRF nwere oge. OmniRoute ka na-emezi nkwado ndabere nke ọha maka njikọta ihe nchọgharị na-abụghị dashboard: ezi ntụkwasị obi URL env vars ọha na-ebu ụzọ tụkwasị obi; A na-eleghara ndị nkụnye eji isi mee `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` ma ọ bụrụ na enyereghị aka `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` yana akara ndị ọgbọ proxy ozugbo dị ka ntụkwasị obi. Ejila ntọala CORS dozie arịrịọ dashboard nke sitere na otu; CORS bụ naanị maka ndị ahịa ihe nchọgharị gafere.
