## 8. Proxy na-apụ apụ

Ndị na-eweta LLM na-aga elu na-akpọ site na HTTP ma ọ bụ SOCKS5 proxy maka njikwa egress, geo-routing, ma ọ bụ IP masking.

`OMNIROUTE_UPSTREAM_HTTP2_ENABLED` ndabara na `true`. Tọọ ya na `false`, `0`, `no`, ma ọ bụ `off` ịmanye HTTP/1.1 na ndị na-ezigara Undici na `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts` na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`: kpọmkwem egress, HTTP(S)/SOCKS proxies, relays, na ha ọhụrụ-socket mbigharịa. Nke a na-echekwa nkwenye akwụkwọ TLS, nyocha proxy, oke njikọ na ntọala idobe ndụ. Malitegharịa OmniRoute mgbe ị gbanwee ya n'ihi na a na-echekwa ndị na-ezipụ ozi na-emepụta ndị ọrụ relay na mmalite. Mgbanwe ahụ anaghị ejikwa akara mkpịsị aka wreq TLS ma ọ bụ ụgbọ njem akọwapụtara nke onye na-eweta ya.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                       | Ọdabara      | Isi mmalite faịlụ                              | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ---------------------------------------- | ------------ | ---------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`       | `open-sse/executors`                           | Kwado onye nnọchi anya SOCKS5 maka oku elu. Wepụ na `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_GZIP_REQUEST_BODIES`          | `true`       | `open-sse/executors/base/gzipRequestBody.ts`   | Gzip ahụ arịrịọ ezigara na `api.anthropic.com` gọọmentị (mkpụrụedemede + 4096, naanị njikọ kpọmkwem; mafere n'azụ onye nnọchi anya, TLS impersonation ma ọ bụ `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS`). Anthropic na-anabata gzip naanị. Ntinye koodu jụrụ (415, 400/403 na-abụghị nke Anthropic, ma ọ bụ mmalite JSON-parse 400) na-ewe iwe na-enweghị mkpakọ otu ugboro na mmalite ya na-anọkwa n'enweghị mgbako ruo otu elekere. Wepụ na `0`, `false`, `off` ma ọ bụ `no`. |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`       | Ndị ahịa-akụkụ                                 | Mmata n'akụkụ ndị ahịa maka nnweta SOCKS5.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `true`       | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara na-na ndabara (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB meriri emerie). Ọdọ mmiri proxy na ntụgharị akaụntụ ọ bụla kwụsịrị ijere onye otu nke dara ada (ajụjụ TCP nyocha, ma ọ bụ 429 site na ya) maka oge na-abawanye ugboro abụọ na nkwugharị ọ bụla, ruo okpu. `false` weghachite nhọrọ dị larịị.                                                                                                                     |
| `SELECTOR_CONTROL_ALLOWLIST`             | _(Edobeghi)_ | `src/lib/proxySubscription/selectorGuard.ts`   | Nye ndepụta ohere maka ebumnuche njikwa mpaghara-isi nke gafere loopback (comma/space kewapụrụ ndị ọbịa ma ọ bụ IPs, `*` ga-emepe LAN/private mana ọ dịghị mgbe metadata/link-local). A na-anabataghachi azụ azụ http/https na ndabara. Uru dashboard DB na-emeri mgbanwe a mgbe edobere na enweghị efu.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_BASE_MS`                | `300000`     | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Oge ntọala (ms) nke 429 ewepụtara, gbagoro okpukpu abụọ na nkwugharị ọ bụla. Gụọ otu ugboro na mmalite. Akpọrọ ya na `1000..3600000`; na-abụghị oke ma ọ bụ enweghị ọgụgụ na-adaghachi na ndabara na ịdọ aka ná ntị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_MAX_MS`                 | `900000`     | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Okpu (ms) nke 429 set-side. Gụọ otu ugboro na mmalite. Akpọrọ ya na `1000..3600000`; na-abụghị oke ma ọ bụ enweghị ọgụgụ na-adaghachi na ndabara na ịdọ aka ná ntị. Mgbe okpu dị n'okpuru ntọala, ọ na-adaghachi na ndabara. Iweda okpu na-ebelata ogologo oge ichefu: ndenye na-ehichapụ `2 x max` ka ọ gwụchara.                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_POOL_SHARED_EGRESS_ORDER`         | `false`      | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara Nwepu (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB override meriri). Maka ndị na-eweta oke nke adreesị egress kpuchiri, họpụta onye otu ọdọ mmiri na-ekerịta adreesị egress onye otu jụrụ nso nso a dị n'okpuru ndị otu ahụike. Naanị ịtụ, ewepughị ya. Chọrọ `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`, nke na-emepụta mgbama ọjụjụ ọ na-agụ.                                                                                           |
| `PROXY_WEBHOOK_REBOUND_MS`               | `300000`     | `src/lib/proxyEvents/proxyTransitionBridge.ts` | Window nlọghachi (ms) capping kwugharịa `proxy.set_aside` webhook alerts kwa onye otu ọdọ mmiri ka mwapu ajụ na-eziga otu mmemme. Gụọ na nke ọ bụla emit. Akpọrọ ya na `1000..3600000`; na-anọghị nso ma ọ bụ enweghị ọgụgụ na-adaghachi na ndabara na-agba nkịtị.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(Edobeghi)_ | Node.js ọkọlọtọ                                | HTTP proxy maka oku elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(Edobeghi)_ | Node.js ọkọlọtọ                                | HTTPS proxy maka oku elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(Edobeghi)_ | Node.js ọkọlọtọ                                | Proxy zuru ụwa ọnụ (na-akwado `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(Edobeghi)_ | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                   | Na-atụnye ebumnuche echo-IP nke proxy egress nyocha na-eji na otu URL. Atọghaghị, nyocha ahụ na-anwa `api64.ipify.org` wee `api4.ipify.org` ya mere, a naghị akọpụta na ọwara IPv4-only anwụọla (#9694).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(Edobeghi)_ | Node.js ọkọlọtọ                                | hostnames/IPs kewapụrụ rikoma ka ịgafe proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`         | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`            | Oke sọket ndị na-emekọ ihe n'otu onye na-ezipụ HTTP/SOCKS proxy nke echekwara. iyi SSE dị ogologo dị ka Codex `/v1/responses` chọrọ njikọ karịa otu mgbe ọtụtụ arịrịọ na-ekekọrịta otu proxy ọkwa akaụntụ. Uru dị n'elu `256` ka agbachiela.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`      | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts`   | SOCKS5 aka aka (jikọọ) akwụsịla na ms. Welie ya mgbe otu nnukwu ọnụ ụzọ ámá nwere otu onye ọbịa (dịka ọmụmaatụ 100 arịrịọ n'otu oge) — ezigbo mmanye aka nwere ike gafere 10s n'okpuru ọdọ mmiri juru eju n'agbanyeghị na enwere ike iru proxy, nke na-apụta dị ka `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable` ụgha. Edebere na `120000`.                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`      | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`              | Mgbe `false` (ndabara), arịrịọ onye proxy ekenyere na-emezughị bụ** jụrụ (emechiri emechi)** kama ịdaghachi azụ na njikọ kpọmkwem — na-egbochi ezigbo ntapu IP. Tọọ `true` iji weghachi azụ azụ azụ DIRECT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`      | `open-sse/executors`                           | Mpempe mkpịsị TLS na-eji wreq-js (na-eṅomi Chrome 124). Mgbochi JA3/JA4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(Edobeghi)_ | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                 | Ndepụta ohere ndị na-eweta rịkọm maka ụzọ TLS proxied ọhụrụ (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`). Direct TLS na-edobe omume ihe nketa ya mgbe edobere ya; naanị ndị na-enye ndị a na-aga site na akwa akara mkpịsị aka Chrome-124. ewepu Groq (`api.groq.com`) ọbụlagodi mgbe edobere listi ikike, n'ihi na Chrome JA3 na-ebute Cloudflare 1010 ebe undici nwa afọ ruru API.                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`      | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts`   | Kwe ka ọnọdụ ihe nchọgharị Claude Turnstile Playwright leghara njehie akwụkwọ HTTPS anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

### Ihe atụ

| Ihe atụ                                       | Nhazi                                                                                                                     |
| --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **SOCKS5 site n'ọwara SSH**                   | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                           |
| **Ụlọ ọrụ HTTP proxy**                        | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com` |
| **Mgbochi mkpịsị aka**                        | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` — chọrọ `wreq-js` (gụnyere)                                                                 |
| **Egress na-achịkwa / enweghị ohere ozugbo**  | Hapụ `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (nke ndabara). Arịrịọ na-ada nke ọma mgbe proxy adịghị adị kama isite n'aka ozugbo.          |
| **Legacy/ dev — kwe ka ndaghachi azụ ozugbo** | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Na-eweghachi omume tupu ikesi ike: njikọ a na-eji mgbe mkpebi proxy dara.                         |

> **Mara (NVIDIA nkwado uzo — #3226):** NVIDIA API-igodo nkwado nkwado ebe njedebe mgbe a chụpụrụ ya na proxy/TLS-patched fetch zuru ụwa ọnụ (unici dispatcher → 504). `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` kpachaara anya na-agafe patch proxy maka otu oku nkwado ahụ site na iji `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`. Nke a bụ akwụkwọ edebere, wezuga oke — ọ na-eme** ọ bụghị** emetụta chat/usage egress. Eji `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts` kechie uzo ngafe ahụ.

## 9. CLI Ngwakọta Ngwa

Na-achịkwa ka OmniRoute si achọpụta ma wepụta CLI sidecars (Claude Code, Codex, wdg).

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                | Ọdabara                                                 | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                                                  | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | `auto` = usoro ọchụchọ PATH; `manual` = jiri naanị ụzọ doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CLI_EXTRA_PATHS`                 | _(Edobeghi)_                                            | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ndenye PATH agbakwunyere maka nchọpụta ọnụọgụ abụọ CLI (nkewapụrụ n'ọnụ ọnụ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CLI_CONFIG_HOME`                 | _(Edobeghi)_                                            | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Wepụ ndekọ ụlọ maka ịgụ CLI configs (`~/.claude`, `~/.codex`). Ga-abụ nke zuru oke na n'ime usoro a n'ụlọ —** ma ọ bụ** , n'ime akpa, ụzọ a na-ekekọta (nke ahụ bụ otú `/host-home` si arụ ọrụ). Ihe ọ bụla ọzọ na-adaghachi na dir home.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`         | `true`                                                  | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Kwe ka OmniRoute dee faịlụ nhazi CLI (nkwalite ume ọhụrụ, data nnọkọ). Tọọ na `false` iji mee ka nhazi CLI ọ bụla dee daa na mperi "na-ede nkwarụ".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CLI_CLAUDE_BIN`                  | `claude`                                                | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala na ọnụọgụ abụọ Claude CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CLI_CODEX_BIN`                   | `codex`                                                 | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala na ọnụọgụ abụọ Codex CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_DROID_BIN`                   | `droid`                                                 | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala na Droid CLI ọnụọgụ abụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_OPENCLAW_BIN`                | `openclaw`                                              | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala na ọnụọgụ abụọ OpenClaw CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CLI_CURSOR_BIN`                  | `agent`, wee `cursor`                                   | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala na ọnụọgụ abụọ onye nnọchite Cursor. Enweghị ya, nchọpụta na-agbalị `agent` na mbụ wee daa azụ na `cursor`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CLI_CLINE_BIN`                   | `cline`                                                 | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala na ọnụọgụ abụọ Cline CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_5DIVE_BIN`                   | `5dive`                                                 | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala gaa na ọnụọgụ abụọ CLI 5dive.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CLI_5DIVE_STATE_DIR`             | `/var/lib/5dive`                                        | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | 5dive's system state dir (profaịlụ auth nwere mgbọrọgwụ); mirrors 5dive nke onwe STATE_DIR ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CLI_CONTINUE_BIN`                | `cn`                                                    | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala gaa n'ihu CLI ọnụọgụ abụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CLI_QODER_BIN`                   | `qodercli`                                              | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala na ọnụọgụ abụọ Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_QWEN_BIN`                    | `qwen`                                                  | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala gaa na koodu Qwen CLI ọnụọgụ abụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CLI_AIDER_BIN`                   | `aider`                                                 | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ omenala gaa na ọnụọgụ abụọ Aider CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

Tọọ nke a na 512000 iji mee ka arịrịọ ahụ dị warara — atụmatụ ahụ na-agụ obere ya. A na-eleghara uru ọ bụla ọzọ anya, yabụ typo enweghị ike ịrịọ maka windo ihe nlereanya ahụ enweghị. |

Ihe ndabara bụ windo gburugburu nke ọnụ ụzọ ámá (1000000); Uru a nwere ike wedata elu ụlọ ahụ, ebulighị ya n'elu windo.                                                                                                                                                                            |

Otu env var WhyCodes na-agụ; ndabara na `~/.whycodes` (`%USERPROFILE%\.whycodes` na Windows).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

### CLI Profaịlụ Auto-mmekọrịta

Ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ ndị a abanyela na ndabara. Enwere ike ịtụgharị ha na dashboard koodu CLI.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                    | Ọdabara | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Mgbe mmekọrịta ụdị onye na-eweta, na-edegharị faịlụ profaịlụ `~/.codex/*.config.toml` na-akpaghị aka site na katalọgụ dị ndụ. chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; adịghị agbanwe agbanwe active/default Codex config, auth, Codex-lb ntọala, ma ọ bụ onye na-eweta nhọrọ.                |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Ka emechara mmekọrịta ụdị onye na-eweta, na-edegharị faịlụ profaịlụ `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` Claude na-akpaghị aka site na katalọgụ dị ndụ. chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; anaghị agbanwe agbanwe active/default Claude config, auth, ma ọ bụ onye na-eweta nhọrọ. |

### Docker Ihe Nlereanya

```bash
# Mount host binaries into the container and tell OmniRoute where they are:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` na-arụ ọrụ naanị mgbe a na-ejikọta ụzọ ahụ n'ezie site na onye ọbịa — jikọtara ya na ugwu dị ka `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (lee profaịlụ `host` na `docker-compose.yml`). A na-eleghara ụzọ nke na-abụghị n'ime ụlọ ndị na-ebu akpa ma ọ bụ n'elu ugwu, n'ihi na a ga-atụfu ihe odide ebe ahụ mgbe a na-emegharị akpa ahụ.

Ihe onyonyo a na-agba dị ka `USER node`, yabụ `/root` enweghị mgbakwasa bụ** ọ bụghị** ezigbo ngafe.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                       | Ọdabara        | Isi mmalite faịlụ                   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ---------------------------------------- | -------------- | ----------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(na akpaaka)_ | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Mmanye nchọpụta akpa na (`1`/`true`) ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ (`0`/`false`). Naanị achọrọ na oge ọsọ ọsọ, nchọpụta akpaaka na-efunahụ ya.                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`        | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Kwe ka nhazi ngwa ngwa CLI na-ede n'ime ụzọ akpa enweghị n'agbanyeghị. Ihe nhata CLI bụ `--allow-container-write`.                                                                                                                                        |
| `DOCKER_UPDATE_TRIGGER_FILE`             | ịtọhapụ        | `src/lib/system/autoUpdate.ts`      | naanị foto Docker : dashboard** Melite Ugbu a** na-ede arịrịọ JSON na faịlụ a (ga-abụrịrị n'ime `DATA_DIR`) maka onye na-ahụ maka ndị ọbịa ka ọ dọpụta ma megharịa akpa ahụ. Hụ [ntuziaka Docker](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#dashboard-updates-in-docker). |

### CLI Binary (`omniroute`) ndị enyemaka

Ngbanwe ndị a na-emegharị omume ọnụọgụ abụọ `omniroute` CLI (ọ bụghị nchọpụta akụkụ ụgbọ ala dị n'elu).

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                    | Ọdabara                           | Isi mmalite faịlụ                       | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`                      | _(sistemu) _                      | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Kwado asụsụ mmepụta CLI. mpaghara BCP-47 (dịka `en`, `pt-BR`). Wepụ sistemu mpaghara env vars (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`                  | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Tọọ `1` ka ibugharịa stdout/stderr nkesa na ọnụ na ọnọdụ a na-elekọta. Dakọtara na ọkọlọtọ `--log` na `omniroute serve`.                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`                 | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Agbara akara nyocha igwe dị ka nkụnye eji isi `x-omniroute-cli-token`. Emepụtara akpaaka na ọrụ 8.12.                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `GEMINI_CLI_HOME`                     | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/cli/commands/setup-gemini.mjs`     | mgbọrọgwụ Gemini na-adịgide adịgide; otu agba ọsọ na-eji ụlọ dịpụrụ adịpụ nwa oge.                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHILD_SIGNAL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `5000`                            | `bin/cli/commands/run.mjs`              | Oge amara na milliseconds tupu mmanye-egbu nwa CLI akara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SKIP_DEFAULT_DATA_ENV` | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Tọọ na 1 jiri DATA_DIR doro anya ka ịwụpụ faịlụ gburugburu data-akwụkwọ ndekọ aha nke onye ọrụ ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYNC_TOKEN`                | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/cli/commands/sync.mjs`             | Token mmekọrịta maka `omniroute sync bundle` (`GET /api/sync/bundle` na-anabata naanị akara mmekọrịta, ọ bụghị igodo API ). Dakọtara na `--token`.                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000`                           | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | N'otu oge HTTP nkwụsị oge (ms) maka arịrịọ nkesa CLI →.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                           | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Oge kachasị (ms) CLI na-echere ebe njedebe ahụike nkesa tupu ibipụta ịdọ aka ná ntị nkwụsị oge. Ọ bara uru maka mmalite oyi nwayọ (dịka Windows). A na-edozikwa site na `--ready-timeout`.                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`                   | `0`                               | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Tọọ na `1` ka ibipụta nchọpụta retry/backoff na stderr n'oge iwu CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`               | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Ndekọ ahaziri maka nchọpụta ngwa mgbakwunye CLI (ngwugwu `omniroute-cmd-*` ). Ọ na-adabara `~/.omniroute/plugins/` mgbe ewepụtaghị ya. CLI-naanị — ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-erute ihe nyocha ngwa mgbakwunye akụkụ nkesa, nke `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` na-atụ aka (ngalaba 2).                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER`        | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Wepu nlele mmelite mgbe edobere ya na uru ọ bụla na-adịghị efu: ọkwa CLI na ọpụpụ usoro yana ọkọlọtọ “mmelite dị” dashboard (`GET /api/system/version` wee wụpụ npm, GitHub na nlele ozi). Ọkwa CLI na-anọkwa na CI, ọnọdụ dị jụụ, yana mmepụta anaghị ede.                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCTOR_LIVENESS_URL`       | _(nke ewepụtara)_                 | `bin/cli/commands/doctor.mjs`           | Ndụ URL nke `omniroute doctor` nyochara . Nhọrọ `livenessUrl` mmemme na-ebute ụzọ; ma ọ bụghị ya, a na-eji ọnụ ahịa a ọnụ. Atọghị, CLI na-enweta njedebe `/api/health/degradation` nke dashboard mpaghara.                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_CATALOG_PATH`     | `src/shared/constants/providers/` | `bin/cli/provider-catalog.mjs`          | Ụzọ katalọgụ onye na-enye iwu nke ndị na-eweta CLI. Nhọrọ `catalogPath` mmemme na-ebute ụzọ. A na-eji ụzọ zuru oke eme ihe dị ka; ikwu ụzọ dozie site na mgbọrọgwụ oru ngo. Ewepụghị ya, CLI na-eji akwụkwọ ndekọ aha ndị na-eweta ya, yana faịlụ `providers.ts` nke ketara dị ka ọdịda. |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_TEST_MODEL`       | _(ndabara onye na-eweta ya)_      | `bin/cli/provider-test.mjs`             | Ihe nlere ọdịda zuru ụwa ọnụ maka ule njikọ ndị na-eweta CLI. Ahaziri ụkpụrụ njikọ ụzọ ụzọ, ụdị onye na-eweta kpọmkwem gabigara, uru a, emesia ụdị nnwale arụnyere n'ime onye na-eweta ya.                                                                                               |

---

## 10. Internal Agent & MCP Integrations

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                              | Ọdabara                                             | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`                            | chọpụta akpaaka                                     | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | URL doro anya maka ngwaọrụ MCP/A2A iji ruo OmniRoute. Na-ewepụ nchọpụta akpaaka localhost. nka A2A dara kama izipu igodo API na URL a (ma ọ bụ `BASE_URL` ọ dabara na ya) mgbe ọ dị larịị http gaa na adreesị ọha, aha nnabata ọha nwere ntụpọ ma ọ bụ onye nnabata igwe ojii, ma ọ bụ jide nzere: jiri https, ma ọ bụ http gaa loopback, akpa ma ọ bụ aha ọrụ, ma ọ bụ adreesị nzuzo.                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(Edobeghi)_                                        | MCP/A2A                                                     | igodo API maka oku akụrụngwa MCP. nka A2A na-eziga ya naanị maka onye na-akpọ oku na-enweghị igodo API (nnọkọ dashboard); onye na-akpọ oku ji mkpịsị ugodi na-eji nke ya eme ihe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(Edobeghi)_                                        | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | Igodo ID maka njirimara ndekọ ndekọ ndekọ MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(Edobeghi)_                                        | Ihe nketa                                                   | Aha nke aha maka `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Ụbọchị nke akụkọ ihe mere eme A2A dobere na nchekwa data mpaghara tupu ịsachapụ kwa ụbọchị ihichapụ ahịrị. Atọghị, na-abụghị ọnụọgụ, ma ọ bụ `<= 0` daa azụ na `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Kill-switch maka njiri mara ebe nchekwa A2A. Tọọ na `0` ka ịwụpụ nchọta ncheta ncheta maka ọrụ kpamkpam; uru ọ bụla ọzọ (gụnyere enweghị ntọala) na-eme ka ọ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Na-enyere ndị nnọchi anya offline/local Issue aka ndekọ njedebe njedebe. Hapụ nwere nkwarụ ọ gwụla ma ọ na-arụ ọrụ usoro ndekọ aha nke mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(Edobeghi)_                                        | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Oge agwụla (ms) maka otu onye nnọchite nke mbipụta na-agba ọsọ. Ejidebere ya na oke ime; daa azụ na ndabara arụnyere n'ime ya mgbe edobere ma ọ bụ na ezighi ezi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _(Nkọwa nke na-arụ ọrụ) _                           | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | CLI remote-mode context/profile maka iwu `omniroute` ; na-akagbu ọnọdụ nọ n'ọrụ na ụlọ ahịa ihe ọnọdụ mpaghara. Dakọtara na `--context <name>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT_KEYCHAIN_DISABLED`           | `0`                                                 | `bin/cli/contexts.mjs`                                      | Gbanyụọ azụ azụ `keytar` OS-keychain nhọrọ maka nzere gburugburu CLI. Mgbe enyere ya, nzere na-anọgide na `config.json` mode `0600` na CLI na-ewepụta ịdọ aka ná ntị ọdịda otu oge; e bu n'obi maka ịrụ ọrụ headless/container ụma.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_ENFORCE_SCOPES`                  | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Manye njikwa ohere dabere na obosara na oku ngwaọrụ MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_SCOPES`                          | _(ha niile)_                                        | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Akụkụ ndị kewapụrụ rikoma: `admin`, `combos`, `health`, `models`, `routing`, `budget`, `metrics`, `pricing`, `memory`, `skills`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_RESEARCH_ENABLED`               | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/mcp-server/tools/perplexityResearch.ts`           | Deba aha ngwa nyocha nke mgbagwoju anya MCP. Naanị `1` na-edeba aha ya; ihe ọ bụla ọzọ na-ahapụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_COMPRESS_DESCRIPTIONS`           | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Tinye nkọwa ngwaọrụ MCP tupu ịdepụta ihe ngosi. Kwado ụkpụrụ: `1`, `true`, `on`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_DESCRIPTION_COMPRESSION`         | `rtk`                                               | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Mkpakọ algorithm/profile. Gbanyụọ ụkpụrụ: `0`, `false`, `off`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                | `10000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Akwụsị mmefu ego (ms) maka njikwa ime ihe nkesa MCP-agụ (ahụike, ike resilience, combos, oke, ojiji).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Mwepu mmefu ego (ms) maka MCP hops na-echere onye na-eweta (`route_request`, `web_search`, `web_fetch`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_SESSION_IDLE_MS`                 | `300000`                                            | `open-sse/mcp-server/httpTransport.ts`                      | TTL (ms) na-arụ ọrụ tupu ihicha ahụ aghọrọ nnọkọ MCP na-adịghị arụ ọrụ. A naghị ewe oge ọ bụla nnọkọ nwere iyi mepere emepe ma ọ bụ arịrịọ ụgbọ elu. Ụkpụrụ emeghị nke ọma, ndị na-abụghị ọnụọgụ ma ọ bụ ndị na-adịghị mma na-adaghachi na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE`                   | `5`                                                 | `src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`                         | Onu ogugu kachasi ihe nleba anya mpaghara-corpus echekwabara na ebe nchekwa (LRU, otu n'ime ndekọ aha mgbọrọgwụ indexed). Ejidere na opekempe `1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MODEL_SYNC_INTERVAL_HOURS`                     | `6`                                                 | `src/shared/services/modelSyncScheduler.ts`                 | Ogologo oge mmekọrịta katalọgụ ụdị n'ime awa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES`         | `70`                                                | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oke ọnụego onye na-eweta ya na oge ntuli aka oke.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_SPACING_MS`               | `1500`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oghere (ms) n'etiti nsonye oke OAuth n'usoro n'ime nnukwu mmekọrịta; A na-enweta njikọ OAuth n'otu n'otu ka ịzenarị ịgbawa elu. `0` na-apụ apụ (n'otu oge).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`         | `250`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Obere nkeji (ms) n'etiti ntinye oke n'usoro n'usoro preflight/monitor nke onye ọ bụla; Oghere netwọk n'otu oge na-akpọ ọtụtụ akaụntụ na otu IP anaghị agbawa elu. Ejikọtara n'ime Codex (`/wham/usage`), DeepSeek, Bailian (saịtị abụọ a na-enweta), OpenCode, na Crof quota fetchers (#6009, #6911). Ụzọ izipu `usage.ts::getUsageForProvider` izugbe (github/glm/minimax/nanogpt/xai/etc.) ekpuchibeghị — iche iche. Cache emetụtaghị ya. `0` gbanyụọ; kpọchiri `0..60000`.                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_JITTER_MS`               | `120`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Arịrịọ ndị ọzọ na-enweghị usoro (ms), atụnyere n'ọkwa ọ bụla echekwabara n'elu iyi. Ihe ndabara na 120; kpọgidere `0..60000`; `0` na-ewepu naanị jitter. Tọọ `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS=0` iji gbanyụọ trotul dum. Jitter anaghị ekwe nkwa mgbochi mmachibido iwu elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CODEX_QUOTA_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                  | `8000`                                              | `open-sse/services/codexQuotaFetcher.ts`                    | Ọgbụgba (ms) maka otu Codex quota fetch (`chatgpt.com/backend-api/wham/usage`). Ụgbọ elu na-eche mbute a tupu izipu arịrịọ ahụ. Mgbe oge nkwụsị ma ọ bụ njehie njem gasịrị, a ga-ejigharị foto ikpeazụ site na nkeji iri na ise gara aga ma ọ bụrụ na windo ya agwụla ma ọ bụ tọgharịa; n'enweghị nke ọ bụla, amaghi oke oke ahụ, ya mere a na-amafe akaụntụ kredit akwụ ụgwọ maka arịrịọ ahụ ma ọ nweghị nkwụsịtụ na-adịgide. Nabata ọnụọgụ dị mma ruo `2147483647`; uru ọ bụla ọzọ na-adaghachi na ndabara. |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_POST_USAGE_REFRESH_DELAY_MS`   | `5000`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Detuo (ms) tupu amachie ndị na-eweta ume ka emechara ihe omume n'ezie, na-enye oge API nke elu iji debanye aha oriri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | chọpụta akpaaka                                     | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`             | Ụzọ zuru oke na Chrome ma ọ bụ Edge executable eji maka mmeghari ohuru Adobe Firefly mmekọrịta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`                 | enyere                                              | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Na-eme ka IMS na steeti ihe ize ndụ ihe nchọgharị dị ọhụrụ yana oge CDP Chrome nwere akaụntụ. Tọọ na `0` iji gbanyụọ mmeghari ihe nchọgharị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`                    | enyere                                              | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Na-aga n'ihu na-arụzi Adobe sessions n'okpuru `DATA_DIR` gafee usoro malitegharịa. Tọọ na `0` ka idowe naanị ebe nchekwa oge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`               | `12000`                                             | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Opekempe kacha nta na millisecond n'etiti Adobe Firefly na-ewepụta ntinye; `0` na-ewepu oghere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`              | `15000`                                             | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Oge dị jụụ karịa na milliseconds ka nrubeisi Adobe nke atọ ọ bụla gachara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS`            | `8000`                                              | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`                   | Base backoff na milliseconds ka nzaghachi Adobe 408 na-agafe agafe; jikọtara ya na nrubeisi ohere gafee na kacha ise mgbalị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MEGABRAIN_API_URL`                             | `https://api.getmegabrain.com`                      | `open-sse/services/usage/megabrain.ts`                      | Ndabere URL maka nnọkọ atụmatụ MegaBrain na oke izu ụka na oke ndị na-eweta (nke ejiri akara OAuth njikọ ma ọ bụ igodo `mb-v1` API kwadoro); mebie naanị maka mgbanaka ma ọ bụ ihe nrụnye ule.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES`         | `false`                                             | `src/instrumentation-node.ts`                               | Gbanyụọ ọrụ ndabere niile: oke ume ọhụrụ, mmekọ ndị na-eweta oke, mgbake njikọ, ahụike nzere, mmekọrịta cloud/model/free-proxy/proxy-health, nhichapụ njide, VACUUM ahaziri na ntọala ntughari ọkụ. bara uru maka CI/test; na nrụnye na-adịte aka, njikọ anaghị eweghachi onwe ya na nchekwa data na-aga n'ihu na-eto eto.                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEADLESS`                            | _(Edobeghi)_                                        | `src/lib/system/headless.ts`                                | Naanị buut proxy-engine: na-awụpụ subsystems nhọrọ, `/dashboard` → 404. Otu dị ka `omniroute serve --headless`. Lee [HEADLESS.md](../guides/HEADLESS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_RUNTIME_BACKGROUND_TASKS`     | _(Edobeghi)_                                        | `src/lib/config/runtimeSettings.ts`                         | Manye ọrụ ndabere n'okpuru nchọpụta ule akpaghị aka. Tọọ `1` ka ịkagbu ule heuristic.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUDGET_RESET_JOB_INTERVAL_MS`        | `600000`                                            | `src/lib/jobs/budgetResetJob.ts`                            | Nleba anya ntọgharị mmefu ego (ms). Ụlọ `10000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

### OAuth CLI Bridge (N'ime)

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe  | Ọdabara         | Isi mmalite faịlụ               | Nkọwa                                             |
| ------------------- | --------------- | ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | chọpụta akpaaka | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Ihe nkesa URL maka akwa mmiri CLI↔OmniRoute auth. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(Edobeghi)_    | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Ihe nrịbama maka àkwà mmiri CLI.                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`           | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Onye ọrụ ID maka nnọkọ akwa mmiri CLI.            |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(Edobeghi)_    | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha nke aha maka `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`.              |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(Edobeghi)_    | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha nke aha maka `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.               |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(Edobeghi)_    | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha nke aha maka `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`.             |

## 11. OAuth nzere onye na-enye

### Ntugharị akara Codex na-agafe agafe

`OMNIROUTE_TOKEN_REFRESH_PASSIVE_PROVIDERS` atọrọ na `codex` na-eme ihe atụ a ka ọ bụrụ onye na-eji nzere Codex emekọrịtara ọnụ, ọ bụghị onye nwe ntụgharị ntughari ume ọhụrụ. Ihe ndabara efu ma ọ bụ atọkwaghị na-echekwa omume ume ọhụrụ. A na-akpachapụ ụkpụrụ ndị kewara rịkọm na enweghị mmetụta; `cx` bu utu aha maka `codex`. Ndị na-enye nkwado anaghị akwado nkwado mmalite. Ndị na-enye ndị ọzọ agbanwebeghị.

N'ebe a na-enye ume ọhụrụ dị ugbu a, ihe atụ na-agafe cache njikọ wee nabata naanị ihe dị iche, akara ohere agwụbeghị. Tokens jụrụ n'elu iyi n'oge arịrịọ ahụ enweghị ike iru eru ọzọ ka mmekọrịta gachara azụ: nzere arịrịọ ahụ na-ejide n'ọtụtụ akara mkpịsị aka SHA-256 anọ, tụfuo ya na arịrịọ ahụ. Naanị oghere akara ka anabatara, yabụ metadata onye na-eweta arịrịọ na-adị ndụ. Ọ nweghị nzere anabatara anabatara. Ọ bụrụ na enweghị ngbanwe dị, `refresh_disabled` abụghị ọnụ ma ọ naghị agụta na mgbaji sekit na-enye ume ọhụrụ. Nzaghachi 401/403 n'ezie na-ejigide ndekọ ndekọ ọdịda nkịtị, oyi oyi n'ọnụ na njehie ndị ahịa; akara nbipu akaụntụ ka na-akpọchikwa kpamkpam.

Akwụkwọ ntuziaka dashboard/API ume weghachi `409` nwere koodu `refresh_disabled` yana ntụzịaka iji nweta ume na ihe atụ na-arụ ọrụ yana mekọrịta. Ntugharị ozugbo (akparịta ụka, ojiji, nyocha ahụike na nkwado mbubata) anaghị egosipụta njehie ahụ; ojiji a na-achọrọ 401 mgbake na-emegharị otu ugboro site na iji akara emekọrịtara anabatara. mbubata Codex auth na-echekwa akara ndị enyere na-enweghị mgbanwe mgbanwe ume ọhụrụ; nbanye OAuth ọhụrụ agbanwebeghị. Ọ nweghị mmekọrịta tupu izipu agbakwunyere na mpụga ụzọ ume ọhụrụ dị adị.

Debe otu onye nwe ntụgharị ma hazie mmekọrịta akara iche iche, tupu ohere akara agwụ (ndụ ụzọ na-arụ ọrụ nke ọma: nkeji ise). Nhọrọ a abụghị mkpọchi ekesara, ndegharị nchekwa data ma ọ bụ nbunye SQLite nkekọrịta. Isi mmalite: `open-sse/services/tokenRefresh/passiveCodex.ts`.

Asambodo arụnyere maka** mmepe localhost** . Maka mbugharị ndị dịpụrụ adịpụ, debanye aha nke gị na njikwa onye nrụpụta nke ọ bụla.

Daghachi azụ na `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
