Daghachi azụ na `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`                       | GitLab Duo (v3.8)             | Wepu GitLab base URL ( GitLab ji aka ya kwado). Ihe ndabara na `https://gitlab.com`. Daghachi azụ na `GITLAB_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `GITLAB_BASE_URL`                           | GitLab Duo (v3.8)             | Ndaghachi azụ azụ maka `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`. A na-eji ya mgbe ụdị `_DUO_` adịghị edozi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitLab Duo (v3.8)             | Ndaghachi azụ azụ maka `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` nke `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` riri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)             | Ndaghachi azụ azụ maka `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` nke `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` riri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                 | Qoder                         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `QODER_OAUTH_AUTHORIZE_URL`                 | Qoder                         | Tọọ iji mee ka Qoder OAuth nwee ike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QODER_OAUTH_TOKEN_URL`                     | Qoder                         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `QODER_OAUTH_USERINFO_URL`                  | Qoder                         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Qoder                         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `QODER_PERSONAL_ACCESS_TOKEN`               | Qoder                         | Kpọmkwem igodo API ndaghachi azụ (gafere OAuth).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`                       | Qoder                         | Oghere ọrụ ID maka Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_QODER_WORKSPACE`                 | Qoder                         | Aha aha maka `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `QODER_CLI_CONFIG_DIR`                      | Qoder                         | Wepu Qoder CLI config dir (nnọkọ PAT dịpụrụ adịpụ, na-ezere imechi nbanye ihe nchọgharị).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `QODER_CLI_MAX_CONCURRENCY`                 | Qoder                         | Nke kacha na-agba ọsọ n'otu oge qodercli (bipụta spawns ma ọ bụ ACP kpaliri); ntinye arịrịọ FIFO. Ewepụghị (ndapụta) = enweghị okpu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QODER_CLI_QUEUE_TIMEOUT_MS`                | Qoder                         | Ogologo oge ole ka arịrịọ Qoder na-echere oghere qodercli (naanị na-eji okpu setịpụ) tupu ọ daa na 429 `qodercli_busy` + `Retry-After`. `120000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `QODER_CLI_TRANSPORT`                       | Qoder                         | `print` (ndabere) spawns `qodercli --print` kwa arịrịọ; `acp` na-edobe otu usoro `qodercli --acp` na-adịgide adịgide kwa PAT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | Qoder                         | njem ACP : gbuo usoro qodercli na-adịgide adịgide mgbe oge a na-adịghị arụ ọrụ gasịrị. `600000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_MS`          | Qoder                         | njem ACP : njedebe maka qodercli ịza ACP `initialize`. `60000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_MAX_SESSIONS_PER_PROCESS`    | Qoder                         | njem ACP : megharịa usoro qodercli na-adịgide adịgide mgbe ọtụtụ nnọkọ a gachara. `500`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `BLACKBOX_WEB_VALIDATED_TOKEN`              | Weebụ Blackbox                | Frontend `tk` akara iziga ka `validated` na `/api/chat`. Achọrọ mgbe Blackbox na-amanye ndakọrịta token; ma ọ bụghị ya, OmniRoute na-adaghachi na UUID na-enweghị usoro. Hụ mbipụta #2252.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_BASE_URL`                    | Vision Bridge nche okporo ụzọ | OpenAI-dakọtara base URL maka oku ọhụụ-akwa na-abụghị Anthropic. Edebere na ihe nketa OpenAI URL env ma ọ bụ api.openai.com. Tụtụ aka na OmniRoute's `/v1` onwe-loop ma ọ bụ ihe ọ bụla OpenAI-compat endpoint (Gemini OpenAI-compat, OpenRouter). Okwu #2232. Mgbe URL bụ OmniRoute nke onwe `/v1`, arịrịọ nkọwa nkọwa na-eziga `x-omniroute-admission-bypass: internal` wee gosipụta ya site na nzere nbibi nke onwe (`sk_omniroute` sentinel na ọnọdụ mpaghara, ma ọ bụ `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY` / `ROUTER_API_KEY` — #1350) yabụ `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` ntinye ọrụ na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_API_KEY`                     | Vision Bridge nche okporo ụzọ | igodo API maka URL dị n'elu. Na-ewepụ onye na-eweta OpenAI / Google env vars maka oku ọhụụ-àkwà mmiri na-abụghị nke Anthropic. ụdị Anthropic na-edobe ụzọ igodo Anthropic raara onwe ha nye. Okwu #2232.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VISION_BRIDGE_NEGATIVE_CACHE_MS` | Vision Bridge nche okporo ụzọ | Ogologo oge ole ka a na-echekwa nsonaazụ ntụgharị "enweghị onye ga-eji eme ihe, na ms (ndabere `30000`). Ụkpụrụ ezighi ezi ma ọ bụ nke na-adịghị mma na-adaghachi na ndabara; `0` gbanyụọ cache adịghị mma. Isi mmalite: `src/lib/guardrails/visionBridgeRouter.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_IMAGE_NORMALIZATION`             | Ọhụụ onyonyo normalization    | Wepụ (wepụ = kwụsịrị, mgbanwe omume efu; `1`/`true`/`on` na-enyere aka). Na-emezi inline ọ bụla (base64 data URI / Claude `source.data`) onyonyo dị na arịrịọ nkata tupu izipu: EXIF orientation etinyere, RGB (alpha gbawara agbawa na-acha ọcha), ogologo ihu `<= 4096` px, ngụkọta pikselụ `<= ~4M`; a na-edogharị onyonyo ndị chọrọ mgbanwe dị ka ntọala JPEG ( PNGs na-enweghị alfa nọrọ na-efu PNG), RGB JPEG/PNG na-akwadorịrị na-agafe emetụghị aka. A na-edozi mmefu ego. Ezubere pipeline ọhụụ Qwen2-VL-style nke na-ajụ onyonyo oversized/exotic nwere mperi 400 ma ọ bụ arụpụtaghị ihe efu. Anaghị ebudata URLs http (s) dịpụrụ adịpụ; ọnụọgụ abụọ `sharp` na-efu ma ọ bụ onyonyo a na-apụghị ịkọwapụta na-agafe na emetụghị aka (ọdịda-emeghe). Ịhazigharị nnukwu onyonyo na-agbakwunye latency arịrịọ. Isi mmalite: `open-sse/utils/visionImageNormalization.ts`. |

> [!WARNING]
>
> 1. Gaa na [nzere Google Cloud Console →](https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials) 2. Mepụta OAuth 2.0 Client ID (ụdị: "ngwa Weebụ") 3. Tinye URL nkesa gị dị ka ikike redirect URI 4. Dochie ụkpụrụ nzere na `.env`.

## 12. Onye na-enye ọrụ-onye nnọchi anya na-agbagha

Wepụ nkụnye eji isi mee `User-Agent` ezigara onye ọ bụla na-eweta elu. A na-edozi nke a n'ike n'ike n'oge ọ na-agba ọsọ site na klaasị ndị mmebe:

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Isi mmalite:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe | Uru ndabara | Mgbe imelite |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.280 (external, cli)` | Mgbe Anthropic wepụtara ụdị CLI ọhụrụ |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Tọọ na `1`/`true` ka ibugharịa aha ngwa ngwa ngwa ndị ọzọ na Anthropic n'ụzọ abụọ metụtara Anthropic (nwaafọ OAuth na CLIProxyAPI). Site na ndabara, onye mmebe ahụ na-akpọ aha ndị na-abụghị Claude-Code ngwá ọrụ (Claude Code canonical mapping ebe otu dị, ma ọ bụghị PascalCase) wee tụgharịa ha na nzaghachi site na `_toolNameMap`, yabụ, a naghị ajụ harnesses na snake_case ngwaọrụ dị ka akara mkpịsị aka ndị ahịa ndị ọzọ. Na-emezigharị naanị. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.155.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Mgbe OpenAI na-emelite Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.155.0` | Wepụ ụdị onye ahịa Codex na-adabereghị na eriri UA zuru ezu |
| `GROK_CLI_CLIENT_VERSION` | `1.0.41` | Wepụ `x-grok-client-version` maka ntinye, mmekọrịta nlereanya, na koodu OAuth ngwaọrụ, gbakwunyere Grok CLI Onye ọrụ-Nnọchite anya maka ntinye na mmekọrịta ụdị. Nabata SemVer na nhọrọ prerelease/build metadata, ruo mkpụrụedemede 128 ka ewepụchara oghere ọcha. Ụkpụrụ atọghị, nke efu, adịghị mma, ma ọ bụ akara nwere nkwụsịtụ na-eji ntụtụ ebugoro. Ntinye njupụta nke onye ọrụ-onye nnọchite anya na-ejigide ụzọ. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.280` | Wepụ ụdị koodu Claude akpọsara na-adabereghị na `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT`. Anthropic ọnụ ụzọ ụfọdụ ụdị na uru a (#12417). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_BUILD_REVISION` | `1e2` | Wepụ suffix agwa 3 OmniRoute gbakwunyere na `cc_version=` na ngọngọ ịgba ụgwọ Claude. Bump n'akụkụ `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` — itinye naanị ụdị ahụ na-akpọsa ụzọ `version.revision` enweghị ezigbo ọnụọgụ ọnụọgụ abụọ. |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.91` | Wepụ ụdị Copilot CLI akpọsara na-adabereghị na `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Mgbe GitHub Copilot Chat na-emelite |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_CLI_CL` | _(Edobeghi)_ | Wepụ ndepụta mgbanwe piper Antigravity CLI `agy`) nke akọọrọ dị ka `cl=` na CLI Onye nnọchite anya. Ọ bụghị ewepụtara na mbipute ma ọ bụ kpughere ọha na-egosi; mkpụrụ n'otu ụdị sitere na njide dị ndụ (1.4.4 → `1003595037`). Etughari na ụdị amabeghị na-ewepụ `cl=` kama ịkpọsa ndepụta mgbanwe ihe nrụpụta ọzọ. |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Mgbe Kiro IDE na-emelite |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Wepụ Kiro social device-koodu `clientId` (ID ọha) |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Nbanye: ozi CRC zuru oke n'otu-frame na iyi mmemme Kiro (mezie iyi ndị mebiri emebi) |
| `KIRO_CREDIT_PRICE_USD` | `0.02` | USD kwa Kiro kredit; na-atụgharị `meteringEvent` kredit ka ọ bụrụ ọnụ ahịa arịrịọ ọ bụla |
| `KIRO_REQUIRE_CREDITS` | `false` | Daa nzaghachi Kiro nke na-akọ enweghị kredit meter kama ịdekọ ya enweghị ụgwọ |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Mgbe Qoder CLI na-emelite |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Mgbe Cursor emelitere |

> [!TIP]
> Ị nwere ike ịgbakwunye onye ọrụ-onye nnọchi anya gabigara maka** nke ọ bụla** onye na-eweta na-eji ụkpụrụ `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Onye mmebe ahụ na-ewuli aha env var nke ọma.

## 13. CLI ndakọrịta mkpịsị aka mkpịsị aka

Mgbe enyere ya aka, OmniRoute na-atụgharị ihe nkụnye eji isi HTTP na mpaghara ahụ JSON ka ọ dabara na mbinye aka nke ngwa CLI gọọmentị. Nke a na-ebelata ohere nke ịdekọ akaụntụ mgbe ị na-echekwa IP proxy gị.

**Isi mmalite:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Onye na-eweta ya

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe       | Nkwalite | Mmetụta                          |
| ------------------------ | -------- | -------------------------------- |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`     | Mimics Codex CLI mbinye aka      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`     | Mimics Claude Koodu mbinye aka   |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`     | Mimics GitHub Copilot mbinye aka |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`     | Mimics Antigravity mbinye aka    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`     | Mimics Cursor mbinye aka         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`     | Mimics Kimi Ntinye mbinye aka    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`     | mbinye aka koodu Mimics Kilo     |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`     | Mimics Cline mbinye aka          |

### Gburugburu ụwa

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe | Nkwalite | Mmetụta                                                              |
| ------------------ | -------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL`   | `=1`     | Kwado ndakọrịta akara mkpịsị aka maka** niile** ndị na-enye otu oge. |

### Kimi Ndokwa CLI njirimara na-ewepụ

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe      | Ọdabara            | Isi mmalite faịlụ                        | Nkọwa                                                   |
| ----------------------- | ------------------ | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`           | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Wepụ ụdị Kimi CLI ezitere n'oge oku OAuth/API.          |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(dabara ejidere)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Wepu ngwaọrụ Kimi eweghara ID ejiri na nkụnye ndị ahịa. |

> [!NOTE]
> Njirimara a na-arụ ọrụ n'akụkụ ihe nbibi nke onye ọrụ-onye ọrụ (§12). Sistemụ akara mkpisiaka na-ejikwa ịtụ nkụnye eji isi mee yana ịtụ mpaghara ahụ, ebe onye nnọchi anya onye ọrụ na-ejikwa eriri UA akọwapụtara. Enwere ike ịgbanye ha abụọ n'onwe ha.

## 14. API Key enye

igodo API maka ndị na-eweta na-eji nyocha ozugbo.** Nhazi ọkacha mmasị:** Dashboard → Ndị na-enye → Tinye igodo API.

Ịtọ ntọala site na mgbanwe gburugburu ebe obibi bụ ihe ọzọ maka nnyefe Docker ma ọ bụ enweghị isi.

Ụkpụrụ amatara: `{PROVIDER_ID}_API_KEY`

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe | Onye na-enye                                            |
| ------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | DeepSeek                                                |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY`   | NVIDIA NIM                                              |
| `JINA_AI_API_KEY`  | Jina AI (Ntọala API + Reader fallback)                  |
| `JINA_API_KEY`     | Jina AI (utu aha maka `JINA_AI_API_KEY`)                |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY`   | Gemini (Google AI Studio) agbakwunyere + nkata fallback |
| `GOOGLE_API_KEY`   | Gemini (utu aha maka `GEMINI_API_KEY`)                  |

> [!NOTE]
> E wepụrụ ndenye `${PROVIDER}_API_KEY` static maka Groq, xAI, Mistral, mgbagwoju anya, Together AI, Fireworks, Cerebras, Cohere, Nebius na Qianfan na v3.8.0 n'ihi na oge na-agba ọsọ anaghịzi agụ ha — ndị na-enye ọrụ na-adabere nanị na Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB zoro ezo. Hụ ngalaba _Audit: Wepụrụ / Nwụrụ Anwụ Variables_ na ala nke akwụkwọ a maka ụzọ ọpụpụ.

> [!TIP]
> A na-echekwa igodo ndị edobere site na Dashboard na SQLite wee buru ụzọ na-agbanwe mgbanwe gburugburu ebe obibi.
>
> **Jina:** ntinye `jina-ai/…`, rerank, nkewa, ngalaba, na `jina-search` eme** ọ bụghị** kwụọ igodo ụyọkọ env mgbe njikọ dashboard `jina-ai` (ma ọ bụ `jina-reader` kekọrịtara) dị — `getProviderCredentials` bụ nke mbụ. A na-eji `JINA_AI_API_KEY` / `JINA_API_KEY` naanị mgbe enweghị igodo dashboard enwere ike iji ya dị. Ndekọ oku na-ekwu na azụghachi azụ azụ dị ka `connection_id=env:JINA_AI_API_KEY`. Kaadị Reader (`jina-reader`, `r.jina.ai`) anaghị ejere `/v1/embeddings` ma ọ bụ `/v1/rerank`.
>
> **Gemini:** `gemini/gemini-embedding-2` (alias `google/gemini-embedding-2`) na-ebu ụzọ jikọọ dashboard `gemini`. A na-eji `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` naanị mgbe enweghị igodo dashboard enwere ike iji ya dị. Ndekọ oku na-ekwu na ọdịda env dị ka `connection_id=env:GEMINI_API_KEY`. Okporo ụzọ multimodal nwa afọ na-eji `x-goog-api-key` megide ihe `:embedContent` / `:batchEmbedContents` — N OpenAI `input` na-aghọ N vectors.

## 15. Ntọala oge nkwụsị

Ụkpụrụ niile dị** millisekọnd** . Mkpebi etiti etiti na `src/shared/utils/runtimeTimeouts.ts`.

### Ọchịchị Oge agwụla

```
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (global override)
├─→ FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (upstream provider calls, default: 600000)
│   ├─→ FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   │   └── TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS (independent, default: 10000)
│   ├── RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 15000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS (independent, default: 0 = off, suggested: 30000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS (independent, default: 2)
│   ├── FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 30000)
│   └── FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 4000)
├─→ STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 600000)
├─→ STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent; unset: 1260000, raised by declared budgets; set: hard cap; 0 disables)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 80000)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS (caps adaptive readiness extensions, default: 180000)
└─→ API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 30000)
    ├─→ API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (derived, default: 300000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 60000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 5000)
    └── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 0 = disabled)
```

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                                | Ọdabara                                                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`                              | _(Edobeghi)_                                                      | Ụzọ mkpirisi zuru ụwa ọnụ — kagburu ma ndabara `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` na `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                | `600000`                                                          | Ngụkọta oge arịrịọ HTTP maka oku ndị na-eweta n'elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                          | `600000`                                                          | Oke juu n'etiti bytes nke dị n'elu mmiri tupu ite ime. Ụdị echiche gbatịrị agbatị anaghị akwụsịtụ > 90s.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `1260000`                                                         | Maximum total active SSE stream lifetime; never resets on upstream bytes and is independent of `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. When unset, the default is the largest per-model `timeoutMs` in the registry (1200000, Codex) plus a 60000 margin, and a target that declares a longer budget (connection `timeoutMs`, then per-model, then provider-level, e.g. GLM's 3000000) gets that budget plus 60000 instead. A positive value is a hard cap for every target that declared budgets cannot raise. Set to `0` to disable for every target.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SSE_COMMENTS`                          | _(enweghị nkwarụ)_                                                | Ma OmniRoute nwere ike wepụta ahịrị nkọwa SSE `:` (dịka ọmụmaatụ nku obi `: keepalive` na `x-omniroute-*` metadata trailers). Agbanyụrụ na ndabara (#10524) ebe ọ bụ na ndị ahịa OpenAI dakọtara siri ike JSON.tuchapụ ahịrị SSE ọ bụla wee daa na nkwupụta `:` ; `data:` obi mgbawa na-adịghị emetụta. Tọọ `on`/`true`/`1`/`yes` ka ị nwetaghachi azụ. Nke `open-sse/utils/sseHeartbeat.ts` na-eji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SSE_COALESCE_MS`                                 | `0` (apụ)                                                         | Wepụ: jikọta mmemme Claude `content_block_delta` na-esochi (otu ngọngọ index na ụdị delta) ezigara ndị ahịa Claude, na-agbanye opekata mpe N ms ọ bụla. Enweghị efu; `signature_delta` na ihe omume oke anaghị ejikọta ọnụ. Na-emetụta ụdị delta ọ bụla ma ọ bụrụ na edobere mgbanwe kwa ụdị n'okpuru. Eji `open-sse/utils/sseDeltaCoalesce.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SSE_COALESCE_THINKING_MS`                        | `0` (apụ)                                                         | Ntugharị kwa ụdị nke `SSE_COALESCE_MS` maka `thinking_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `SSE_COALESCE_TEXT_MS`                            | `0` (apụ)                                                         | Ntugharị kwa ụdị nke `SSE_COALESCE_MS` maka `text_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SSE_COALESCE_INPUT_JSON_MS`                      | `0` (apụ)                                                         | Ntugharị kwa ụdị nke `SSE_COALESCE_MS` maka `input_json_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SSE_FRAME_COALESCE_MS`                           | `0` (apụ)                                                         | Wepụ: ogbe SSE ezigara n'ime N ms n'ime otu dee onye ahịa. Agba nke mbụ na okpokolo agba (`[DONE]`, `message_stop`, `error`, `response.completed`/`failed`/`incomplete`) anaghị egbu oge; na batching na, ITL na-atụle oghere ogbe. Eji `open-sse/utils/sseOutputSink.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `SSE_STALL_WATCHDOG`                              | `timer`                                                           | Mmejuputa Byte-stall elele maka nzaghachi mgbasa ozi. `timer` na-eweghachi otu ngụ oge n'otu ntabi anya elu (ọkụ kpọmkwem na mmefu ego ụlọ ahịa); `interval` na-edobe otu nlele kwa iyi na oge `clamp(budget/4, 1s, 5s)`, yabụ ụlọ ahịa na-ere ọkụ n'ime oge + mmefu ego. Eji `open-sse/utils/streamStallWatchdog.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS`                     | `80000`                                                           | Oge iji nweta mmemme na-abụghị ping SSE mbụ. Na-eketa `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` mgbe atọrọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `180000`                                                          | mpio nkwado mmemme izizi kachasị maka arịrịọ nkwanye ugwu buru ibu, ngwa ọrụ dị arọ ma ọ bụ nnukwu ebumnuche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_POLICY_ENABLED`             | `true`                                                            | Gbuo-mgbanwe maka `/goal` heuristic. Tọọ `false`/`0`/`off` iji gbanyụọ nchọta n'ụzọ zuru ezu — oge echere na mgbake iyi anaghị ebuli elu site na arịrịọ body/headers, na-ebelata mmụba oge nkwụsị nke ndị ahịa na-achịkwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `600000`                                                          | Window ịdị njikere mmemme mbụ maka ndị nnọchite `/goal` achọpụtara na-agba ma ọ bụ arịrịọ manyere `x-omniroute-agent-goal`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_STREAM_RECOVERY`            | `true`                                                            | Kwado mgbake mmiri mbụ na-akpaghị aka maka ndị ọrụ `/goal` achọpụtara. Tọọ `false`/`0`/`off` iji gbanyụọ nbanye nke ebumnuche-kpọmkwem. Nke a nwere ike naanị ADD mgbake n'elu onye ọrụ ndabara — ọ dịghị mgbe ọ ga-ewepụ mwepu ntọala `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`/DB doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_DROP_NONSTANDARD_EVENTS`         | `true`                                                            | Wepụ ihe omume `codex.*` SSE na-abụghị ọkọlọtọ (dịka `codex.rate_limits`) nke na-eji 502. ON dapụtara (#11014) mebie OpenAI SDK's `responses.stream()`. Tọọ `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` ka ibugharịa ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS`                    | _(Edobeghi)_                                                      | Wepụ ụgbọ njem ihe nkesa Codex. ebe njedebe WebSocket (`ws://`/`wss://`) nke mpaghara `codex app-server` sidecar. Mgbe ejikọtara ya na token, a na-ebugharị arịrịọ Codex n'elu JSON-RPC n'akụkụ ụgbọ ala kama azịza HTTP API. Enwere ike ịtọlite kwa-njikọ site na `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerUrl`. Eji `open-sse/executors/codex/appServerConfig.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`              | _(Edobeghi)_                                                      | Enyere akara capability/bearer inline n'ime ihe nkesa ngwa. Otu-njikọ gabigara: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN_FILE`         | _(Edobeghi)_                                                      | Ụzọ faịlụ na-ejide akara ike-ihe nkesa (site na `codex app-server --ws-token-file`). A na-eji ya mgbe emebere `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`. Otu-njikọ mebie: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerTokenFile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_CWD`                   | `/tmp`                                                            | Akwụkwọ ndekọ aha na-arụ ọrụ ntụgharị ngwa-ihe nkesa na-abanye. Njikọ ọ bụla wepụrụ: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerCwd`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_APPROVAL`              | _(Edobeghi)_                                                      | Amụma nnabata gafere na ntụgharị ngwa-nkesa (dịka `never`, `on-request`). Otu-njikọ mebie: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerApprovalPolicy`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_SANDBOX`               | _(Edobeghi)_                                                      | Amụma igbe igbe gafere na ntụgharị ngwa-nkesa (dịka `read-only`, `workspace-write`, `danger-full-access`). Mgbe ewepụtaghị ihe mmebe ahụ na `workspace-write` (agbasiri ike; na mbụ `danger-full-access`). Otu-njikọ mebie: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerSandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_AUTO_APPROVE`          | `false`                                                           | Na-akwado nkwado nke ngwa-ihe nkesa na-akpaghị aka (command/file/permission na onye ọbịa). Gbanyụọ na ndabara — kpaliri akpaaka agọnarị; Oku ngwa ngwa ngwa anaghị emetụta (ha na-eme njem `item/tool/call` passthrough dị iche). Nabata `true`/`1`/`yes`. Otu-njikọ mebie: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerAutoApprove`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge ịnata nkụnye eji isi mee nzaghachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_FETCH_START_TIMEOUT_CAP_MS`            | `110000`                                                          | Oke nchere maka isi okwu nzaghachi na arịrịọ nkwanye (ms). `0` gbanyụọ okpu a; onye na-eweta nke ọma `fetchStartTimeoutCapMs` na-ebute ụzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_IMAGE_RESPONSE_START_TIMEOUT_MS` | `600000` (10 min)                                                 | Nzaghachi mbụ-mmalite n'ikpeazụ (ms) maka Codex POSTsimage-generation/edit akwụ ụgwọ, ma kpọmkwem ma site na HTTP(S)/SOCKS proxy. A naghị emegharị arịrịọ ndị a mgbe oge nkwụsịtụ ma ọ bụ ọdịda netwọk gasịrị. Edge relays (`vercel`/`deno`/`cloudflare`) na-emekwa otu mgbalị, mana debe oge nke `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` nke ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `30000` (30s)                                                     | Opekempe nzaghachi-mmalite nchere (ms) maka mbọ onye nnọchi anya ọ bụla. Oge nkwụsịtụ na-emegharị otu ugboro na oghere ọhụrụ; tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ agbụ ma jigide omume gara aga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `300000` (minti 5)                                                | Nzaghachi-mmalite ala (ms) maka mbọ ọnweghị onye nnọchi anya nke onye lekwasịrị anya ya bụ private/LAN/loopback (dịka ọmụmaatụ `localhost`, `host.docker.internal`, `192.168.x.x`), yabụ anaghị ebipụ mmalite oyi Ollama site na ndabara 30s dị n'elu. Na-arụ ọrụ dị ka ala: nchere dị irè bụ ibu nke uru a na `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`; `0` ma ọ bụ uru na-ezighi ezi na-ewepụ ala mpaghara. Ọnweghị mmetuta ndị nnabata anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_IMAGE_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `300000` (minti 5)                                                | Nzaghachi-mmalite ala (ms) maka arịrịọ onyonyo na-enweghị onye nnọchi anya ozugbo: onyonyo generation/edit/variation njedebe (dịka ọmụmaatụ `/v1/images/generations`, `/v1/images/edits`) ma rịọkwa ahụ ndị na-ebu ngwa `image_generation` akwadoro. Na-arụ ọrụ dị ka ala dị ka ala mpaghara n'elu (ọ na-ebuli echere dị irè); `0` ma ọ bụ uru na-ezighi ezi na-ewepụ ya. Codex image-generation/edit POSTs anaghị eji ya: ha na-edobe oge `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_IMAGE_RESPONSE_START_TIMEOUT_MS` raara onwe ya nye.                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_VOLUME_ADAPTIVE`        | `1`                                                               | Welie windo mmalite nzaghachi ozugbo site na arịrịọ ịkwụ ụgwọ ụgwọ: ozu karịrị 512 KB ga-enweta × 2, karịa 2 MB × 3, kpuchie na 180 s na ọ dịghị ewetu ala dị elu dị elu (onye ọrụ na-agafe ma ọ bụ ala local/LAN n'elu). Nnukwu ọnọdụ na-ejupụta nke ọma ka ntinye nha na-enweghị onye na-ahọrọ echiche maka ụlọ echiche ka isi na ya, ya mere mpio dị larịị na-egbutu nnukwu mmiri na-agbago ngwa ngwa n'elu ala kpọmkwem. Tọọ `0`/`false`/`off` ka ọ tụnye omume dị larịị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_RESPONSE_RETRY_TIMEOUT_MS`      | `600000` (10 min)                                                 | Ulo elu (ms) maka mgbali RETRY ọhụrụ-socket ka agbachara mbọ nzaghachi-mmalite oge n'elu (#13703). Na-emetụta naanị mgbe onye na-akpọ oku etinyelarị akara ngosi njedebe nke ya (njikọ edoziziri / ihe nlereanya / onye na-eweta /`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` cascade); mgbaàmà ahụ bụ ezigbo njikọ na ọkụ na mbụ n'ụzọ a chọrọ, yabụ na nke a bụ azụ azụ na-emesapụ aka karịa okpu — dị larịị na-enweghị ya, nnwale ahụ jikwa windo `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS` dị mkpụmkpụ ahụ dị ka nnwale agbakọtara na 504'd ụdị echiche nwayọ nwayọ-TTFB. A naghị ekwe ka ọ dị n'okpuru ala dị larịị n'elu; mgbe onye na-akpọ oku na-enyeghị akara ngosi njedebe oge ọ bụla, ịnwale ọzọ na-eme ka ala dị larịị agbanweghị. |
| `FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS`                           | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge iji nweta ahụ nzaghachi zuru oke.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `30000`                                                           | oge nguzobe njikọ TCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `4000`                                                            | Nọgide na-adị ndụ oghere oge nkwụsị ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS`                           | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | TLS akara mkpisiaka proxy (wreq-js) agwụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS`                      | `10000`                                                           | Na-agbatị oge-na-mbụ-byte na ahụ wreq-js TLS-mkpịsị aka mkpịsị aka ahụ kpọmkwem; `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS` naanị enweghị ike ijide ahụ kwụsịrị ebe ọ na-edozi ozugbo ndị nkụnye eji isi mee bịarutere (#12656). Oge nkwụsị na-akagbu onye na-agụ wreq wee daa azụ na onye na-ezipụ direct/proxy ; `0` gbanyụọ onye nche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `15000`                                                           | OpenCode executor naanị, na naanị mgbe `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_STALL_ROTATION` njirimara ọkọlọtọ dị na (ndapụta kwụsịrị): bounds echere nke mbụ ozu byte nke a streamed Azịza zaghachi mgbe nkụnye ndị isi ya (#13484). A na-emepe iyi nzaghachi na `response.created`, ya mere ịgbachi nkịtị gafere windo a bụ ụlọ ahịa: a na-eme ka akaụntụ ahụ dị jụụ na arịrịọ ahụ na-atụgharị na akaụntụ ọzọ otu ugboro; ụlọ ahịa nke abụọ na-ada ngwa ngwa. `0` gbanyụọ onye nche ọbụna na ọkọlọtọ na.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS`              | `0` (= kwụsịrị)                                                   | OpenCode executor naanị: kpachie nchere maka isi okwu nzaghachi elu na oku nzaghachi mgbe akaụntụ ọzọ dị — arịrịọ kwụ n'ahịrị kwụsịrị ichere wee gaa n'ihu kama ijide windo nkụnye eji isi mee n'uju. Naanị oku nzaghachi agbasa ozi na-emetụta (Oyi nzaghachi na-emepe na `response.created`, ya mere ịgbachi nkịtị enwere kwụ n'ahịrị, ọ bụghị ọgbọ); Emecha nkata na oku na-abụghị iyi, na mpio ahụ na-ebelata ụlọ mmalite mmalite dị mma, ọ dịghị mgbe ọ ga-agbatị ya. Uru atụnyere mgbe ị na-enyere: `30000`. `0` gbanyụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS`   | `2`                                                               | OpenCode executor naanị: ugboro ole n'otu arịrịọ, ndị nkụnye eji isi mee echere n'elu nwere ike ịkwaga na akaụntụ ọzọ. Akaụntụ ikpeazụ fọdụrụ na-edobe windo nkụnye eji isi mee oge niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_AND_RESUME`                        | `false`                                                           | OpenCode executor naanị: jiri obi mgbawa dọba arịrịọ ahụ ka 429s na-agafe agafe ugboro ugboro (ma ọ bụ akara nrịbama ọdọ mmiri ọhụrụ), wee gụgharịa otu ụkwụ nwere mkpuchi ihe ruru akaụntụ 3 n'usoro kama ịchụpụ ụgbọ mmiri niile (#13924). Gbanyụọ na ndabara: 429 ọ bụla na-atụgharị gaa na akaụntụ ọzọ dịka ọ dị na mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_REPLAY_SERVE_SETASIDE`             | `false`                                                           | OpenCode executor naanị: mgbe ọ bụla akaụntụ cooldown-njikere na ogige-na-resume replay ụkwụ na-ewepụtara site na proxy-ọjụjụ ebe nchekwa, na-ejere ha ozi na agbanyeghị kama ịmegharị ihe ọ bụla (#14851, na-esochi na #14750). Gbanyụọ na ndabara: ụkwụ na-emegharị ahụ bụ ihe efu n'ọnọdụ ahụ, dịka ọ dị na mbụ #14750.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_READINESS_STALL_RETRY`                    | `false`                                                           | Mkparịta ụka gụgharia naanị: mgbe ahụ mbụ dị elu kwụsịrị tupu ya emee mmemme enwere ike, wepụta otu mbọ nke abụọ site n'otu ụzọ ntụgharị ahụ yana otu mmefu ego njikere na enweghị ntaramahụhụ akaụntụ. Gbanyụọ na ndabara: ahụ nke mbụ kwụsịrị na-ada arịrịọ na-enweghị nnwale ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_RESELECT`                          | `false`                                                           | OpenCode executor naanị: mgbe 429 si n'aka onye na-eweta egress-bucketed na akaụntụ proxy-enweghị n'okpuru ọnọdụ ọdọ mmiri ambient, jụọ njikọ ọdọ mmiri maka onye ọzọ maka mbọ ọzọ kama ịnwale otu adreesị egress ahụ. Iwu, anaghị ewepu: ọdọ mmiri ike gwụrụ na-edobe omume dị ugbu a. Gbanyụọ na ndabara: 429 ọ bụla na-atụgharị gaa na akaụntụ ọzọ dịka ọ dị na mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_STRAIN_MARKER_PATH`                | _(Edobeghi)_                                                      | OpenCode executor naanị: mebie ụzọ nke ọdọ mmiri-strain markker na-agụ tupu adọba ụgbọala (`{since, reason, ttl_s}`, ndabara `/tmp/opencode-pool-strain.json`, #13924). Ogige ihe nrịbama ọhụrụ na-agụghị; anọghị ya ma ọ bụ stale daa azụ na counter gbawara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS`                     | `30000`                                                           | Oge nkwụsị proxy hop maka arịrịọ àkwà mmiri `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `FIRECRAWL_BASE_URL`                              | `https://api.firecrawl.dev`                                       | Tụtụ aka na onye nrụpụta web-fetch Firecrawl na ihe atụ nke onwe (API key optional off-cloud).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `FIRECRAWL_TIMEOUT_MS`                            | `30000`                                                           | Oge ngwụcha nke arịrịọ ọ bụla maka onye nrụpụta webụsaịtị Firecrawl.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `TAVILY_BASE_URL`                                 | _(Edobeghi)_                                                      | Base URL maka Tavily `/usage` quota fetcher (oke ndị na-eweta); leghaara anya mgbe ọ na-atụ aka na `api.tavily.com`. Ịtọghaghị na-adaghachi na ntọala njikọ URL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                                                          | N'ozuzu ihe nkesa na-arịọ arịrịọ oge maka akwa mmiri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `60000`                                                           | Oge izipu nkụnye eji isi mee nzaghachi site na akwa mmiri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `5000`                                                            | Bridge na-adị ndụ oge agwụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `0`                                                               | Ọgbụgba oghere oghere (nkwarụ 0 = ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS`                             | `30000`                                                           | Oge amara na SIGTERM/SIGINT tupu ọpụpụ ike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
