| `OMNIROUTE_DEFAULT_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`              | `120000`                                                          | Ndaghachi nke `src/shared/utils/fetchTimeout.ts` na-eji mgbe emebere `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `8000`                                                            | Oge nkwụsị (ms) maka ntọala `validationRead` na `modelsProbe` na `src/shared/network/safeOutboundFetch.ts`. Welie maka akara ngwụcha nwayọ (Cerebras, Cloudflare AI, Groq) iji gbochie ịkụnye n'etiti active/error na dashboard. Daghachi na 8000ms maka ezighi ezi (< 1000) ma ọ bụ ụkpụrụ na-abụghị ọnụọgụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                | `25000`                                                           | Nweta oge a kapịrị ọnụ na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). Ntugharị agbagharị agbagharị ga-adarịrị tupu oge client/agent (~ 30s) yabụ ndị na-akpọ oku na-ahụ ọdịda kpọmkwem ọsọ ọsọ kama ịnọpụ n'ọkwa n'elu. Capped na `29000` ka ọ na-ebute ụzọ mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS`                      | `10`                                                              | Nnwale azụ azụ nkekọrịtara maka ụzọ direct/relay/proxy nwaa-otu ugboro na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). `0` = nwaa ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `60000`                                                           | Oge ngwụcha nke arịrịọ nwa afọ wreq-js (`claudeTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Amara oge ngwụcha JS zuru oke agbakwunyere n'elu oge ngwụcha nke obodo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `30000`                                                           | Oge ngwụcha nke arịrịọ nwa afọ wreq-js (`perplexityTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_GRACE_MS`                     | `10000`                                                           | Amara oge ngwụcha JS zuru oke agbakwunyere n'elu oge ngwụcha nke obodo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_SEARCH_HINT`                      | `0` (apụ)                                                         | agbakwunyere "I nwela ọchụchọ webụ arụnyere. Zaa ajụjụ ozugbo site na iji nsonaazụ ọchụchọ." gaa na ozi sistemu onye na-akpọ oku (`perplexity-web/protocol.ts`). Gbanyụọ na ndabara — Pplexity na-achọ agbanyeghị, ahịrịokwu ahụ wee banye na nzaghachi dị ka meta-nkọwa maka ndị ahịa koodu. Tọọ `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji weghachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `60000`                                                           | Oge ngwụcha nke arịrịọ nwa afọ wreq-js (`grokTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_GRACE_MS`                     | `10000`                                                           | Amara oge ngwụcha JS zuru oke agbakwunyere n'elu oge ngwụcha nke obodo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `30000`                                                           | Nwa afọ wreq-js arịrịọ oge nkwụsị (`notionTlsClient.ts`); `notion-web` na-ebuli ya kwa arịrịọ `180000` ruo ogologo ọgbọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Amara oge ngwụcha JS zuru oke agbakwunyere n'elu oge ngwụcha nke obodo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_BROWSER_POOL`                          | `on`                                                              | Ọdọ mmiri ihe nchọgharị Playwright ekekọrịtara maka nkata kuki webụ na-akwado ihe nchọgharị (`browserPool.ts`); tọọ `off` iji gbanyụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OBSCURA_BIN`                                     | `auto-detect`                                                     | Ụzọ na ọnụọgụ abụọ `obscura` ejiri dị ka injin mbụ site na ọdọ mmiri ihe nchọgharị na Cloudflare Playground executor (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`); achọpụtara na akpaghị aka site na PATH sistemu mgbe edobere ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OBSCURA_CDP_ENDPOINT`                            | _(Edobeghi)_                                                      | Tụtụ aka na Obscura (`http://host:port`) na-agba ọsọ kama ịmụba otu; modul enweghị usoro ahụ (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OBSCURA_PORT`                                    | `random free port`                                                | ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri doro anya maka `obscura serve` spawn; a na-ahọrọ ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri efu na-akpaghị aka mgbe ịtọghe ya (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `WEB_COOKIE_USE_BROWSER`                          | `0`                                                               | Họrọ arịrịọ nkata kuki webụ banye n'ụzọ nke ihe nchọgharị na-akwado (`browserBackedChat.ts`); `1` ka enyere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL`                               | `https://www.kimi.ai`                                             | Base URL maka webụ Kimi (international kimi.ai Connect-RPC) executor (`kimi-web.ts`); mebie naanị maka njedebe mirror/proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `KIMI_WEB_CHAT_URL`                               | `<KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL>/apiv2/kimi.gateway.chat.v1.ChatService/Chat` | Ebe ngwụcha nkata zuru oke maka onye ọrụ webụ Kimi (`kimi-web.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                    | _(Achọpụtara ya na akpaghị aka)_                                  | Ụzọ na usoro Chrome/Edge nke enwere ike ime maka ntinye ihe nchọgharị Adobe Firefly mmekọrịta (`adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`); na-ewepụ nchọta akpaaka kwa-OS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                        | _.build/ nke kwụụrụ onwe ya pụta_                                 | Mwepụta oge wuo maka akwụkwọ ndekọ aha mmepụta nke kwụụrụ onwe ya site na nzọụkwụ nrụpụta mgbe emechara (`scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`); wuo ngwá ọrụ, ọ bụghị oge ojiri gaa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

Mgbalị ebumnuche combo na-eketa oge nkwụsị nke arịrịọ gbagoro agbago (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`, ma ọ bụ `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` mgbe ọ na-enye ndabara ndabara). Tọọ `targetTimeoutMs` na ngwakọta, ndabara ndabara, ma ọ bụ onye na-eweta gabiga naanị iji mee ka ngwakọta daa ngwa ngwa; ụkpụrụ ndị dị n'elu oge mgbago elu ugbu a ka etinyere na oge nkwụsị elu.

`comboTimeoutMs` bụ mmefu ego elekere mgbidi dị iche iche gafere ebumnuche ọdịda. Hapụ ya na ịtọgheghị ma ọ bụ `0` ka idowe ntugharị na-akparaghị ókè (nkwụsị nkwụsi ike nkeji iri na `comboPredicates.ts` ka na-emetụta). Uru dị mma na-anọchi netwọk nchekwa ahụ maka combo. Debe `comboTimeoutMs` ogologo oge karịa `targetTimeoutMs` ya mere ọdịda ka na-enwekwa oge ka emechara ebumnuche mbụ nwayọ.

### Ebe mgbaji sekit

Ntụzigharị ihe mgbaji sekit nke ọkwa onye. Ndị ndabara na-egosipụta ụkpụrụ a gbanyere mkpọrọgwụ ejiri kemgbe v3.6 maka njikọ 500 +.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                                            | Ọdabara   | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------- | ---------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_THRESHOLD`                   | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ọgba ọdịda ọdịda sochiri anya maka ndị na-eweta OAuth tupu onye nbibi ahụ agawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_RESET_MS`                    | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Tọgharịa windo (ms) maka onye na-eweta OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_THRESHOLD`                 | `12`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ọnụ mbata ọdịda sochiri anya maka ndị na-eweta igodo API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_RESET_MS`                  | `30000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Tọgharia mpio (ms) maka onye na-eweta igodo API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_THRESHOLD`                   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ọnụ ụzọ ọdịda ọdịda nke nsoro maka ndị na-eweta mpaghara (Ollama, LM Studio, ...).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_RESET_MS`                    | `15000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Tọgharia mpio (ms) maka onye na-emebi ihe na-eweta mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `10`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ọkwa ndị na-eweta: ọdịda dị n'ime mpio tupu ndị na-eweta OAuth niile abanye n'ime oyi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `900000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ọkwa ndị na-eweta: windo ọdịda-mkpọtụ (ms) maka ndị na-eweta OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ọkwa ndị na-eweta: cooldown (ms) ozugbo agafere ụzọ ndị na-eweta OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`      | `5`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | onye na-eweta OAuth na-abanye DEGRADED na ọtụtụ ọdịda a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`     | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | onye na-eweta OAuth max resetTimeout escalation multiplier.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT`   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | onye na-eweta OAuth na-abawanye mgbe nke a gachara ọtụtụ okirikiri mepere emepe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`        | `15`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ọkwa onye na-eweta: ọdịda dị n'ime mpio tupu onye na-eweta igodo API niile abanye n'ime oyi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`        | `1800000` | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ọkwa ndị na-eweta ihe: windo ọdịda ọdịda na-atụgharị (ms) maka ndị na-eweta igodo API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`              | `600000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Njikwa ọkwa onye na-eweta: cooldown (ms) ozugbo agbaru igodo igodo API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`    | `7`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Onye na-eweta igodo API na-abanye DEGRADED n'ọtụtụ ọdịda a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`   | `4`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Onye na-eweta igodo API max resetTimeout abawanye ụba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT` | `3`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Onye na-eweta igodo API na-abawanye ka ọtụtụ okirikiri mepere emepe gachara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ọkwa onye na-eweta: ọdịda tupu onye na-eweta mpaghara niile abanye n'ime oyi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ọkwa ndị na-eweta: Window ọdịda ọdịda (ms) maka ndị na-eweta mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ọkwa ndị na-eweta: cooldown (ms) ozugbo agafere oke ndị na-eweta mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`                               | `60000`   | `open-sse/services/apiKeyRotator.ts`                 | Ntugharị igodo ọ bụla (ms) etinyere site na ntụgharị igodo ọtụtụ igodo API mgbe 429 elu na-ebu enweghị `Retry-After` (#14573). Ụkpụrụ na-abụghị nke ziri ezi ma ọ bụ na-abụghị ọnụọgụ na-adaghachi na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_AVALANCHE_PROTECTION_ENABLED`                      | `false`   | `open-sse/services/accountFallback/antiAvalanche.ts` | Wepụ ihe nche mgbochi oke mmiri: naanị `true` doro anya na-enyere ya aka. Mgbe ọ na-aga, 429 nke na-akpọ akara akara nkeji kwa nkeji (yana oke njupụta 400) na-akwụsị ọdịda ọdịda n'ofe igodo ndị ọzọ fọdụrụnụ. Oke ike gwụrụ 429 na-aga n'ihu na-ada n'agbanyeghị nnukwu ngwa ngwa, na HTTP 413 emetụtaghị (ọ na-edobe #13350 retry-na-ọzọ-akparamagwa akaụntụ). |
| `OMNIROUTE_AVALANCHE_BURST_COOLDOWN_MS`                       | `60000`   | `open-sse/services/accountFallback/antiAvalanche.ts` | Model-mkpọchi cooldown (ms) etinyere mgbe onye nche na-agba ọkụ, kama mkpọchi na-arị elu. Ntọgharị elu dị ogologo na-emeri mgbe niile. Ụkpụrụ na-abụghị nke ziri ezi ma ọ bụ na-abụghị ọnụọgụ na-adaghachi na ndabara.                                                                                                                                            |
| `PIN_DROP_BACKOFF_LEVEL`                                      | `2`       | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                         | Omimi azụ azụ ebe a na-ahụta onye na-eweta pin cache adịghị mma ma tụba pin ahụ maka ọdịda.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PIN_DROP_GRACE_MS`                                           | `20000`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                         | Window mgbochi-flap (ms) na-anabata obere oyi na-adịru nwa oge tupu idowe ntụtụ-nchekwa gburugburu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### Ihe atụ

| Ihe atụ                             | Nhazi                                                   |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Ọgbọ koodu na-adị ogologo oge**   | `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=900000` (15 min)                    |
| **Na-ekekọta mkpokọta iyi oge ndụ** | `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS=1260000` (21 min)             |
| **Ọsọ-ọdịda maka mmepụta API**      | `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=10000`                     |
| **Ụdị echiche agbatịkwuru**         | `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS=300000` (nkeji 5 n'etiti nkeji) |

## 16. Ịbanye

Sistemụ osisi na-edegara ma faịlụ ndekọ stdout na agbagharị agbagharị. A na-agụ nhazi niile site na `src/lib/logEnv.ts`.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                        | Ọdabara                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `APP_LOG_LEVEL`                           | `info`                     | Ọkwa ndekọ kacha nta: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `APP_LOG_FORMAT`                          | `text`                     | Ọkpụkpọ mmepụta: `text` (mmadụ nwere ike ịgụ) ma ọ bụ `json` (ahaziri).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `APP_LOG_TO_FILE`                         | `true`                     | Dee ndekọ na faịlụ n'akụkụ stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `APP_LOG_FILE_PATH`                       | `logs/application/app.log` | Log faịlụ ụzọ (ihe metụtara mgbọrọgwụ ọrụ ma ọ bụ `DATA_DIR`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILE_SIZE`                   | `50M`                      | Oke faịlụ buru ibu tupu ntụgharị. Nabata: `50M`, `1G`, `512K`, ma ọ bụ bytes larịị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                  | `7`                        | Ụbọchị iji dowe faịlụ ndekọ ngwa atụgharị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILES`                       | `20`                       | Ndabere faịlụ ndekọ kacha atụgharị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                 | `7`                        | Ụbọchị idobe request/call log ndenye na nchekwa data.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CALL_LOG_MAX_ENTRIES`                    | `10000`                    | Ndenye ndekọ oku kacha na nchekwa ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CALL_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`                | `100000`                   | Ahịrị kacha dị na tebụl `call_logs` SQLite tupu kwachaa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `MEDIA_STORE_MAX_MB`                      | `2048`                     | Oke okpu na MiB maka mgbasa ozi emepụtara ka edobere n'okpuru `DATA_DIR/media`. Okpu gara aga na-ebu ụzọ wepụ faịlụ ndị kacha ochie; faịlụ dị nnọọ e dere na-anọ mgbe niile. Ọnụ ọgụgụ zuru ezu n'elu 0; A na-eleghara uru ọ bụla ọzọ anya. Na-emeri ntọala dashboard Ntọala > Nchekwa > Okpu nha ụlọ ahịa mgbasa ozi. Isi mmalite: `src/lib/media/mediaStore.ts`. |
| `ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS`                     | _(Edobeghi)_               | Manye ịbanye ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ arịrịọ zuru ezu, na-agafe ntọala dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `MAX_PENDING_REQUEST_AGE_MS`              | `3600000` (1 hour)         | Ndenye ndekọ arịrịọ na-arụsi ọrụ ike nwa mgbei tupu ihicha ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MAX_PENDING_DETAILS`                     | `5000`                     | Nkọwa kachasị na-echere (integer ziri ezi); ọnụ okpu: zachaa; byte/map okpu: tinye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_CAPTURE_STREAM_CHUNKS` | `false`                    | Chekwaa iberibe iyi na arịa ọkpọkọ mgbe `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`. Wepụ (`true`) — na ndabara iji chekwaa diski.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_MAX_SIZE_KB`           | `512`                      | Ogo ndekọ ndekọ ọkpọ ọkpọ kacha na KB mgbe `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CALL_LOG_MIN_FREE_DISK_MB`               | `512`                      | Oghere diski efu kacha nta na MB na olu na-ejide `DATA_DIR/call_logs`. N'okpuru ya, oku log artifact na-ede na-awụlikwa elu na ahịrị na-egosi `detail_state` `missing`. `0` gbanyụọ nlele ahụ. Ntụle ego dara ada anaghị ada akwụkwọ.                                                                                                                              |
| `PROXY_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`               | `100000`                   | Ahịrị kacha dị na tebụl `proxy_logs` SQLite tupu kwachaa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                   | `false`                    | Tinye client/egress IPs na prefixes akaụntụ na ndekọ njikwa `[ProxyEgress]`. Ndekọ proxy-log dashboard/database na-ejigide nkọwa zuru ezu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_LOG_FIRST_CHUNK_TIMING`            | `false`                    | Tọọ na `"true"` ma ọ bụ `"1"` ka ịdekọ `first_chunk_ms` (zipụ mmalite na byte ahụ bara uru nke mbụ) na ahịrị proxy-log. Kechie ọ bụla upstream ahu na a umengwụ passthrough iyi na patches ahịrị ozugbo byte rutere; gbanyụọ na ndabara ya mere nzaghachi na-agafe na-emetụghị aka.                                                                                |
| `APP_LOG_ROTATION_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS`      | `60000` (1 min)            | Ugboro ole `src/lib/logRotation.ts` na-enyochagharị nha faịlụ ndekọ na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CHAT_LOG_TEXT_LIMIT`                     | `65536`                    | Ogologo eriri ogologo ka edobere n'ime ihe ndekọ ndekọ nkata (ndabara 64 KB).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CHAT_LOG_ARRAY_TAIL_ITEMS`               | `128`                      | Ọnụọgụ nke ihe n'usoro echekwabara n'ọdụ mgbe a na-ewepụ ibu ndekọ ndekọ nkata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_DEPTH`                      | `6`                        | Omimi kacha elu tupu akwụchapụ ụgwọ ndekọ ndekọ nkata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_OBJECT_KEYS`                | `80`                       | Igodo ihe kacha echekwabara na nrụnye ndekọ nkata (0 = na-akparaghị ókè).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_BODY_KB`                    | `1024`                     | Nha ahụ zuru oke request/response (KB) tupu e jiri nchịkọta efu dochie ya kama ịbụ clone zuru ezu. Welie nke a ma ọ bụrụ na mkparịta ụka ogologo oge na-egosi onye na-edobe ọnọdụ kama ịbụ ezigbo ozi na dashboard.                                                                                                                                                |
| `CHAT_DEBUG_FILE`                         | `false`                    | Mgbe eziokwu, `serializeArtifactForStorage` na-amali mkpọtu dabere na nha. Naanị ihichapụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
