## 17. Nchekwa nchekwa

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                           | Ọdabara                                                                                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEAP_DIAGNOSTICS_ENABLED`         | `0` (apụ)                                                                                  | Tọọ `1` ka ịdebanye metric nrụgide V8 site na `src/lib/healthzLag.ts`. Nha ndekọ na ọnụọgụ ụgbọ elu, arịọla ọdịnaya, traktị ma ọ bụ mkpọ foto. Na-agba ọsọ n'ime ihe ndekọ-loop stall recorder, yabụ `OMNIROUTE_EVENT_LOOP_STALL_THRESHOLD_MS=0` na-agbanyụkwa ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEAP_DIAGNOSTICS_THRESHOLD_MB`    | `900`                                                                                      | Ọnụ ụzọ ziri ezi maka heapUsed (MiB) nke na-akpalite ndekọ nbanye nyocha. Ụkpụrụ ezighi ezi na-eji 900 MiB; A na-ejedebe ozi dị elu otu ugboro n'ime sekọnd 60.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`                        | _auto_ (ọla efu);**`1024` na foto Docker**                                                 | **Akwadoro** Docker/standalone V8 oke oke (MB). Mgbe edobereghị ya, a na-ahazi ya n'ike (~ 35% nke sistemu RAM, agbakwunyere na `[512, 4096]`); `512` bụ naanị ala mgbe enweghị ike ịgụta ebe nchekwa mkpokọta. Na `run-standalone.mjs` (Docker CMD), an** doro anya** agbakwunyere uru dị ka `--max-old-space-size` na** merie** n'elu ọkọlọtọ obo NODE_OPTIONS na-emegiderịta (ọkọlọtọ ikpeazụ V8). `omniroute serve` ka na-ahọrọ ikpo NODE_OPTIONS dị adị (#5238). Edokwala ma nọmba dị iche iche — usoro ndekọ a ịdọ aka ná ntị na-aha ma ụkpụrụ na onye mmeri.** Onyonyo Docker gọọmentị na-edobe `1024` mgbe niile, yabụ na nhazigharị anaghị aga ebe ahụ.** Onye nnọchite koodu `/v1/responses` chọrọ `8192`–`12288` gbakwunyere cgroup headroom — hụ [Docker Nduzi — oge ojiri gaa RAM](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#runtime-ram-for-coding-agents). |
| `OMNIROUTE_RSS_PRESSURE_MB`                  | _auto_ (`max(3072, 2 × OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB)`; gbanyụọ mgbe emebere `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB` ) | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Usoro zuru oke-RSS fuse (MB) maka ebe nchekwa native/external nke V8 `heapUsed` enweghị ike ịhụ; n'elu ya arịrịọ ọhụrụ nweta 503 `resource_pressure`. RSS adịkarịghị ada ma ọlị, yabụ jikọta ya na `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`. |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`                      | `50`                                                                                       | Ndenye sistemu echekwara kacha elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`                     | `2097152` (2 MB)                                                                           | Nha nnukwu cache ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_TTL_MS`                        | `300000` (minti 5)                                                                         | Ngwa cache ntinye TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`                    | `100`                                                                                      | Okpomọkụ kacha echekwara = 0 nzaghachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`                   | `4194304` (4 MB)                                                                           | Ngụ cache kacha elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_ENTRY_BYTES`             | `262144` (256 KB)                                                                          | Ogo kacha nke otu ntinye na cache semantic ebe nchekwa. A na-echekwa azịza buru ibu na SQLite naanị na anaghị achụpụ ndenye ebe nchekwa ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_TTL_MS`                      | `1800000` (30 min)                                                                         | Ntinye cache semantic TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_PRUNE_INTERVAL_MS` | `1800000` (30 min)                                                                         | nkeji SQLite kwachaa. `0` gbanyụọ mmalite na ọsọ oge; ụkpụrụ ndị ọzọ dị n'okpuru `60000` daa azụ na ndabara. Uru ndị dị n'elu `2147483647` ms jigidere ngụ oge Node kachasị, na-ezere njupụta na ntuli aka 1ms. Mbụ na-agba ọsọ mgbe 1s; ndị na-agụ oge anaghị emegharị ma na-agba ọsọ anaghị agafe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_PRUNE_BATCH_SIZE`  | `2000`                                                                                     | Ahịrị kwa SQLite prune DELETE, a kpọgidere ya na `[100, 50000]`; na-enye aka aka aka omume n'etiti batches.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_PRUNE_MAX_BATCHES` | `50`                                                                                       | Ekekọrịtara mmefu ego ogbe DELETE kwa ọsọ gafere ngafe okpu nke nhọrọ, agbakwunyere na `[1, 10000]`. Ndekọ azụ na-aga n'ihu na ọsọ ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_ROWS`          | `0`                                                                                        | Okpu ahiri SQLite nhọrọ (`0` = ọ dịghị). Mgbe ihichapụ `expires_at <=` ahụ run-start ISO timestamp, chụpụ `created_at` kacha ochie, wee `rowid`, gụnyere ahịrị ndị dị ndụ, dabere na mmefu ego nkekọrịta. A na-agbaji ọnụ ọgụgụ ka ọ bụrụ ọnụọgụ (ruo oke ọnụọgụ nchekwa nchekwa). Ihe efu, nke na-abụghị ọnụọgụ, na-enweghị njedebe ma ọ bụ nke na-adịghị mma maka mgbanwe anọ nke prune na-adaghachi na ndabara ha; irighiri ihe gbadaa ala. Ikwachaa anaghị agbanwe TTL, ebe nchekwa LRU ma ọ bụ vector-cache omume.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE`                | `5`                                                                                        | Mgbọrọgwụ nke mpaghara-corpus nke na-edobe ndeksi ebe nchekwa n'otu oge (`src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`). LRU: na njedebe a na-achụpụ ma wughachi ya na ajụjụ na-esote ya. Ejidebere na opekempe `1`; uru na-abụghị ọnụọgụ na-adaghachi na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `STREAM_HISTORY_MAX`                         | `50`                                                                                       | Ihe omume iyi kacha nso nso a na nchekwa ihe nlele ndụ Dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT`                     | _(Edobeghi)_                                                                               | Ihe ngbanwe gburugburu ụwa-ogologo gabigara (ihe ngosi) maka ndị na-eweta ọ bụla na-enweghị mgbanwe `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`. Mgbe atọrọ ya** dochie** katalọgụ, dashboard gabigara na ụkpụrụ ndekọ aha maka ndị na-eweta ya; mgbe a tọghị ya, OmniRoute na-eji yinye arụnyere arụnyere ya, ebe ikpeazụ ya bụ `128000`. Hụ [ogologo okirikiri nke onye na-eweta ya](#per-provider-context-length-context_length_provider).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `USAGE_TOKEN_BUFFER`                         | `100`                                                                                      | Edobere isi ụlọ ihe ngosi ọzọ mgbe ị na-enyocha oke ojiji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

### Ogologo okirikiri nke onye na-eweta ya (`CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`)

`open-sse/services/contextManager.ts` (`getEnvOverride()` / `resolveTokenLimit()`) na-agụkwa a** onye na-eweta ya** ụdị nke ewuru aha ya site n'aka onye na-eweta ID n'oge ojiri gaa, yabụ enweghị ike depụta ya n'usoro n'usoro na tebụl dị n'elu ma ọ bụ na `.env.example`.

**Aha.** `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`, ebe `<PROVIDER>` bụ onye na-eweta ID elu-cased, na agwa ọ bụla na-abụghị `A-Z` / `0-9` dochie `_`. Onye na-eweta ID bụ nke a na-ahụ anya na mkpebi utu aha (akụkụ `provider` nke eriri `provider/model` ozugbo edozichara utu aha dị mkpirikpi). Omenala OpenAI- ma ọ bụ Anthropic-dakọtara ọnụ na-eji ọnụ ha emepụtara ID, yabụ UUID bụ akụkụ nke aha agbanwe agbanwe. Uru bụ ọnụọgụ ọnụọgụ dị mma nke token; ihe ọ bụla ọzọ (efu, `0`, adịghị mma, na-abụghị ọnụọgụ) na-eleghara anya na mkpebi na-aga n'ihu na nzọụkwụ ọzọ.

| Onye na-enye ID                                             | Aha mgbanwe                                                                |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| agent router                                                | CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER                                                 |
| oghere oghere                                               | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENROUTER                                                  |
| openai-compatible-chat-0f8e2c1a-5b7d-4e3f-9a6c-2d1b8e4f7a90 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE_CHAT_0F8E2C1A_5B7D_4E3F_9A6C_2D1B8E4F7A90 |

**Ibu ụzọ.** Maka otu ụzọ `provider/model`, nzọụkwụ mbụ na-eweta uru na-emeri:

1. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>` maka onye na-eweta ya.
2. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT` (njedebe gburugburu ụwa).
3. Window ihe nlere ọ bụla: ihe na-adịgide adịgide gabigara (nke edobere na dashboard, ma ọ bụ tinye ya na akpaghị aka site na nchọpụta nchọpụta, lee `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL`), ma ọ bụghị ya, katalọgụ / ụdị.dev synced synced.
4. Ndebanye aha onye na-eweta `defaultContextLength`.
5. Ụdị aha heuristics (`claude` 200000, `gemini` 1000000, `gpt` / `o1` / `o3` / `o4` / `codex` 400000).
6. Ndabara nke onye na-eweta ihe arụnyere, wee `128000`.

Usoro abụọ a metụtara gburugburu ebe obibi** onye na-eweta ya niile** : ụdị ọ bụla nke onye na-eweta ahụ na-enweta otu oke ahụ, ha na-emeri kwa dashboard nke ụdị ọ bụla. Mgbe naanị otu ụdị nke onye na-eweta chọrọ windo dị iche, jiri ihe nfefe nke otu ụdị na dashboard kama.

**Oke.** Agbụ a na-eme ka nlele oge arịrịọ-ohere ọnọdụ (njụjụ `context_length_exceeded` / "Ntinye karịrị windo ọnọdụ" jụrụ na `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`), mmefu ego mkpakọ ngwa ngwa yana oke mkpokọta mkpokọta. `context_length` nke `GET /v1/models` kpọsara ka nwere ike si na katalọgụ ma ọ bụ metadata ndekọ ma ekwekwaghị n'ezie na ọ ga-egosipụta na ọ ga-emebi gburugburu.

**Ọmụmaatụ.** Onye na-eweta ihe nlereanya ya na-anabata windo token 1,050,000 ebe katalọgụ na-akọ 128,000:

```dotenv
CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER=1050000
```

Malitegharịa ihe nkesa ahụ mgbe ị gbanwee ya: a na-agụ uru ahụ site na gburugburu usoro ihe nkesa. Na** ngwa desktọọpụ** ihe nkesa na-agba ọsọ dị ka usoro nwatakịrị ma ọ naghị ahụ usoro agbanwe agbanwe agbanwe mgbe emechara ngwa ahụ; tinye ahịrị n'ime faịlụ `.env` ngwa ngwa (usoro nyocha na [Electron Guide: Environment file searchup](../guides/ELECTRON_GUIDE.md#environment-file-lookup) ), wee kwụsị kpamkpam wee malitegharịa ngwa ahụ.

### Mkpakọ

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                    | Ọdabara | Nkọwa                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS` | ịtọhapụ | Tụkwasị oru ngo `.rtk/filters.json` na-enweghị a `.rtk/trust.json` hash. Jiri naanị na mmepe mpaghara a na-achịkwa. |

### Igwe ebe nchekwa (atụmatụ 21)

### Ọnụ mmemme-loop nke ebe nchekwa, nka, na ume ọhụrụ token (#10349)

OmniRoute bu a** otu Node usoro** . Ebe nchekwa extraction/retrieval, nkà ịgba ntụtụ, na onye na-eweta ihe ngosi na-agba ọsọ na nke ahụ** otu ihe omume akaghị** dị ka `GET /healthz` na dashboard. Ha abụghị eriri onye ọrụ.

| Ọrụ                           | Koodu                              | Ọdabara                                      | Njikwa onye ọrụ                                                                                                       |
| ----------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Mwepụta/iweghachi ebe nchekwa | `src/lib/memory/`                  | Dashboard**memoryEnabled** (ndabere na)      | Gbanyụọ** Ntọala → Ebe nchekwa** . Enweghị mgba ọkụ env kill dị iche karịa ịkwụsị njirimara na ntọala.                |
| Ngwunye nka                   | `src/lib/skills/injection.ts`      | Dashboard**skillsEnabled** (ndabere na)      | Gbanyụọ** Ntọala → Memory/Skills** (`skillsEnabled`). Mkpọtụ igbe igbe dị n'okpuru naanị agbụ agbụ mgbe agbanyere ya. |
| Token nweta ume               | `src/sse/services/tokenRefresh.ts` | Gbanyere maka ndị na-eweta OAuth/web ejikọrọ | Kwụpụ onye na-eweta ya ma ọ bụ hapụ akara ka ọ dị irè; o nweghi `TOKEN_REFRESH=0` env today.                          |

Ọ bụrụ na `/healthz` adịghị nwayọ na igbe dị jụụ, gbanyụọ ikike + ebe nchekwa na mbụ, wee lelee ibu catalog/compression (#10303, #9685). Atụmatụ ndị a na-ewepụta na `await` point mana ka na-asọ mpi maka otu eri.

Na-etinye akwa oyi akwa, ụlọ ahịa vector na mkpisigharị maka sistemụ nchekwa na-adịgide adịgide (`src/lib/memory/`).

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                   | Ọdabara                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_TTL_MS`      | `300000` (minti 5)          | TTL maka cache ntinye ebe nchekwa (kwa mbinye source/model/dim ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_MAX`         | `1000`                      | Edebere ndenye kacha LRU n'ime oghere ntinye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MEMORY_TRANSFORMERS_MODEL`          | `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`   | HF repo id maka nbanye `@huggingface/transformers` mpaghara MiniLM pipeline (~ 23 MB int8, ~ 400 MB RAM).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_MODEL`                | `minishlab/potion-base-8M`  | HF repo id maka tebụl nchọta potion/Model2Vec static. Ebudata ya dị umengwụ n'ime cache dir.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_CACHE_DIR`            | `<DATA_DIR>/embeddings`     | A na-eji akwụkwọ ndekọ aha na-echekwa faịlụ ụdị potion static. Ihe ndabara n'okpuru `DATA_DIR` mgbe edobere ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `HF_HUB_ENDPOINT`                    | `https://huggingface.co`    | Wepu mmakọ Ihu Hub base URL nke `staticPotion.ts` na-eji (dịka ọmụmaatụ njedebe enyo maka ntọlite ikuku nwere oghere).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `MEMORY_VEC_TOP_K`                   | `20`                        | Top-K nke nchọta `sqlite-vec` brute-force vector na-eji n'ime `src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MEMORY_RRF_K`                       | `60`                        | Ntugharị ọkwa Fusion mgbe niile `k` maka ngwakọ FTS5 + vector eweghachite (ntụziaka sqlite-vec).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `VECTOR_STORE_DISABLE_VEC`           | `false`                     | Test/diagnostic seam na `getVectorStore()` (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`): mgbe `true`, na-amanye ụlọ ahịa vector ka ọ `null` (na-eme ka gburugburu cloud/WASM na-enweghị `sqlite-vec`), iweghachite ebe nchekwa na-eweda ya na nchọ okwu FTS5. Hapụ enweghị ntọala na mmepụta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `NOTION_API_KEY`                     | _(Edobeghi)_                | igodo API maka azụ azụ Notion (nke `genericBackend.ts` mara azụ azụ azụ na-eji).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NOTION_API_URL`                     | `https://api.notion.com/v1` | Ndabere URL maka Notion API (nwere ike ịkagbu maka ndị ọzọ Notion kwadoro onwe ya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_KEY`                   | _(Edobeghi)_                | igodo API maka Obsidian Vault backend (nke `genericBackend.ts` mara azụ azụ azụ na-eji).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_URL`                   | `http://localhost:27123`    | Base URL maka Obsidian Vault API (nwere ike ịkwụsị maka oghere dịpụrụ adịpụ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED`         | `false`                     | TV6 pịnyere ebe nchekwa decay master mgba ọkụ.** Nwepu (na-apụ apụ)** — mkpochapụ** na-ehichapụ** ncheta rere ure. Gbanyụọ ya, `access_count`/`last_accessed_at` bụ telemetry dị ọcha na ọ nweghị ihe ehichapụrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_EPISODIC_DAYS`   | `30`                        | TTL (ụbọchị) mgbe nke ahụ gasịrị, ebe nchekwa `episodic` na-ejighị ya na-emebi. `0` na-emekwa ka mgbochi nke episodic kwa. Ụdị na-adịgide adịgide (`factual`/`procedural`/`semantic`) na-adịghị egbochi mgbe niile. Elekere ire ere na-adaberekwa na `last_accessed_at`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ACCESS_IMMUNITY` | `3`                         | Ebe nchekwa gbara `>=` nke a ọtụtụ oge na-agaghị emebi emebi n'agbanyeghị ụdị. `0` na-ewepụ mgbochi ohere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_SWEEP_INTERVAL`  | `0` (nkwarụ)                | Ogologo oge (sekọnd) maka ịsachapụ ire ere oge oge na `src/lib/memory/typedDecay.ts`. `0`/unset = enweghị mkpochapụ oge. Nbanye ugboro abụọ: chọkwara `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRICT_SYSTEM_PROVIDERS`  | _(Edobeghi)_                | Id ndị na-eweta rịkọm nke kewapụrụ (ọnọdụ enweghị mmetụta) na-anabata ozi `system`** naanị na index 0** (`src/lib/memory/injection.ts`). Maka ndị a, oghere nchekwa nchekwa etiti dị n'etiti adịghị mma na mkparịta ụka ọtụtụ ntụgharị, yabụ ebe nchekwa bụ merged/prepended dị ka ozi sistemụ na-eduga kama. Ihe ndabara na naanị `xiaomi-mimo`/`mimo`; gbatịkwuo maka oghere njedebe dakọtara na OpenAI nke kwadoro onwe ya (dịka Qwen3.5 +/3.6) nke ndebiri nkata ya na-akwado otu mmachi ozi-usoro na-eduzi otu. |

### Obere-RAM Docker Ihe atụ

`128` bụ naanị dashboard. Ndị na-etinye koodu na `FATAL ERROR` a n'oge ogologo `/v1/responses`. Ejila ihe atụ a dịka ọnụ ụzọ Claude/Codex/Grok.

```bash
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=128
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=20
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=524288        # 512 KB
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=25
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=1048576     # 1 MB
STREAM_HISTORY_MAX=10
```

## Mmekọrịta Arena ELO

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe           | Ọdabara       | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                                                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_ENABLED`     | `true`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                     | Oge Arena AI leaderboard ELO mmekọrịta, ahazi ya site na ọkọlọtọ Njirimara Dashboard ma ọ bụ jiri `false` pụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `MODELS_CATALOG_PREFIX_MODE` | `dual`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/models/catalog.ts` | Ụdị prefix ejiri maka ids nlereanya na `GET /v1/models`. `dual` na-akpọsa ma mkpirisi utu aha dị mkpụmkpụ yana prefix nke ndị na-eweta canonical maka ụdị ọ bụla (ndakọrịta azụ — na-eme ka katalọgụ ahụ okpukpu abụọ); `alias` na-ewepụta otu id kwa ụdị; `canonical` na-ewepụta naanị prefix onye na-eweta-id (ndị na-eweta aha ha bụbu id nke canonical na-edobe otu ntinye ha). Ndị ahịa nwere ike ịkagbu otu arịrịọ site na `?prefix=alias`. Lee [API_REFERENCE](API_REFERENCE.md#model-id-prefixes-prefix). |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_INTERVAL`    | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/arenaEloSync.ts`                                                            | Oge mmekọrịta n'ime sekọnd.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

| `PROMPTQL_GRAPHQL_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.prompt.ql.app/promptql/playground-v2-hge/v1/graphql` | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | ebe njedebe GraphQL ejiri maka ọrụ chat/session.            |
| `PROMPTQL_CREDITS_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.pro.ql.app/v1/graphql`                               | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`, `open-sse/services/usage/promptql.ts` | ebe ngwụcha GraphQL na-ajụ kredit balance/usage.            |
| `PROMPTQL_TOKEN_REFRESH_URL` | `https://auth.pro.ql.app/ddn/project/token`                        | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | A na-eji njedebe njedebe maka ume ọhụrụ token kacha mma.    |
| `PROMPTQL_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `180000`                                                           | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Oge kacha (ms) iji mee ntuli `thread_events` tupu oge agwụ. |

| ------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `UNPRICED_USAGE_BUDGET_POLICY` | _(Edoghị; emechiri emechi ma ọ bụrụ na agbanyere ọpụpụ boolean)_ | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts` | Oke-igodo daily/weekly USD : `fail_closed` gbochiri ojiji eji akwụghị ụgwọ n'ezie; `count_as_zero` na-agụ ya ka $0 ka ọ na-emefu ọnụ ahịa. Enum a kapịrị ọnụ (DB tupu env) na-ewepụ ọpụpụ boolean. Ụkpụrụ na-ezighi ezi na ọdịda nchọta ọnụahịa agaghị emechi. |

## Oke koodu ojiji kilo

Ntụle USD nkeonwe yana nyocha ojiji Kilo Pass maka onye na-eweta koodu Kilo. Nhọrọ — ihe ndabara na Kilo API ọha; mebie naanị maka nrụnye relay/test. Nyocha na-eji akara nnweta OAuth dị na njikọ ahụ.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe | Ọdabara               | Isi mmalite faịlụ                     | Nkọwa                                                                 |
| ------------------ | --------------------- | ------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KILO_API_URL`     | `https://api.kilo.ai` | `open-sse/services/usage/kilocode.ts` | Base URL ejiri nweta nguzozi koodu Kilo nkeonwe yana ojiji Kilo Pass. |

## Adobe Firefly Web Provider (Unofficial/Experimental)

Oge nchọgharị na-akwalite ume ọhụrụ maka ndị na-eweta webụsaịtị Adobe Firefly (`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`). Nhọrọ — niile nke ndabara ka edobere maka ịwụnye desktọpụ nkịtị.

> **Ewepụrụ na #9255.** Oge ọsọ ọsọ Chrome nke CDP jikọtara (adobeFireflyChromeRuntime.ts) jiri ọrụ nbanye Playwright dochie ya, na mkpịsị aka ya adịkwaghị. ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_ CDP_PORT / VISIBLE / HEADED / PING / FORCE_RESTART variables, gbakwunyere ADOBE_FIREFLY_LOGIN_WAIT_MS na ADOBE_FIREFLY_FORTER_WAIT_MS, ka a na-agụghị ebe ọ bụla na codebase — mwube ha enweghị mmetụta.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                   | Ọdabara      | Isi mmalite faịlụ                               | Nkọwa                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------ | ------------ | ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`      | `0`          | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts` | Tọọ `1` maka ezigbo Chrome enweghị isi (nke amaara maka imepụta; naanị nbipu).                          |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`      | `1`          | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Ihe nchọgharị na-arụ ọrụ na-ekpo ọkụ opt-in/out. `0` na-ewepụ ọkụ ọkụ (mgbake etiti 408 ka na-emetụta). |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`         | `1`          | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ ịnọgide na-enwe oge Adobe Firefly na diski.                                         |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`    | _(Edobeghi)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Opekempe (ms) amanye n'etiti ntinye aka na-esochi, na-ewepụ ndabara arụnyere n'ime ya.                  |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`   | _(Edobeghi)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Oghere ọzọ (ms) agbakwunyere ka ọ nwesịrị ihe ịga nke ọma, na-ewepụ ndabara arụnyere n'ime ya.          |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS` | _(Edobeghi)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`       | Oge nkwụsị (ms) tupu ịnyefe arịrịọ ọgbọ, na-ewepụ ndabara arụnyere n'ime ya.                            |

## 19. Mmekọrịta Model (Dev)

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                  | Ọdabara       | Isi mmalite faịlụ                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_ENABLED`           | _(Edobeghi)_  | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Mfefe siri ike maka ụdịrị mịkọrị ọnụ ahịa dev. Tọọ ntọala = nsọpụrụ > AI (`modelsDevSyncEnabled`). `0`/`false`/`off`/`no`** na-emeri DB** ma na-awụpụ ma mmekọrịta oge oge yana nyocha `getModelsDevPricing()` SQL/JSON (nweghachi mgbe etinyere dashboard n'otu akaghị ihe omume). `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` Force mekọrịta na. Ọnụ ego save/clear ka na-akpọ `backupDbFile("pre-write")`, nke na-abụghị op n'okpuru nkeji 60 ma ọ bụ `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`. |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_INTERVAL`          | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Ogologo oge mmekọrịta katalọgụ ụdị-oge mmepe n'ime sekọnd.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/contextWindowResolver.ts` | Ogologo oge (sekọnd) maka nhazigharị nke onwe-window reconciler (5004): pins onye na-eweta-kwupụtara windo site na nchọpụta `/models` ka `auto:discovery` na-agafe mgbe ha si na katalọgụ ahụ pụọ. Tọọ na `0` iji gbanyụọ. Na-ejigharị data emekọrịtalarị (ọ nweghị ihe ọhụrụ ewepụtara); anaghị edegharị `manual` gabiga.                                                                                                                                       |

| `OPENROUTER_CATALOG_TTL_MS`                    | `86400000` (24h)                       | `src/lib/catalog/openrouterCatalog.ts`                                             | OpenRouter ụdị katalọgụ cache TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

Gbanyụọ maka ndị ahịa na-atụ anya IDs naanị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

Tọọ `1` (ma ọ bụ `true`/`yes`) iji kpọsaa `gweb/gemini-4-argon` na webụ Gemini wee hapụ onye ọrụ ya họrọ ụdị ahụ. Gbanyụọ na ndabara n'ihi na enyochabeghị ndị ahọpụtara ụdị menu Argon megide akaụntụ Weebụ Gemini dị ndụ; mgbe gbanyụọ, ihe nlereanya na-adịghị depụtara na a na-ajụ arịrịọ maka ya dị ka ihe atụ amaghi ama, dị ka ọ dị na mbụ. Gụọ na mmalite, yabụ mgbanwe chọrọ ịmalitegharịa.                                                                                 |
| `CATALOG_BUILD_TIMEOUT_MS`                     | `8000` (8s)                            | `src/app/api/v1/models/catalogCache.ts`                                            | echere ụzọ oyi maka mwughachi katalọgụ `GET /v1/models` agbakọtara (#12627). N'oge nkwụsị, a na-enye 200 nke ikpeazụ mgbe otu dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYNCED_CATALOG_STALE_AFTER_MS`      | `2592000000` (ụbọchị 30)               | `src/lib/db/models/activeSyncedCatalog.ts`                                         | Afọ mgbe nke a gasịchara ndepụta ụdị synced njikọ na-akwụsị ịbụ ikike maka ịkwọgharị wee daa imeghe na ndekọ (#12849). Ahịrị ndị na-enwebeghị akara oge na-agụta dị ka ihe ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ANALYTICS_CACHE_TTL_MS`             | `60000` (60s)                          | `src/lib/usage/analyticsResponseCache.ts`                                          | Ogologo oge ole ka a na-ejigharị nzaghachi `GET /api/usage/analytics` nwayọ maka otu eriri ajụjụ (#15629). `0` gbanyụọ cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ANALYTICS_CACHE_MIN_COMPUTE_MS`     | `100` (100ms)                          | `src/lib/usage/analyticsResponseCache.ts`                                          | Naanị nzaghachi nyocha nke were opekata mpe a ogologo a ka echekwara, yabụ obere oge na-ahụ data ọhụrụ mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

Mgbe atọrọ na integer N na-abụghị nke adịghị mma, akụkụ ojiji-akụkọ ihe mere eme nke `GET /api/cache` (ngwa ngwa-cache ngụkọta + omume) na-eje ozi site na cache nzaghachi nkekọrịta mgbe ọ were opekata mpe N ms iji wuo, kwa windo `trendHours` kpọgidere, maka `OMNIROUTE_ANALYTICS_CACHE_TTL_MS` (akụkọ ihe mere eme nwere ike ịdịruo TTL ahụ; `DELETE /api/cache` na-emebi ya). Akụkọ = akwadoghị ma ọ bụ ezighi ezi na-agụ ozugbo na arịrịọ ọ bụla.                            |

`ENABLE_CC_COMPATIBLE_PROVIDER` bụ naanị maka relays ndị ọzọ na-anabata ndị ahịa Claude Code naanị. OmniRoute na-edegharị arịrịọ ka ndị relays nabata ha. Ọ bụrụ naanị na ịchọrọ iji Claude Code CLI, ma ọ bụ na ị maghị ihe relays ndị a bụ, debe nke a nwere nkwarụ ma tinye onye na-eweta Anthropic na-arụkọ ọrụ mgbe niile kama.

## 21. Proxy Health

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                                | Ọdabara                  | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                                                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROXY_FAST_FAIL_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `2000`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                    | Nlele ahụike na-ada ngwa ngwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_LATENCY_WINDOW_HOURS`                      | `3`                      | `src/lib/db/proxies.ts`                                                                     | Window oge (awa) maka ịgbakọ nkezi latency nke proxies ndị chọrọ ntuli aka na atụmatụ ọdọ mmiri emebere nke latency kachasị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS`                       | `30000`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                    | Nyocha ahụike cache TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `NONE_HONORED_WINDOW_HOURS`                       | `24`                     | `src/lib/db/reasoningEffortCompliance.ts`                                                   | Window na-esochi n'ime awa maka reasoning_effort = ọ nweghị onye na-agụ nnabata na GET /v1/models/health.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NONE_HONORED_CACHE_TTL_MS`                       | `20000`                  | `src/lib/db/reasoningEffortCompliance.ts`                                                   | Cache TTL na ms maka nseta ihuenyo enweghị nnabata (15-30s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `NONE_HONORED_MIN_SAMPLE`                         | `3`                      | `src/lib/db/reasoningEffortCompliance.ts`                                                   | Opekempe, ọ nweghị arịrịọ tupu ihe nlereanya agụ ihe a na-asọpụrụ ma ọ bụ na asọpụrụghị ya; n'okpuru a nlereanya na-agụ amaghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_UNHEALTHY_CACHE_TTL_MS`             | `2000`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                    | Cache TTL maka nyocha ahụike proxy dara ada. Debe nke a dị mkpụmkpụ karịa `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS` ka oge nkwụsị proxy na-adịru nwa oge n'okpuru nnukwu mgbanwe mgbanwe ngwa ngwa na-enweghị gbanyụọ ngwa ngwa-ada maka proxies nwụrụ anwụ n'ezie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_ENABLED`                            | `true`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Tọọ `false` iji gbanyụọ onye nhazi ahụike proxy ndabere nke na-enyocha proxies edebanyere aha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_INTERVAL_MS`                        | `600000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | N'azụ ahụike-onye na-ahazi oge mkpochapụ na ms (nke kacha nta `60000`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RELAY_INTERVAL_MS`                  | `21600000`               | `src/lib/proxyHealth/relayCadence.ts`                                                       | Oge nyocha na ms maka proxies Edge-relay (`deno`/`vercel`/`cloudflare`), onye nyocha ya na-akpọlite iche iche enweghị ihe nkesa ( `60000` kacha nta).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RECOVERY_INTERVAL_MS`               | `600000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Oge mgbake ndabere na ms: ugboro ole a na-enyocha proxies na-adịghị mma na mbụ ka onye nnọchiteanya eweghachitere laghachi na ntụgharị na-enweghị ịmalitegharị. | `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL`                           | `https://httpbin.org/ip` | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                        | Ebumnuche nyocha ikike iru nke onye nhazi na ebe njedebe `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test`. N'ala na 1 ma kpuchie na 50.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_STAGGER_MS`                    | `100`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                        | Igbu oge na ms n'etiti ọpụpụ nyocha abụọ n'ime ogbe. Na-enweghị ya, batch dum na-ahapụ n'otu oge na egress IP na-ekekọrịta nwere ike imebi ebumnuche nwere oke. Tọọ na `0` iji gbanyụọ oghere; dị na 5000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_USE_PROVIDER_TARGET`                | `true`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/providerProbeTarget.ts`                                                | Tọọ “ụgha” ka ịkwụsị inyocha ezigbo onye ọbịa nke onye na-eweta proxy ekenyere (`GET /models`, enweghị igodo API ) ma jiri `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL` mgbe niile kama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_AUTO_DEACTIVATE`                    | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/statusPolicy.ts`                                                       | Mgbe `false` (nke ndabara), ihe nleba anya iru eru akpaghị aka (onye nhazi + bọtịnụ `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` "Nnwale niile")** naanị ọgụgụ** ma deekwa ọkwa proxy — naanị onye ọrụ na-edobe active/inactive, yabụ nyocha na-adịghị mma enweghị ike ijikọ proxy ekenyere (#6246). Tọọ `true` ka iweghachite ule-na tọọ omume.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `FLUSH_EMPTY_RETRY_ENABLED`                       | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                          | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara Nwepu (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB override meriri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-emegharị mgbasagharị ntụgharị efu na-atụgharị site na ụzọ nzere nkịtị (ruo `STREAM_RECOVERY.EMPTY_TURN_RETRY_MAX` emegharị) kama ikpughe 200 efu ma ọ bụ ọdịnaya efu 502.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `EMPTY_STREAM_SIBLING_RECOVERY_ENABLED`           | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                          | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara Nwepu (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB override meriri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-akwado Claude/Codex nyocha ọdịnaya efu na-emefu ihe dị adị na-anwale na njikọ ọzọ tozuru oke mgbe ntụgharị `empty_content` gasịrị, na-enweghị nkwụsịtụ ma ọ bụ mkpuchi ihe nlereanya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

| `PROXY_POOL_EGRESS_OBSERVATION`                   | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                          | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara Nwepu (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB override meriri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-egosi nlele naanị ọdọ mmiri egress n'okpuru ọdọ mmiri proxy na dashboard (iche egress IPs, njikọ na nke a kacha hụ n'azụ otu IP n'ime awa 24 gara aga, site na ndekọ proxy). Nhọrọ na-adịghị emebi emebi na `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`: proxy na-apụ na mkpebi pool/rotation ozugbo (nyocha ọnọdụ ndụ nke scope-pool resolution na-ewepụrịrị ya) ma na-emegharị ya ozugbo ozugbo ọ malitere ịgafe nyocha ọzọ. | `RATE_LIMIT_AUTO_ENABLE`                          | _(Edobeghi)_             | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                     | Mee ka ọnụọgụ nchekwa net on/off mee ka akpaaka n'agbanyeghị ntọala Dashboard na-adịgide adịgide (`resilienceSettings.requestQueue.autoEnableApiKeyProviders`). Na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on` ịmanye, `false`/`0`/`off` ịgbapụ. Mgbe ọ na-arụ ọrụ (nke ndabara), njikọ igodo API na-arụ ọrụ na-echekwa oke oke na-akpaghị aka. Ịtọ n'otu njikọ `rateLimitProtection: false` na-ewepụ nchedo doro anya mana ọ naghị apụ na netwọk nchekwa a; idobe env var a na `false` bụ usoro zuru ụwa ọnụ iji mee njikọ igodo API na-enweghị ahịrị oke-oke.                                   |

OFF na ndabara (na-ekpuchi njikọ Cooldown / Onye na-eweta Circuit Breaker). Nabata `true`/`1`/`on` iji mee ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

Agụnyere nke ukwuu site na ọdịda na-esochi. A na-eji naanị mgbe `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MAX_MS`                        | `300000` (minti 5)       | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                              | Okpu kacha mma (ms) tupu anwalegharịa provider/connection dara n'agbanyeghị. A na-eji naanị mgbe `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`                         | _(gbanyụọ → ) _          | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (mkpụrụ) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (echiche) | **Kedu:** mgbake n'ụzọ doro anya nke iyi gbagoro agbago elu (ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri free-claude-code). Jide windo SSE mmeghe ruo `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS` (750 ms) yabụ _pre-commit_ cutoff — nke na-eme tupu byte ọ bụla erute onye ahịa — ga-emepe ọzọ wee nwagharịa na-adịghị ahụ anya.** Mgbe iji mee ka:** flaky/upstreams nke na-emekarị 0-byte-truncate na mmalite iyi; hapụ OFF ma ọ bụrụ na ịnweghị ike ị nweta ihe ruru 750 ms nke akara oge-na-mbụ na iyi ọ bụla. Nabata `true`/`1`/`on`. Mkpụrụ ntọala Resilience nọgidere; ntọala Dashboard na-emeri otu oge atọrọ. |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_MIDSTREAM_ENABLED`               | _(gbanyụọ → ) _          | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (mkpụrụ) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (echiche) | **Kedu:** aga n'ihu n'etiti iyi (Fase 4.4) — mgbe a _post-commit_ truncation (bytes eruola onye ahịa), rịọgharịa ya na akụkụ akụkụ ederede dị ka onye enyemaka prefill wee kpachie suffix efu. Ederede dị larịị OpenAI-dakọtara naanị iyi; Ọ dịghị mgbe ọkụ na ngwá ọrụ oku na-efe efe.** Mgbe iji mee ka:** ogologo ọgbọ na-ebipụ n'etiti azịza na ị na-anabata ọdụ enwetara na-abata dị ka otu gbawara kama token-site-token. N'adabereghị na `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED` (profaịlụ ihe egwu dị iche iche). Nabata `true`/`1`/`on`.                                           |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_ENABLED`              | _(gbanyụọ → ) _          | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` → `open-sse/services/throughputWatchdog.ts`                | Ịbanye na-arụsi ọrụ ike-iyi bara uru na-ahụ maka mmepụta. Na-achọpụta iyi na-aga n'ihu na-eziga chunks mana ọ nọ n'okpuru ọnụego ntinye aka ahaziri; obi mgbawa, mmemme ojiji, deltas efu, na usoro tool/reasoning adịghị ewere ka ọganihu. Kewapụ na oge arụghị ọrụ na njedebe siri ike. Mkpụrụ naanị ndabara: boolean echekwara na `resilienceSettings` (Dashboard → Settings → Resilience) na-ebute ụzọ.                                                                                                                                                                   |
