A ghaghị ijikọta ya na `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`. Lee `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_ENABLED`            | `0`                                                                         | `src/lib/security/localEndpoints.ts`                                      | Mgba ọkụ bụ isi maka ụzọ `/api/local/*`. Mgbe edobeghị ya ma ọ bụ ọ bụ `0`, ụzọ `/api/local/*` niile na-eweghachi 503 na production. Ọ ghaghị ịbụ `1` na deployments na-abụghị loopback iji mee ka Redis launcher na ndị na-amalite ọrụ mpaghara yiri ya nke otu-pịa rụọ ọrụ. Ọ na-arụkọ ọrụ dị ka nchedo abụọ na nhazi route-guard nke `isLocalOnlyPath()` (`LOCAL_ONLY_API_PREFIXES` dị na `src/server/authz/routeGuard.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_TOKEN`              | _(edobeghị)_                                                                | `src/lib/security/localEndpoints.ts`                                      | Bearer token maka ndị na-akpọ `/api/local/*` na-anọghị na loopback (dịka desktop app). Mgbe edobere ya, arịrịọ sitere na IP ndị na-abụghị loopback ga-enwerịrị `Authorization: Bearer <token>`. A chọrọ ya mgbe `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_ENABLED=1` na deployments na-abụghị loopback.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_CONTAINER_NAME`               | `omniroute-redis`                                                           | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Aha akpa maka onye na-ebido Redis otu-pịa (`omniroute redis up`). Ma CLI ma GUI `RedisLauncherPanel` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_HOST_PORT`                    | `6379`                                                                      | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Port onye nnabata maka onye na-ebido Redis otu-pịa. Gbanwee ya ma ọ bụrụ na onye nnabata ejirila 6379 jikọọ. Port dị n'ime akpa ahụ ka bụ 6379.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST`                    | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Interface onye nnabata nke onye na-ebido Redis otu-pịa na-ebipụta na ya. Onye na-ebido ya na-amalite Redis NA-ENWEGHỊ paswọọdụ, yabụ ijikọ na `0.0.0.0` na-enye kọmputa nnabata ọ bụla dị na LAN gị Redis na-enweghị nkwenye — gbasaa ya naanị ma ọ bụrụ na gị onwe gị tinyekwara paswọọdụ na instance ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `REDIS_BIND_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Interface onye nnabata nke docker-compose na-ebipụta Redis sidecar na ya (#9286). Redis compose na-agba ọsọ na-enweghị `requirepass`; akpa ngwa na-erute ya site na netwọkụ compose (`redis:6379`) — port a na-ebipụta dị naanị maka ngwaọrụ ndị dị n’akụkụ onye nnabata. `0.0.0.0` na-ekpughere LAN niile Redis na-enweghị nkwenye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `REDIS_PORT`                                   | `6379`                                                                      | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Port onye nnabata maka Redis sidecar nke compose.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `APP_BIND_HOST`                                | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`, `docker-compose.prod.yml`                           | Interface onye nnabata nke docker-compose na-ebipụta dashboard/API/live-WS nke ngwa ahụ na ya (#12568). Ebe `REQUIRE_API_KEY=false` bụ ndabara `.env.example` a na-ebufe, `0.0.0.0` na-ekpughere proxy LLM `/v1` na-enweghị aha nye LAN/WAN niile — gbasaa ya naanị mgbe `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` dị ma ọ bụ mgbe reverse proxy dị n’ihu na-amanye nkwenye nke ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `QDRANT_BIND_HOST`                             | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Interface onye nnabata nke docker-compose na-ebipụta Qdrant memory sidecar na ya (#12578). Otu ihe kpatara nchegbu banyere ikpughe na LAN dịkwa maka `REDIS_BIND_HOST`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `BIFROST_BIND_HOST`                            | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Interface onye nnabata nke docker-compose na-ebipụta Bifrost router sidecar na ya (#12578). Otu ihe kpatara nchegbu banyere ikpughe na LAN dịkwa maka `REDIS_BIND_HOST`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `REDIS_KEY_PREFIX`                             | `omniroute:`                                                                | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | Prefix oghere aha a na-etinye n’isi igodo Redis OmniRoute ọ bụla (rate limiter, auth cache, quota store, warmup circuit breaker). Ọ na-egbochi esemokwu igodo mgbe a na-ekekọrịta Redis instance ahụ na ngwa ndị ọzọ (#11042).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN`             | _(unset — mechanism disabled)_                                              | `src/lib/api/internalServiceAuth.ts`                                      | Nzuzo e kesara maka hops REST nke ime na-echekwa njirimara (#9260): akụkụ OmniRoute na-akpọ ụzọ OmniRoute mpaghara ndị ọzọ na-eziga ya dị ka `x-omniroute-internal-service-token` ka e wee chekwaa njirimara onye kpọrọ oku mbụ. A na-atụnyere ya site na `timingSafeEqual`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN_FILE`        | _(unset)_                                                                   | `src/lib/api/internalServiceAuth.ts`                                      | Ụdị faịlụ nzuzo nke akara ọrụ ime: ụzọ gaa na faịlụ nke ọdịnaya e wepụrụ oghere n’akụkụ ya bụ akara ahụ. A na-enyocha ya naanị mgbe inline var adịghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_ENABLED`            | `true`                                                                      | `src/lib/catalog/openrouterProviderStats.ts`                              | Tinye stats nhazi kwa izu nke OpenRouter na ndepụta ndị na-enye ọrụ nke dashboard (#9324). Ọ na-arụ ọrụ na ndabara; tọọ `false` ka ịwụpụ fetch nke ndabere kpamkpam (ọ naghị egbochi ọrụ, ọ dịghịkwa ada ada).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_TTL_MS`             | `86400000` (24h)                                                            | `src/lib/catalog/openrouterProviderStats.ts`                              | TTL cache maka snapshot stats ndị na-enye ọrụ OpenRouter, n’ime milliseconds.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_IMAGE`                        | `redis:7-alpine`                                                            | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Redis image nke 1-click Redis launcher na-eji. Gbanwee ya gaa na `redis:8-alpine` ma ọ bụ mirror registry nkeonwe dịka ọ dị mkpa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `QDRANT_HOST`                                  | `qdrant`                                                                    | _(opt-in cluster profile)_                                                | Aha host nke Qdrant sidecar mgbe `--profile memory` na-arụ ọrụ. Ndabara na-arụtụ aka n’aha ọrụ qdrant dị n’ime netwọk; gbanwee ya maka deployment mpụga. A na-eji ya naanị mgbe `qdrantEnabled` bụ `true` n’ime koodu (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts:108`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `QDRANT_PORT`                                  | `6333`                                                                      | _(opt-in cluster profile)_                                                | REST port nke Qdrant sidecar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `QDRANT_GRPC_PORT`                             | `6334`                                                                      | _(profa ụyọkọ opt-in)_                                                    | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri gRPC nke sidecar Qdrant. Ọ bụ ọbaakwụkwọ ndị ahịa na-eji mgbe ha họọrọ gRPC karịa REST maka ọrụ nkwanye data.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `QDRANT_API_KEY`                               | _(unset)_                                                                   | _(profa ụyọkọ opt-in)_                                                    | Igodo API nhọrọ maka Qdrant Cloud ma ọ bụ ihe atụ on-prem nke chọrọ nyocha. Ọ bụrụ na ọ tọgbọ → a naghị eziga isi `api-key`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `QDRANT_COLLECTION`                            | `omniroute-memory`                                                          | _(profa ụyọkọ opt-in)_                                                    | Aha mkpokọta maka embeddings ebe nchekwa mkparịta ụka OmniRoute. A na-emepụta ya na mbido mbụ site na akụkụ `QDRANT_VECTOR_SIZE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `QDRANT_EMBEDDING_MODEL`                       | `text-embedding-3-small`                                                    | _(profa ụyọkọ opt-in)_                                                    | Aha ụdị embedding ndabara e dekọrọ na metadata mkpokọta Qdrant. A na-emepụta embeddings n'ezie site n’aka onye na-enye ọrụ ọ bụla ubi `embeddingModel` dị na ntọala OmniRoute na-atụ aka na ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `QDRANT_VECTOR_SIZE`                           | `1536`                                                                      | _(profa ụyọkọ opt-in)_                                                    | Akụkụ vector embedding. Ọ ga-adaba na ụdị ị na-eji emepụta embedding (text-embedding-3-small → 1536; ada-002 → 1536; nomic-embed-text → 768).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `QDRANT_HNSW_EF_CONSTRUCT`                     | `128`                                                                       | _(profa ụyọkọ opt-in)_                                                    | Izizi n’oge nrụpụta index HNSW. Ọnụọgụ dị elu = nrụpụta na-eji oge karịa, ọchụchọ na-adị ngwa karịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_ENABLED`                   | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Mgbanwe isi maka ntụgharị akaụntụ onye ọrụ nwere ike ịhazi. Mgbe ọ bụ `false`, ọ dịghị klas `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_*` dị n’okpuru na-ebute nlọghachi akaụntụ (ọnọdụ isi gbanyụrụ na-egbochikwa klas 429/500/502 ndị a na-arụ ọrụ na ndabara). Ọ na-enyere ihu n’ihu na-elekọta ọrụ (dịka ngwa desktọpụ VibeProxy) ịtụgharị iwu ntụgharị nke ya n’elu igwe nlọghachi akaụntụ nke backend.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_RATE_LIMIT_RESET_SECONDS`  | `0`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Oge nchere oyi (sekọnd) a na-etinye n’akaụntụ a kpachibidoro ọnụego mgbe upstream enyeghị ihe ngosi nrụpụta doro anya. `0` = jiri oge nchere ndabara nke igwe ahụ kama iji mgbanwe a kapịrị ọnụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_DISABLE_TAG_WITHOUT_RESET` | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Nṅomi nke mmasị ihu onye ọrụ “etinyela mkpado dị ka nke gafere oke ọnụego na-enweghị oge nrụpụta”.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_429`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ịgbalite nkwado ndabere dabere na koodu ọnọdụ maka njehie `429`. Mgbe ọ bụ `false` (na `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_ENABLED=true`), `429` agaghịzi akpalite ntụgharị akaụntụ, kama a ga-ezighachi ya onye ahịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ njehie `429` dị n’ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_WINDOW_SECONDS` achọrọ tupu a atụgharịa akaụntụ ahụ. `1` (ndabara) na-atụgharị ya ozugbo, na-echekwa omume akụkọ ihe mere eme.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Oge mpịakọta (sekọnd) a na-agụta njehie `429` n’ime ya iji tụnyere `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_500`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ịgbalite nkwado ndabere dabere na koodu ọnọdụ maka njehie sava `5xx` (ewezuga `502`, nke nwere klaasị nke ya). Mgbe ọ bụ `false`, njehie ndị a agaghịzi akpalite ntụgharị akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ njehie `5xx` dị n’ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_WINDOW_SECONDS` achọrọ tupu a atụgharịa akaụntụ ahụ. `1` (ndabara) na-atụgharị ya ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Oge mpịakọta (sekọnd) a na-agụta njehie `5xx` n’ime ya iji tụnyere `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_502`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ịgbalite nkwado ndabere dabere na koodu ọnọdụ maka njehie `502` (ọnụ ụzọ adịghị mma). Mgbe ọ bụ `false`, `502`s agaghịzi akpalite ntụgharị akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ njehie `502` dị n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS` nke achọrọ tupu atụgharị akaụntụ ahụ. `1` (ndabara) na-atụgharị ya ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Window na-ag滑 (sekọnd) nke a na-agụ njehie `502` n'ime ya iji ruo `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400`                      | `false`                                                                     | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Nhọrọ a ga-eji banye (ndabara OFF): mgbe ọ bụ `true`, `400` nkịtị (arịrịọ ọjọọ) na-ebute ntụgharị akaụntụ. Nke a na-agbakwunye naanị — ọ naghị egbochi omume injin dị ugbu a ebe `400` nwere ederede metụtara oke ọnụego/quota ka na-ebute failover n'agbanyeghị ọkọlọtọ a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ njehie `400` dị n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS` nke achọrọ tupu atụgharị akaụntụ ahụ (a na-elele ya naanị mgbe `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Window na-ag滑 (sekọnd) nke a na-agụ njehie `400` n'ime ya iji ruo `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

### Onye Nhazi Claude Warmup

Warmup nke cron na-achị maka njikọ Anthropic OAuth ndị ahọpụtara, ka windo oke ọnụego nke awa 5 meghee site n'arịrịọ nhazi dị mfe kama ịbụ site na arịrịọ mbụ n'ezie (#8848). Onye nhazi ahụ na-anọ n'ọnọdụ agbanyụrụ ma ọ bụrụ na `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED` abụghị truthy **na** njikọ ahụ enweghị ọkọlọtọ na `settings.claudeWarmup.connections`; ndepụta njikọ efu pụtara na a gaghị eme warmup ọ bụla ọbụna mgbe env var ahụ gbanyere.

| Variable                       | Default                     | Source File                  | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED`     | _(unset → off)_             | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Mgba ọkụ izugbe maka onye nhazi warmup. Ọ na-anabata `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` (na-eleghara nnukwu na obere mkpụrụedemede anya, ma wepụ oghere n'akụkụ). Uru ọ bụla ọzọ, ma ọ bụ mgbe edobebeghị ya, na-ahapụ onye nhazi ahụ agbanyụrụ. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CRON`        | `0 7 * * *`                 | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Okwu cron nwere ubi ise maka oge warmup, a na-enyocha ya na `America/Los_Angeles` (mpaghara oge reset nke Anthropic) n'agbanyeghị elekere host.                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CONCURRENCY` | `3`                         | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Ọnụọgụ njikọ a na-eme warmup n'otu oge n'oge tick ọ bụla. A na-amachi ya n'etiti `1` na `10`; uru na-abụghị ọnụọgụ na-alaghachi na `3`.                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_MODEL`       | `claude-3-5-haiku-20241022` | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Model a na-eji maka arịrịọ warmup. Gbanwee ya naanị ma ọ bụrụ na model ndabara adịghị n'ime atụmatụ gị; họrọ model dị ọnụ ala nke ka na-emeghe windo ahụ.                                                                           |

### Oge VNC Nbanye-Browser na Alias Data-Dir

Chromium+VNC dị n'ime container nke a na-eji maka ijide nzere nbanye-browser mmekọrịta (`/api/vnc-session`), tinyere alias `DATA_DIR` nke ochie. Ha niile bụ nhọrọ — ndabara VNC na-elekwasị anya na image `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` nke etinyere n'ime ngwugwu, a na-atụgharịkwa ha naanị maka image container omenala, ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri, ma ọ bụ nhazi lifecycle.

| Mgbanwe gburugburu                    | Ndabara                                                                  | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                               | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local`                                           | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Mkpado onyonyo Docker maka akpa nbanye Chromium+VNC. Wuo `docker/vnc-browser/chromium` ma ọ bụ tụọ nke a na onyonyo omenala.                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                                                                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Faịlụ mmemme runtime akpa eji amalite akpa VNC (dịka, tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `podman`).                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri VNC/noVNC a na-ekpughe n’ime akpa ahụ.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri Chrome DevTools Protocol dị n’ime akpa ahụ.                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ụzọ ndekọ profaịlụ Chromium dị n’ime akpa ahụ.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | `$HOME/.omniroute/browser-login-profiles`                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ndekọ dị na igwe nnabata nke na-echekwa profaịlụ nbanye ihe nchọgharị echekwara.                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000` (10 min)                                                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge nkwụsị (ms) tupu ewepụ nnọkọ VNC na-adịghị arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000` (30 min)                                                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oke siri ike (ms) maka ogologo ndụ otu nnọkọ VNC.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọnụọgụ kachasị nke nnọkọ VNC nwere ike ịrụkọ ọrụ n’otu oge.                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge ngwụcha (ms) a na-eche ka ihe nchọgharị dị n’ime akpa wee dị njikere maka CDP.                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge ngwụcha (ms) maka ịnakọta nnọkọ/cookies e jidere mgbe nbanye mechara.                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | `--remote-debugging-port=9222 --no-first-run --no-default-browser-check` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Nhọrọ ahịrị-iwu ndị ọzọ a na-enyefe Chromium dị n’ime akpa ahụ.                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`                                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Netwọk Docker raara onwe ya nye nke akpa nbanye VNC na-esonye na ya (#12571) kama bridge ndabara, ka akpa ndị ibe ghara iru ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri CDP bridge ya.                                              |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(edobeghị)_                                                             | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | **Aha ọzọ ochie** maka `DATA_DIR`, a na-enyocha ya naanị mgbe `DATA_DIR` na `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR` abụọ edobebeghị. Ọ na-achọta cache nnọkọ web-thread Notion (`<dir>/notion-web-thread-sessions.json`). |

## 26. Nwale & E2E Harness

A na-eji `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`, `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, na `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`. Hapụ uru ọ bụla n'okpuru adịghị eguzobe na mbugharị mmepụta.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                     | Ọdabara                        | Isi mmalite faịlụ                            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                         | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`        | E2E bootstrap mode (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) maka onye na-agba ọsọ Playwright.                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | daa azụ na `INITIAL_PASSWORD`  | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`        | agbanyere paswọọdụ onye nchịkwa n'ime gburugburu Playwright.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                         | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`        | Gbanyụọ ntuli aka nyocha ahụike mpaghara n'oge Playwright na-agba.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                         | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`        | Gbanyụọ akara akara ahụike OAuth n'oge ule.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(Edobeghi)_                   | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                | Ewepụrụ ndị na-eweta rịkọm kewapụrụ na nchacha ntule ume ọhụrụ (dịka `codex,openai`). Ezubere iche maka ịgbanyụ ndị na-eweta ahụike — mkpụmkpụ-TTL na-enye ume ọhụrụ ebe ndị na-enye ohere ka na-emeghachi omume naanị. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                         | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`        | Mechie mkpọtụ nlele ahụike na Playwright stdout.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`        | Mafee mmepụta Next.js tupu Playwright amalite (njikarịcha CI ).                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                            | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`                | Mafee OmniRoute iwepụ nko (nke CI na-eji mee ka `node_modules` adịghị mma).                                                                                                                                             |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                       | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`        | Chere oge (ms) maka ihe nkesa ahụ ka ọ dị mma tupu ya agbaa ule ecosystem/protocol.                                                                                                                                     |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login` | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`    | URL ihe njide anwụrụ ọkụ Electron na-atụ anya na ngwa ngwugwu ga-eje ozi.                                                                                                                                               |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                        | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`    | Ngụkọta oge nkwụsị (ms) tupu eriri anwụrụ ọkụ akwụsị.                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                         | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`    | Dezie mpio (ms) ka ibe ibe gachara.                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(na akpaaka)_                 | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`    | Ụzọ doro anya gaa na ngwugwu Electron enwere ike ime ya.                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir) _                    | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`    | Akwụkwọ ndekọ data maka ọsọ anwụrụ ọkụ Electron.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                            | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`    | Tọọ `1` iji chekwaa ndekọ data anwụrụ ọkụ ka emechara.                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                            | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`    | Tọọ `1` ka ịkwanye ndekọ Electron ka ọ bụrụ stdout n'oge ọsọ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                            | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`    | #7592: malitegharịa megide otu data dir wee kwupụta mmalite nke abụọ na-ahọrọ onye ọkwọ ụgbọ ala SQLite.                                                                                                                |
| `OMNI_COVERAGE_ROOT`                   | _(Edobeghi)_                   | `scripts/quality/coverage-node-register.mjs` | Mgbọrọgwụ nchekwa nke ejiri njide mkpuchi mkpuchi (Node register hook + Vitest Provider) na-edozi kpọmkwem isi mmalite mkpuchi. Tọọ site na njigide mma naanị; hapụ ịtọghe na mmepụta.                                  |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(PATH anya)_                  | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`            | Wepụ ụzọ ọnụọgụ abụọ Devin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                         |

### Pipeline ntụgharị akwụkwọ

`scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` ji ya (iwu `npm run i18n:run` ). A naghị edobe mgbanwe isii niile na ndabara — debere ha na `.env` naanị na igwe ga-enwe ike ịme ntụgharị akwụkwọ docs.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                         | Ọdabara      | Isi mmalite faịlụ                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------ | ------------ | ----------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`            | _(Edobeghi)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Mepee URL ntọala dakọtara na AI maka azụ ntụgharị asụsụ.                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`            | _(Edobeghi)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Ihe nrịbama maka ntụgharị asụsụ azụ azụ (abanyebeghị).                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`              | _(Edobeghi)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Id ụdị, dịka `gpt-4o-mini` ma ọ bụ `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_FALLBACK_MODELS`    | _(Edobeghi)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs`  | Yinye ihe atụ nke kewapụrụ rikoma nhọrọ nwalere n'usoro mgbe ụdị nke mbụ dara (oke 429, 401/402, 5xx, nkwụsị oge, netwọk); Achọgharịghị arịrịọ (400/413/422) emejọrọ ebe ọzọ.                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`         | `60000`      | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Oge nkwụsị nke otu-arịọrọ na millisekọnd.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_REASONING_EFFORT`   | _(Edobeghi)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs`  | Njikwa ntụgharị uche nhọrọ, ezigara dị ka ọ dị na azụ azụ mgbe edobere ya.                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY`        | `4`          | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Arịrịọ ntụgharị asụsụ ndị yiri ya mgbe ị na-agafe ọtụtụ faịlụ / mpaghara.                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_ENGINE`             | `llm`        | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | `llm` na-eji azụ azụ n'elu; `google` tụgharịrị site na njin Google na-echekwa ma ọ nweghị nke mgbanwe LLM chọrọ.                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_FALLBACK`    | `1`          | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Site na injin `llm`, mkpirisi ihe nlereanya ahụ enweghị ike imepụta (onye nche mmepụta dara na mbọ ọ bụla, ma ọ bụ njehie ụdị) bụ nke Google tụgharịrị kama ịdaba na mpaghara. `0` gbanyụọ ya. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_API_KEY`     | _(Edobeghi)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Igodo ntụgharị asụsụ ojii nke nhọrọ v2. Unset na-eji njedebe webụ enweghị igodo (oke oke, ọ dị mma maka ebili mmiri mgbe ụfọdụ).                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_CONCURRENCY` | `6`          | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Arịrịọ Google yiri nke mpaghara ọ bụla nke ọsọ na-ekekọrịta.                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `30000`      | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Oge nkwụsị nke otu-arịọrọ maka injin Google, na millise seconds.                                                                                                                               |
| `UI_KIT_READ_TOKEN`                        | _(Edobeghi)_ | `scripts/check/check-ui-kit-upstream.mjs` | Token naanị ịgụ maka elu UI kit repo nke onwe, nke `npm run check:ui-kit-upstream` na-eji yana usoro ọrụ "UI Kit upstream watch" kwa izu. Na mpaghara nlele ahụ na-eji nzere git gị.           |

## 27. Nri radar ( Bochum nkeonwe)

Ihe mgbakwunye nhọrọ nke ọkọlọtọ njirimara RADAR_ENABLED gbara (ndapụtara — ọkọlọtọ njirimara na-atụgharị site na Settings/DB, ọ bụghị env var; lee [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md#flag-radar_enabled-default-off) ). Ngbanwe anọ ndị mbụ dị n'okpuru bụ nhọrọ nhọrọ maka ntinye aka nke onwe ma ọ bụ ndụdụ na isi igodo nkwado. Nke ise, `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`, bụ njikọ dị iche na-enweghị ndabara na panel arụmọrụ nke onye nwe ya. Hụ [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md) maka doc modul zuru oke yana [mmalite ya na ngwụcha ngwụcha yana usoro nhazi eduzi](../frameworks/RADAR.md#end-to-end-activation-and-guided-setup).

Onye na-agụ ọkwa ọkwa Home/Changelog anaghị ahazi ya site na mgbanwe gburugburu ebe obibi na ọ dabereghị na ọkọlọtọ njirimara RADAR_ENABLED. Ọ na-agụ ebe nchekwa ọha `news.json` URL ekwuputara na `src/shared/utils/releaseNotes.ts` na GET naanị; IDs nchụpụ ka dị na nchekwa ihe nchọgharị mpaghara.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe            | Ọdabara                                      | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `RADAR_FEED_URL`              | `https://radar.omniroute.online`             | `src/lib/radar/{sync,referralsSync,offersSync,intelSync}.ts` | Base URL kesara site na katalọgụ bịanyere aka na ya iche, ntinye aka, onyinye nkwado na nri Intel. Wepụtụ aka na ọrụ ejiri aka gị ma ọ bụ nke agbadoro anya. |
| `RADAR_FEED_PUBKEY`           | _(igodo ndabara etinyere)_                   | `src/lib/radar/pinnedKeys.ts`                                | Ed25519 igodo ọha (base64-DER SPKI ma ọ bụ PEM) ejiri iji nyochaa mbinye aka nri site na ndepụta ahaziri iche.                                               |
| `RADAR_CONTRIBUTOR_CLAIM_URL` | `https://radar.omniroute.online/auth/github` | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL bọtịnụ dashboard "Abụ m onye ntinye aka ga-emepe (GitHub OAuth supporter-key claim flow).                                                                |
| `RADAR_SUPPORTER_PLANS_URL`   | `https://radar.omniroute.online/planos`      | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL bọtịnụ dashboard "kwado ọrụ ahụ" ga-emepe ( ibe payment/plans ).                                                                                         |
| `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`             | _(Edobeghi)_                                 | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | Njikọ ọrụ naanị onye nwe ya-njikọ. achọrọ HTTPS belụsọ maka HTTP loopback SSH n'ihu; ụkpụrụ atọpụghị ma ọ bụ ezighi ezi emepụtaghị ihe nsoroụzọ.             |
